DEDICATED TO......MY SISTERS😘



 _Bissimillah Rahmanir_ _Rahim_



----------------Cikin sauri ta isa inda yake;"please Prince mukoma"cikin shagwaba tayi maganar kamar zatayi kuka.

Hannun shi ta kama suka isa wajen.

Zaunar dashi tayi;" tace banson ka wahala barshi na amso maka"daga kai yayi alamar tabbatar wa,saboda shima bazai iya zuwa wajen ba yayi mashi nisa.

Tafe take har takai inda rumfar su Shatu take sallama tayi su duka kallon ta suke,kamar sunga wanii photo,ko wata kalar halitta.

Tsoki tayi ta daure fuska tace kai Prince ya cika fitina,yanzu duk abincin da aka tara mashi baici ba,sai wannan furar,cikin zuciyarta take maganar.


Shatu ce tayi karfin halin cewa;"sannu,me kike sone"kamar kada tayi magana sai ya mutsar fuska take tace;"kuna da fura ne?"

Cikin sauri Shatu tace;"Eh"

Kamar wadda bata son magana tace;"kudama mani,kuyi sauri na sayi furar kune dan naga kuna da tsabta,bana son kazanta kada kisa man hannu "cikin sauri Shatu ta fara dama mata furar da ludde.

Ni kam mutuwar zaune nayi daman akwai kalar wa'innan mutanan a duniya,gaskiya idan kazo birni ganinka zakayi kamar kaje wata duniya.


Shatu ta mika mata ta amsa tace;"daya ta biyo ni ta amshi kudii" Shatu ta mike dan ni ko motsi banyi saboda Al'ajabi.

Ina nan zaune nayi tagumi ina jin zafin ciwo,ga tunanin auren Lado ya hanani sakat, banda faduwar gaba babu abinda nike.


Jim kadan sai gata da gudu cikin murna tace;"Nanah taso kiga wani Balarabe,mijin wannan matar, "mikar dani take amman jikina tamkar babu lakka a jikin,na kasa motsi.


Kama kaina nayi nace;"Shatu muje gida banda lafiya"kallona take"tace kinga kudin da suka bamu jakka biyar,nace babu chanji suka ce na dauka,gobe ma mukawo"girgiza kai nayi na dauki kwaryata nayi gaba.

Bayana Shatu ta biyo tana kirana,harda dan gudu ta hada ta iskeni inda nike tace,"wai mike damunki ne??naga yau baki da walwala ko dai ciwon ne,ga kudi munsamu muje a saka maki magani Mana"kuka yazo man.

Cikin kuka nake maganar nace;"Shatu bani da sa'a a rayuwar nan,an haifeni cikin wahala,na tashi cikin wahala,kuma Shatu zan mutu cikin wahala,tunda nayi wayau ban san wani abu ba waishi so,daga Baba saike cikin duniyar nan ke kaunata,ina bukatar a nuna man kauna nima,ina bukatar namijin da zai soni dan Allah ya kula dani don Allah, "kuka take tamkar ranta zaifita.

Itama Shatu hawaye take tace;"nasan kina cikin wahala,amman har kullun ke kike fada man komi na da iyaka,nasan ba kowa ne mutum ne zai iya jure halin da kike ciki ba,amman nima yau zan fada maki kiyi hakuri kodan Baba"

Kuka nike sosai cikin kukan nace;"Inna ke son ta aura man Lado mai jakkai na kauyan mu "zaro ido tayi.

Kulle bakin ta tayi tace; "Amman koni da ba kauyan ku nike ba amman ai nasan halin da gidan Lado suke ciki, wannan wace irin kiyayya ce??Inna zata kaiki cikin wahala?"

Nan na fada mata abinda ya faru jiya.

Tace"wallahi kada ki yadda duk abinda zata yi maki,idan kinji ba zaki iyaba ki gudu ki tafi wajen dangin Innar ki,nasan zasu goya maki baya"toshe bakina nayi saboda yadda kuka na ke kara karfi,jin an fadi dangin Inna ta. 

Kallona Shatu tayi tace;"kada ki damu duk inda dangin Innar ki suke nasan zasu soki,karma dai idan kakar ki na nan"cikin koɗaɗɗiyar muryata nace"Babu mai sona wallahi duk duniya sai ku biyu,idan suna sona miyasa ba zasu zo inda nike ba,shekara wajen nawa,kinga kenan ba su Sona"lalle kam yau naba Shatu tausayi saboda yadda hawayen ta suka kasa tsayawa.

Ganin kukan ta yasa na tsagaita nawa nace;"Shatu ki daina kuka hakanan"jan majina tayi tace"dole nayi kuka saboda ina tausaya maki,saboda zanbar ki ke daya,babu mai taimaka maki"kallon ta nike cikin mamaki!!

Nace;"Ina zaki kuma Shatu idan kika brni wahalar zata yi man yawa,wajen ki kaɗai nike samun sauki,gashi yanzu Inna ta daina bani kudin babur,kona ragin hanya ta daina ban,idan bakinan kenan kasa zan dunga zuwa??"


Hawaye Shatu take tace;"kada ki damu ,daman kunyar fadi maki nike an kusa yin  aure na,nan da mako biyu za'a sakani a lalle,amman insha Allah kafin lokacin Allah zai kawo mafita"kuka nike duniya zata min zafii kenan??

Hannuna taja mukayi siyayya yau harda nama ta sai mana,cikin kudin da muka samu,muka iso bakin hanya muka iske jabe yana jiran mu,sai fada yake,wai tun dazun yana jiran mu.

Muka hau Amalanken muka zauna nide banda tunani babu abinda nike ta bude mana naman,hakan naci saboda ta matsaman saina chi idan ba haka ba itama batacii.


Bayan mun kai kauyen mu a nata kiran magriba na sakko tace amshi;"ta saka man sauran chanjin a cikin hannuna "Jabe zaija Amalanke nace "Jabe tsaya mana kudin menene?"dariya tayi ta ɗagoman hannu Jabe na jan Amalanken tace;"chanjin kudin ne ki sayi wani abu domin kuwa kin fini bukata,ki fito gobe da wurii"nima daga mata hannu nayi nace;"nagode"na danji sanyi a raina nashiga ciki da sallama.

Inna na iske zata shiga daki da alamun alwala tayi.

Nashiga ciki nace;"Inna sannu da aiki" tsaki tayi sannan ta iskeni inda nike tsaye"bani kudi na nashiga daki"na warware zane na bata nace"Inna ina abinci na?"kallona tayi sama da kasa tace"banyi ba tunda Ubanki bai kawo ba,yana chan wajen neman maki magani sarkin yan ciwo daga gama fadin haka ta shiga ciki.

Nayi dakina daman bana jin wata yunwa,alwala nayi na shiga zanyi sallah sai naji alamar wani abu na bina,nayi saurin fita zuwa ban daki. 

Nafito ina tsaki banson rashin Sallah ya iskeni yanzu ba.

Daki na koma na zauna na boye kudina gefen buhuna.


         ****************

A gajiye suka isa gida,kowa yayi part dinshi.

Yana zuwa masu kula dashi suka rufa mashi baya, toilet aka hada mashi ruwan wanka,ya shiga yayi wanka ya fito.

Ya fito yazo bakin mirror ya zauna,gyaran kai za'ayi mashi,hannu ya mika a bashi kum din,yayi nuni da a fita,mutum guda ke tsaye bayan shi,wanda daman ko a Bauchi haka suke,indai yazo to kullum yana biye dashi.


Hannu ya daga zai kai a kanshi yaji hannun shi ya rike.

Cikin sauri na bayan shi yace;"Subhanallah..!! Allah ya taimaki magajin sarki ka kawo na gyara maka,wannan ba aikin ka bane"maida hannun shi yayi,ajiyar zuciya yayi yace wai haka aikin nan ke da wahala??

Kallon shi yayi yana tsaye yace;"Sani wai haka wasu mutane ke wahala??"cikin mamaki sani yake kallon shi dan tunda yake dashi bai taɓa kiran sunan shi na asali ba, kai ko kallo ma  bai isheshi ba bai yin magana ko da yana bukatar abu, saidai idan ya fahimta ya bashi abun.

Lalle yau ansamu wani sabon abu ya shiga rayuwar shi,ko menene shi abun oho??

Cikin ladabi yace;"Allah ya taimaki magajin sarki wani abu ka gani? "daga kai yayi.

A hankali yace;"wasu kananun yara ne guda biyu na hanga,daya da tazo amsar kudi,na sayi furar su,kayan dake jikinta da yadda ta ruɗe akan Princess ta bata One thousand shine abin yaban mamaki"Allah ya taimake ka kuma idan ka bin cika daga kauye suka fito,kuma suna doguwar tafiya,ga kaya akai"kai ya jinjina.

Shiru Sani yayi yana tunanin,yau itace rana ta farko a rayuwar Prince ya damu da wasu,idan ka debe Jarabtar shi(wato MANAL)bai taba yin maganar wasu ba sai akan yan matan nan guda biyu kuma yan kauye,ikon Allah yace.


A hankali ya gyara kanshi yaji ya gaji, lumshe ido yayi yana tuna furar nan mai dadi,a hankali yace;"gobe ma zan koma".


Wanka Princess tayi ta saka riga da siket English wears,ta saka wata hadadiyar kimono a sama ta daure wutsiyar ta a gefe.

Wayar ta tayi kara taga Hanan cikin sauri ta dauka"Hello My dear I missing you so much please come back tomorrow I need your help, please, please"tayi maganar kamar zatayi kuka.

Dariya Hanan tayi tace;"Umm why don't picking my call??lokacin kuna shan love ko,amman ta da Friends"tsaki Kursim tayi tace;"love ko wahala,bai da lokacin kallo na,sai ma wasu ban zayen masu fura daya ke kallo suna gaban inda muke zaune,harda ma furar yasha,kenan baida time dina saina yan fura"


Dariya Hanan tayi tace;"amman kishin ki yayi yawa harda yan fura kike kishi?? please ki daina irin wannan kishin ai abun kunya ne,haba kada wani yaji mana,yanzu ina Abuja tare da su Mom, maybe next week mudawo zanyi tunani akan Prince,amman wallahi ban taɓa jin mutum mai taurin kanshi ba,kamar dai dutse,zanyi tunani kafin na dawo,insha Allah zamu samu mafita idan yaki ta lalama, wallahi nasan abinda duk kika yi mashi shi saiya aure ki,amman akwai hatsari,sai dai idan nadawo idan bai chanza ba zamu bi ta bayan gida,kada kidamu matar Prince Anuwar"dariya Kursim tayi,sukayi sallama magan ganun Hanan sunyi tasiri a zuciyar ta.


Fita tayi ta nufi part din Umman ta.


        *************

A dakina har dare yayi naji karar shigowar Baba,na mike na nufi waje.

Sannu da zuwa Baba,andawo lafiya??

Murmushi yayi yace;"yawwa ina Innar taki,baki shiga dakin ta kuyi fira,zaman kaɗaici baida dadi kinji"daga Kai nayi.

Ledar hannun sa na amsa dai-dai lokacin da Inna tafito,"sallati ta rabka kamar wacce aka yima wani abu,yanzu ita ledar Malam ke kika koma karba kenan na zama kishiyar ki,kema uwar ki zaki taya kishi duk da bata duniyar?"tsawa Baba yayi sannan ya daka mata tsawa yace;"meyasa ke baki da hankali baki san kalar maganar da za kiyima diyar kiba, kullun yarinya cikin wahala,kuma duk da haka ba zaki barta ta huta ba,ki shiga taitayin ki"


Harara ta dallah man ta koma daki.

Ya mikoman wata leda yace"kinga wannan takice wannan kuma ki rike zanyi tafiya kwana biyu zanyi,zanje wajen dan uwana Dan-musa an aiko bashi da lafiya kuma kinsan daga nan zuwa Dan-musa akwai dan nisa shiyasa zanyi kwana biyu,wannan magani ne shi kuma ki jika a ruwa kisha,su kuma kudin kisayi wani abu kici idan bata baki abinci ba"godiya nayi ma Baba sosai, duk da a raina banji dadi ba,dan nasan wahala zata karu,kai yau littinin amman abubuwa sai chakude wa suke,ga auren Shatu na fara lissafa kwana ki,ga Baba zaiyi tafiya.

Daki na koma na hada kudina waje daya na kwanta ina tunanin abinda zai faru bayan tafiyar Baba,dan nasan kwana biyun nan zan fuskan ci matsala.

Lumshe ido nayi nace bari nayi bacci da wuri yanda zan tashi da wurii.


       **************
Bayan Kursim ta fito part din Maman ta,part din Anuwar ta nufa kai tsaye ta shiga.

Anyi sa'a yana falo kuma shi kadai ne, Video call yake da Dad ɗin shi,harda Nifra sunyi magana duk tana nan zaune,ta danji dadi yadda yayi wayar,Nifra ke fadin sunyi Missing dinshi, Murmushi kawai yayi,haka suka gama wayar ya kashe,bai kalle taba kuma yaga shigowar ta.

Mikewa yayi har ya juya tace;"Prince baka ganni ba,kuma kasan wajen ka nazo"cikin shagwaba tayi maganar.

Ma ballan rigar ta ta cire, yadda zaiga kwalliyar ta.

Juyowa yayi ya dawo ya zauna, motsa baki yayi zaiyi magana,cikin doki da jindadi ta maida hankalin wajen shi"Allah na godema yau zai kulani muyi fira kenan"ta fada a zuciyar ta.

Cikin cool voice dinshi yace;"Gobe da wuri ki maidani nasha furar nan"cikin mamaki take kallon shi!!

Murmushi tayi ganin yana kallon ta tayi saurin boye mamakin ta tace"Wow naji dadi da nuna kana son furar nan,amman gaskiya bazan barka ka dunga shan irin wannan furar ba, akwai fura mai kyau da ake saidawa a manyan wurare a Katsina  kuma gidan nan ko naman dawisu kake so akwai ballan tana fura,zan sa a dinga kawoma daga sashen Mai martaba"juyar da kai yayi.

Yace;" na fada maki bana shan fura wannan kaɗai nike son sha"yayi maganar cikin dagawar murya,duk da ya daga murya Amman ita kaɗai kejin shi.


Cikin sauri tace;"ohh sorry Prince Allah ya kaimu goben"tashi tayi tace"dare yayi bari na kwanta"cikin sauri tabar wajen,zuciyar ta na mata zafi.

A part din tana zuwa ta kori kowa tace a subar ta ita daya.

Tsakiyar gado taje ta hau tayi ihunta.


A rasa abinda zai zame mata matsala sai masu fura,yaran kauye,kodai furar tsafi ce??

 MANAL kadai ya damu da ita a duniya,duk wahalar da take mashi bai gani,saima takaishi wajen masu fura gobe da safe.

Anya ba akwai tsafi ba??
Kodai Aljanu ne??
Shiyasa su duka suke da da kyau harma dai mai furar,yaga wannan ya rude ina ga mai furar??
Kai bazan bari su hadu da mai furar nan ba,dan kila mayune,ya zanyi kenan??

Ga Prince da taurin kai, idan yana son Abu bai chan zawa.

Ya zanyi kenan gobe da Prince??

Falalar Tasbihi, Da Godiya Ga Allah, Da Yin La ilaha Illal Lah, da yin Kabbara

Abu Huraira, Allah ya yarda da shi, ya ruwaito cewa; Manzon Allah tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, ya ce; wanda ya ce;
سُبْحَانَ اللهِ الْعَظِيمِ وَبِحَمْدِهِ. 
Subhanal-lahil azeem wabihamdih. 
(sau dari)
	... A rana, za a kankare masa zunubansa ko da sun kasance kamar kumfar teku ne (wajen yawa).

Abu Ayyub Al-Ansari, Allah ya yarda da shi, ya ruwaito daga Manzon Allah, tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, cewa ya ce: "Wanda Ya ce;
لاَ إِلهَ إِلاَّ اللهُ وَحْدَهُ لاَ شَرِيكَ لَهُ، لَهُ الْمُلْكُ وَلَهُ الْحَمْدُ، وَهُوَ عَلَى كُلِّ شَيْءٍ قَدِيرٌ. (عشر مرة).
La ilaha illal-lahu wahdahu la shareeka lah, lahul-mulku walahul-hamd, wahuwa 'ala kulli shayin

(sau goma).
	To kamar wanda ya 'yanta bayi hudu ne daga cikin 'ya'yan (Annabi) Isma'ila.

Daga Sa'ad bn Abi Wakkas, Allah ya yarda das hi, yace; mun kasance a wajen Manzon Allah, tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, sai ya ce; "Yanzu dayanku yana kasa aikata kyawawan ayyuka dubu a kowace rana? Sa'ad bn Abi Wakkas, Allah ya yarda da shi, ya ce; "Wani balaraben kauye ya zo wajen Manzon Allah, tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gareshi, sai ya ce; koya mini wadansu kalmomi da zan rika fada. Sai ya ce; ka ce;

Ta mika mashi ya amsa,tare da murmushi a fuskar shi.

Kallo ta bishi dashi na mamaki!

[1/28, 10:11 PM] Maryam Marubuciya: *JARABTAR MU*

Yadda Annabi, Tsira Da Amincin Allah Su Tabbata A gareshi, Yake Kirga Tasbihinsa.

Daga Abdullahi bn Amr, Allah ya yarda da shi, yace; Na ga Annabi tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, yana kirga tasbihi da (yatsun) hannun damansa.


DEDICATED TO...MY SISTERS😘

Page-17-18


 _Bisssimillahir Rahmanir_ _Rahim_


--------------Walkiyar da akayi ne yasa nace daman akwai hadari a garin?

Haba shiyasa yau akayi zafi,mu kuma muka samu chini ki,ashe hadari ne.

Kafin na ida tunanin saidai naji sab kowar ruwa!!

Alhamdullah!! Yau na tsira da zuwan Lado,duk na chinshi ai bazai fito cikin ruwa ba,iska na kadawa lokaci guda naji zuciyata tamkar an wanketa.

Wani sabon littafi ne da Baba ya siyoman na dauka,nafara karan tawa sosai littafin ya dauki hankali na.

Tun farkon fejin littafin,mai littafin ya fadi tayaya zaki zabi mijin aure,ya kawo hadisai da kisso shi.

Littafi yayi dadi sosai komai yabi shi daki-daki.

Juya bayan littafin nayi,dan ganin sunan littafin,FANNUL TA'AMULI BAINAR ZAUJEN,murmushi nayi nace"lalle Baba na sona duk abinda yasan zan karu shiya keyi man,ko ya azani hanya,ina mai bukatar a barni na zabi miji,mai irin suffofin da mai littafin nan ya saka,ba irin Lado,da ace kowa ce mace zata karanta tashi lalle kam da ta karu da yawa.



Ya kamata iyaye su sani koda addini ya basu damar zaba ma 'ya 'yan su miji,to hakan ba yana nufin kowa ne namiji ba,ai addini ya bada kalar mijin da ya dace,yanzu idan aka aura man Lado,Inna bata tunanin mako mata da yaran da zan haifa.

Anya nayima yaran da zan haifa adalci,idan na zaban masu Lado a matsayin Uba??

Shiru nayi ina tunani lokaci guda hawaye suka wanke man fuskata.


            ************
Wanka Kursim ta fito,cikin sauri na shirya,ta kira ma aikatan part dinta,tabasu gyaran zogalan suyi mashi hadi kamin ta dawo.

Ta wuce part din Umman ta cikin fargaba mizata ce mata game da raunin fuskarta.

Tana isa masu kula da part din Umman ta suka ce tayi wajen Abban ta,hamdala tayi dan batason Umma tasan akwai matsala,dan tasan halin Umman ta yadda take sonta ace wani ya fitar mata jini,kila idan Umma taji,tasan bazata barta ta auri Annuwar ba.


Waya tayi aka kawo mata zogalan,ta kalli zogalan yayi kyau kamar wani abincin kasar waje.

Tace"Nasan zaka soshi,tunda ita komi nata mai dadi ne,lalle wadan nan mutanen Aljanu ne"kai tsaye tayi sashen shi.

Kaf..!!sukayi karo,saura kadan zogalan ya zube,tayi saurin rikewa,tare da ja daga baya!!

Kallon shi take cikin mamaki!!

Tace"Ina zakaje bayan ruwa akeyi tun dazun??kuma ai daka kirani nazo na kaika"juyawa yayi ciki tabi bayan shi.


Zauna tayi kusa da kujerar da yake zauna,ta aje mashi zogalan,tace"gashi nan na kawo maka"kallo yabi zogalan dashi,kamar baita ba ganin zogala ba.


Spoon ya dauka yafara chii,yana ya mutsa fuska kamar.

Yana tamnewa sai yayi murmushi,yana kallon zogalan.

Mutuwar zaune Kursim tayi,lalle sun gamu da Aljanu.

Miyasa yake son komi nata??hadda zogalan ma,hadda sakar ma zogalan murmushi,lalle zata saka a bin cika mata mutane ne ko Aljanu.

Inama Hanan zata gane,maganar da take fada mata da gaske ne,akwai matsala fa akan mai furar nan.


Koka min ta gama tunanin da take,har ya chinye babu komi. 

Cikin sauri ta zuba ruwa ta aje mashi,baiko kalli ruwan ba.

Dauka tayi ta mika mashi ya amsa,tareda sakar mashi murmushi.

Juyar da fuskar shi yayi wani waje.

Yana gamawa ya mike tsaye,ya juya zaiyi hanyar bedroom dinshi.

Cikin sauri tasha gaban shi,ta langwabar da kai,ta reda da aza kanta bisa kirjin shi,tana shafawa tace"please Prince mu koma mu zauna fira fa nazo muyi,kuma inaga har yanzu ba'a dauke ruwan ba"tana maganar kamar zatayi kuka.

Tana gamawa yasa hannu ya fizge ta daga jikin shi,ya saketa ta fadi TUSS...!!


Yayi gaba kamar ba mutum yayi ma haka ba.

"Wash"Kursim tace,tayi saurin tashi daga faduwar da tayi.

Tabi bayan shi,lokacin har ya shige cikin daki.

A bakin Wadirop ta iske shi,zai cire kayan jikin shi.

Yana cire rigar suka hada ido.

Kallon shi take tamkar yau ta fara ganin shi.

Cikin sauri ya maida rigar ya tako inda take tsaye.

Ganin yadda ya chanza yasa hankalin ta tashi.

Kuma yanzu mitayi mashi??

Kallon ta yayi tun daga sama har kasa kana yace"ke wace irin mutum ce??na tsani ta kura,bani son a cika matse man,sannan dan yana gidan ku zaki shigoman daki bata reda naiman izini ba,haka aka koya maki"kallon shi take idon ta cike da hawaye.

"Bana son ganinki kada ki kara shigo man daki inba da izini na ba,idan nan gidan kune,banda iko dashi saina kama daki a Hotel"kuka Kursim take sosai wannan wane irin kaskan cine??

"Fita"yace cikin tsawa!!

Cikin sauri tabar dakin tana kuka da gudu.

Zama yayi bakin bed dinshi ya rike kai,mizaima yarin yar nan tabar zuwa inda yake.

Jinshi yake tamkar wanda yayi shekara.

Wayar shi ya dauko ya danna kiran Abie.

Yau kamar zaiyi kuka,babu ko gaisuwa yace"Abie wallahi zan maka biyayya akan duk abinda kace,amman kabar ni nadawo gida na gaji"shiru Abie yayi.


Sai chan yace"wani abu akayi maka?"cikin sauri yace"a'a kawai dai ban saba da garin bane"Abie yace"to ai bakayi abinda aka turoka ba dan har yanzu banga alamun so ko shakuwa ba".

"wallahi Abie zanyi duk abinda kakeso na tuba"cikin sauri Abie yace"harda auren Kursim kenan"shiru yayi ya kasa magana.

Abie yace"nabaka nan da kwana biyu kayi tunani"daga haka ya kashe wayar.


Shiru yayi ya rasa abinda ke mashi dadi.

Bayan yayi shirin kwanciya bacci yaki zuwa.

Tashi yayi ya tsaya bakin window yana kallon taurarin dake a sama.

Yarasa minene mafita yanzu.

Kode ya amince idan antashi auren ya gudu yaje wata kasa ne?.

Haka za'ayi,sai kuma ya tuna wani abu sai yayi tsoki.


 A ban garen Kursim tana komawa korar masu aikinta tayi ta fada tsakiyar gado tana kuka.

Lalle kam duk wahalar da take,bazai taba sonta ba.

Baita ba yaba mata ba kullun a kwayar idon shi idan ta kalla babu sonta a ciki ko kadan.

Haka tayi ta kuka har dare yayi nisa.

Kode ta yafe Prince dan babu alamun sonta gashi bai dauke ta da daraja ba,bata da muhimman ci kenan a rayuwar shi.

Kila idan na aure shi ya chanza,to taya zan aure shi??

Haka tai ta ihu ita kade tana bazan iya ba!!

Har barawo bacci yayi gaba da ita.

A ban garen Annuwar ya kasa samun mafita har dare yayi nisa

            ************

Koda na tashi da safe yauma kamar kullun nasan aiki na,haka nayi nagama,na fara aikin fura.

Na daka sai ga Ma'u ta fito daga dakin Inna tana mika tare da hamma,ta kalle tace"Nanan duwa"nayi shiru tasan na tsani sunan.

Tace kibar wannan abun kizo ki sayawo man waina tashar Ado.

Nace"amman ai'kin ga aiki nike,sauri nike da fita tallah da wuri,gashi zanje diban zogala"shiru tayi tana kallo na,yaushe har nayi bakin da zan fadi mata magana.


Juya wa tayi cikin sauri ta koma dakj,sai gata ta fito tare da Inna.

Wata katuwar muciyar tuwo Inna ta dauko tayo kaina tana fadin.

"Daman jiya da bakin cikin ki na kwana na rashin zuwan Lado,kuma yanzu dan na kyale ki shine zaki bata ma yar tawa rai,yarinya mai Uwa da Uba,dakuma gata gaba da baya,shine saboda bakin ciki kike so ta zauna a cikin hawala,yunwa ta kamata da gatan ta"ganin da gaske tayo kaina tana kara fadin"ai gwara na sabattaki da ki sabatta min ita da yunwa,ko kin mutu Uban ki kade keda takaici,itafa?"

Nayi hanyar waje ina cewa"Dan Allah Inna kiyi hakuri"ganin idon ta ya rufe yasa nayi saurin fita.

Tace"idan kika fita,kika dawo kinsan sauran kizo ki idaman fura ta,yar bakin ciki da Lado yazo jiya,niyyah ta yau ko tallah bakiyi,dan yadda Lado ke kashe ma Mace kudi idan yana neman aure shine ni kike mani bakin ciki,to wallahi gwara na sabatta ki,dawa ne bakin ciki zanji kenan?kibata man kibata ma diyata baki isa ba zaki dawo ki iskeni".

Nide ina lade jikin katan ga inajin ta.


          ***************

"Morning Princess Am back wooo"ta ida maganar cikin waka.


Yadda taga Kursim yasa tayi saurin karasawa bisa godan!

Bubbuga bayan ta take tana cewa"Kursim Hanan ce bude idon ki,miya same ki"a hankali Kursim ta bude ido tana kallon Hanan.

Da fuskar ta wadda hawaye suka bushe.

Jikin Hanan Kursim ta kwantar da kanta tace"Hanan ina tsanar kaina saboda son da nike mashi,bazai taba sona ba,kiyiman addu'a,Hanan yace kada na kara rabar shi,baison ganina,dami zanji da son shi,koda kiyayyar da yake man?"ta ida maganar cikin kuka.

Hanan tace"miya farune?"nan Kursim ta fada mata komi.

Cikin sauri Hanan ta zame jikinta ta mike tsaye!!

Idon ta sun rufe ranta a bace"Kursim tace minene haka ina zaki?"kai tsaye tace"Umma zan fada mawa,chin kashin yayi yawa koda ke ba Princess bace,wulakan ci yayi yawa,baison darajar dan Adam ba,Umma zan fada mawa nasan zata taka mashi burki,yasan dawa yake tare"cikin sauri Kursim ta rikota tace"ki rufaman asiri kada kowa yaji,wannan sirri nane,nafa da maki,idan har kina sona da gaske,kawai kitaya ni da addu'a,idan kuma kika fada ma Umma baki man adalci ba,kinfi kowa sanin Umma".

Dawowa tayi ta zauna tare da yin tagumi ranta a bace.

Kallon Kursim tayi tace"wallahi na rantse maki,sai Prince ya duka da kafar shi yana rokan ki,akan ki aure shi,kiba shi jikin da yake wulakan tawa,sai ya nemi hada jiki dake,na rantse naki"shiru kursim tayi.

Tana tunanin taya za'ayi hakan ya faru,lalle kam har yanzu Hanan bata san waye Prince ba.

Taya za'ayi ya duka mata har yana rokan ta,wannan maganar bazata yiyu ba.

To amman mi Hanan zatayi ne??

    
           *************

Tashi nayi ina lekan Inna dake tsaye da muciya taki koma daki.

Nasan idan Inna ta buga man,muciyar nan kila ai ban iya tashi.

Yanzu ya zanyi kenan,gashi Inna batajin lallashi idan ranta ya baci zuciyar ta makan cewa takeyi.

Ju yawa nayi,karaf idona suka hadu dana Lado!!

Yana bisa jakin shi,rike da sanda wadda yake bugun jakin da ita.

Washe man jajayen hakuran sa yayi,yana niyyar sabko wa.

Haba ai bansan lokacin da nayi cikin gida a guje ba.

Nafisan azabar Inna akan kallon Lado.

Kaf...!!mukayi karo da Inna!!

Zaroo ido nayi waje tamkar zasu fito...!!

Qurrah
*JARABTAR MU*

Page-19-20



 _Bissimillahir Rahmanir_ _Rahim_ 



----------------Mari ta kai man,wanda saida na duka kasa,ina ɗagowa ta sake marina a dayan bangaren kumatu na.

Na dafe kunci tare da sakin wani irin ihu.

Lado ne ya shigo zauren da gudu!!

Ganin shi yasa Inna ta fara cewa "yanzu ki samu miji kamar Lado a garin nan shine zaki dinga gudun shi,duk garin nan wake kashe ma mace kudi kamar shi?Dan Allah kayi hakuri Malam Lado,kila ma kunya ce tasa ta gudu,amman aiba halinta bane"

Washe katon bakin shi yayi tare da wasu jajayen hakoran shi da goro ya ba ta hakuri yace ba komai ,yana sosa keya yace"kada adamu Inna ai babu komai ,sai a hankali zamu saba"


Sakin baki nayi ina kallon shi,tsoho kamar wannan yana duka ma Inna,ko kunya baiji ba hadda su cewa Inna.

Hannu ya saka a cikin dauɗaɗɗiyar rigar shi da tasha faci,ya dauko jakka biyar(dubu Daya) yaba Inna.

Jikin Inna har kyarma yake wajen amsa,tare da cewa "kai Malam Lado basu yi yawa ba" kara washe baki yayi yace "kada kidamu ban son ta fita tallah ne yau".

Kallon banza nayi mashi,taya za'ayi mutumin dake jan jaki yayi wannan kyautar,ga matan shi chan su ne suke ciyar da  kansu,yaran shi tamkar almajirai haka zaka gansu, Allah dai ya shirya wasu mazan,kallar su Lado,gida bai koshi ba ankai waje.


Dunguzar da Inna tayi min tasa na dawo daga duniyar tunanin da nike tace "bakiji yana magana tun dazun? Matsa "ta turani har saida na kusa fada mashi.

Kana ta dunga godiya har kasa takai,ta harareni ta shige ciki,tabar mu a zaure.


Kuraman ido yayi yana kallona,yana lasar labban shi tamkar wani maye sai kara matsoni yake.

Sai a lokacin na kalli jiki na,riga da zane ne,saman rigar ya matseni sosai,komai nawa yafito fili,kusan ma rabin kirjina a bude yake, saboda yadda rigar ta kamani.


Cikin sauri na warware Dan kwalin kaina,nan gashina ya bayyana baki silik,ga doguwar jela data sabko bisa baya na,da ɗan kwalin na rufe gabana.

Kasa magana yayi sai wani in-inah yake,yana kara lasar bakin shi,yana zaro ido,ƙut kakeji yana hadiye miyau.

Daya matso sai in gusa,da kyar ya iya furta"nikam bazan iya jira ba,nan da sati guda nike so a kai man ke,zan baki kulawa kinji,dan idan na aure ki sai nayi wata ban fita ba,zan maki komai kike so kinji"


Kara ɓata fuska nayi na turo baki,jikin shi har kyar ma yake,yasa hannu zai tabo bakina.

Nayi saurin matsawa tare da sakar mashi harara tamkar kwayar idona zata fado.

Yace "kiyi hakuri yar gidan Liman,wallahi komai naki kyau gare shi,kin san yanzu dole na koma gida ban iya zuwa wajen aiki,jikina har kyar ma yake "ya fiddo kudi yace "ga wa'innan duka ki dauka,kibar ni  ko taba ki inyi naji sauki,har da bakin nan duk garin nan babu mai irin shi sannan ga..."daga mashi hannu nayi nace ya isa haka.

Dan nasan kirjina zai ce,na kulle su da ɗan kwalina amman yadda yake kallon wajen tamkar ya samu  wani abun kallo.

Ya miko man kudi,nace"wallahi koka fita cikin zauren nan ko nayi maka ihu kwarto,yadda har gidan ka sai sunji,kuma su fito na nunaka,kuma kasan harda matar ɗan ka"

Cikin sauri yace" Allah ya baki hakuri,duk me yakawo wannan daman dadin mu naso muji,na dauke maki wannan wahalar da kike ciki,kullum nazo na dinga biyan Innar ku,tabar aza maki tallah"kallon shi nayi.

Nace "nafison wahala ta kashe ni da inyi abinda kake so ,niba jahila bace, ba zan saba ma mahaliccina ba saboda abin duniya,kaje chan ka nemi dai-dai dakai"

Magana nike amman har yanzu ya kasa dauke idon shi daga kaina,kuma da alama magan ganuna basu yi mashi zafi ba.

Washe hakora yayi yace"zan tafi zan turo maki da sako kinji? kici fa kada kibari wani yaci "ganin yadda yake wani kara matsowa yasa nayi ciki da gudu.

Kici bis mukayi da Inna!!

  
             ***********
Kwance yake cikin bargo saboda yadda kan shi ke mashi ciwo,jiya kasa bacci yayi saboda rashin samun mafita.

A tunanin shi Kursim zata shigo, saboda yana son furar nan,kuma yasan tana da ita, shiru-shiru babu Kursim har rana tafarayi,gashi sosai kanshi ke ciwo, runtse ido yayi saboda yadda kan ke ciwo.


Gashi shi kaɗai ne a part ɗin shi,tun jiya yace bai son kowa sai idan shi ya kira su,hatta Sani da kullum suna tare sai idan yana tare da Kursim,shi kaɗai ke raba su.


Wayar shi ya jawo,ya shiga Call long dinshi, number Kursim yake nema yasan tana kiran shi,sau daya ya taɓa daukar kiranta,tunda yaji itace bai kara dauka ba,amman duk da haka kullum cikin kira da Message take.


Kallon number yake amman ya kasa kira,sai ma tsakin daya ke faman yi.


Kashe wayar yayi baki daya,ya juya ya runtse ido.

Yanzu laifin iyayen shi yake gani,saboda me zasu kawo shi a nan?

        
   Wanka Kursim tayi,Hanan tasa aka shiryama Kursim abinci.

Tare suka zauna bisa kujerun Dining table,da kanta Hanan ta zubama Kursim abinci.

Kallon Kursim tayi da tayi tagumi,cire hannun Hanan tayi,kana Kursim ta kalle ta.

Dan karamin tsaki Hanan tayi,ta cigaba da chin abincin.

Har ta gama Kursim bata ko taɓa abincin ba.

Hanan ta dauki wayar ta tayi kira"Hello akwai jirgin  da zaije Abuja yau?"kallon ta Kursim tayi cike da mamaki!!

Sabke wayar tayi,ta dauki hand bag ɗinta tace"zan je gida,daga chan na wuce Abuja yau babu jirgi sai gobe,zanje a chan na kwana,sai anjima"dafe kai Kursim tayi.

Tace"Haba Hanan saboda matsala fa kika zo kuma shine zaki koma,bayan komai ba muyi ba,idan kika barni dawa zanyi shawara,Umma ce kuma kinsan halinta,ki taimaka min"rike jakar ta tayi.

Dawowa Hanan tayi ta zauna bisa kujerar falon Kursim.

Tace"wallahi Kursim ban iya daure wa,zuciyata zafi take min,meyasa zakiso wanda baison ki,bayan ga mutane da yawa suna bukatar soyayyar ki,kiyi tunani,bawai ban son soyayyar ku bane ba,a'a nima ina maki fatan kisamu abunda kike so amman wulakan ci yayi yawa,gashi yau saboda baki ganshi ba kin kasa chin abinci,duk kinman ta wulakancin da yayi maki jiya"shiru Kursim tayi.

Da fata Hanan tayi tace"ya kamata yau kada kije inda yake,dan yasan ya maki laifi,kuma ai karema yana da rana,wallahi a yau sai yayi kewar ki,mutum fa ance rahama ne, please ki daure"dariya Kursim tayi.

Tace"I can't do that"zaro ido Hanan tayi,dariya Kursim tayi tare da bugun bayan Hanan.

Tace"Ohh sorry My Friend Am just kidding"dariya Hanan tayi.

   
            ***********

Kunne na Inna taja kamar zata cire,zanyi kara ta tushe man baki,ta ja ni ciki,ingizani tayi na fada akan turmi.

Tace"Amman wannan diya da wulakan ci kike,yanzu ina kallon an baki kudi,amman ƙiri-ƙiri kika ƙi karba, saboda kada na amfana da ke,yan kudin da nike so na tara,na saima Ma'u samiru da tasho shi dasu kike ma bakin ciki,kada na fidda diya ta kunya ko?"


Cikin kuka nace Inna jikina fa zai taba.


Mari takaiman tace"duk iskan cin da kikeyi idan kinje tallah kina dawowa da kudi, saboda su a hannun ki kike ajesu,shiyasa su ba zaki hanasu ba,sai Lado,duk iskan chin shi, bazai yishi cikin zaure ba,iyakar shi ya shafe ki,to miye a ciki tunda ya aje maki kudi,daman tunda naga yana lasar baki nasan za'a rina addu'a nike tayi Allah yasa ki bashi haɗin kai, ashe saida kika yi halin naki na bakin ciki "kuka nike sosai.

Nace"Haba Inna hakan mafa zina ne,mutane ke daukar wannan ba komai ba ,amman wallahi haramun ne,namiji ya taɓa jikin mace,Inna duk kan gabobin jikin mu ranar gobe kiyama duk sai sun bada sheda akan mu,hakan dakike dauka ba komai ba,to wallahi Inna Zina ce,bata yadda kike tunani ba, sannan ba ta yadda kika san ake Zina ba kawai a fili ba, to zina harta Ido akwai,wallahi Inna bazan iya ba "Kalamaina mai makon su saka Inna tayi laushi saima har zukata da su kayi.

Ina gamawa takaiman bugu a baki tace"har kin isa kice zakiman wa'azi, saboda tsabar bakin ciki irin naki kada na fidda diyata kunya,to kudi sai kin samo su,yau ba zaki fita tallah ba dan kada Lado ya ganki,amman kishir ya gobe dana yau dana gobe zaki hada"shiru nayi na wuce na isa wajen daki na,na ci gaba.


Da cewa "da ache inada Uwa na tabbata bazata aikeni zuwa aikata Zina ba,mutane suna daukar hada jiki da namiji ba komai bane ba,sun manta Ubangiji haka yace kada mu kusanci Zina,kusan tar ta ma haramun ne inaga aikata ta,ta kowane irin yanayi ana aikata Zina,jan dogon numfashi nayi,inama ace inada mahaifiyar da zata azani akan turbar gaskiya".

Ya Allah kasa na zama uwa tagari ga 'ya'yana.

A hankali nike dakan saboda jiki na babu kwari.


               ***********
Sai wajen azuhur ya farka daga barci,ya mike waya ya kira Sani yazo ya hada mashi ruwan wanka.

Bayan ya fito sallah yayi ya shirya cikin kananun kaya.


A falo ya fito ya zauna abinci aka hada mashi a gaban shi.

Haka ya yini,lalle maganar Hanan gaskiya ce mutum rahama ne, saboda yau Anuwar jinshi yake yau garin yafi kullun rashin dadi saboda yadda ya yini shi kaɗai.

Hakan yasa tunani yayi mashi yawa.

Lambun kusa dashi ya fita,yayi tsaye hannu cikin aljihu yarasa me zaiyi ya gaji da garin nan laluba wayar shi yayi yakira Abie yace mashi shi dai ya amince gobe ya koma gida.


Ya laluba aljihun shi babu ita.

Dan guntun tsaki yayi,ya tuna tana daki,kuma a kashe.

Hanyar dakin shi ya nufa tare da tunani iri-iri shi dai ya rasa mafita, amince wa da auran Kursim shine mafita.

Tsaye yayi yana tunanin kamar zaiyi missing din wani abu.

To minene kenan???


Kursim zaune suke a falon ta Hanan na bata labarin abinda ya faru Abuja,daga kai kawai take hankali ta baya tare da ita.

Tana son ganin Anuwar ta kowane hali.

Waya Hanan ta dauka ta latsa Kira.

Tace"Hello Doctor,kaga message dina ina bukatar medicine din da na rubuto maka, okay babu a Katsina sai Legos Ina bukatar ayima order dinshi yanzu,ina bukatar shi a yau"Kursim kallon ta tayi kowa ne magani ne babu a Katsina sai Legos??

Tsaki Hanan tayi tare da cewa"why dis question"sai tayi tsaki ta kashe wayar.

Kursim tace"Hanan wane magani ne babu katsina har sai Legos?"tsaki tayi tace"manta kawai"Kursim bata damu ba suka cigaba da firar su.


          ************

Ina gama aiki na kaima Inna bako ta kalleni ba, saboda wai inama 'yarta bakin cikin samiru,idan abin kirkine tasa 'yar tata mana.

Daki na koma naje nayi wankan tsarki,naji dadin samun tsarki,ina son daukar qur'ani dan dashi kaɗai nike samun sauki.


Daki na koma na gabatar da Sallah.

Addu'a kawai nike Allah ya maido da Baba lafiya,ko mike faruwa bai dawo ba,anya lafiya??

Hakade nayita tunani.

Nafito zanyi alwallar isha'i,Inna ta fito da alama fita zata yi ,tunda matan kauyan mu na aure basu fita saida daddare.

Tace"kinsan Lado yace zai zo ko,to bari duk abinda ya kawo ko lasawa kikayi,sai na shaƙe ki ya fito,ki kaima Ma'u ta aje tana daki"kai na daga.

Ta fita tana bala'i tana kara fadin idan naci sai ta shakeman makoshi.

Ni dai naje nayi Sallah,ina gamawa saiga yaro ya shigo ana kiran Nana.

Cikin sauri Ma'u tace kace gatanan.

Ma'u ta shigo d'akin ta zauna bakin gado tace"Nana ana maki sallama,kuma ina ga Lado ne"shiru nayi raina a ɓace.

Tace "Haba 'yar kanwa ta tashi mana kada Inna ta iskeki kinsan sauran,kuma duk abinda ya baki ki amso kinji kanwata"shiru nayi ina kallon Ma'u.

Lalle duk turbar da Uwa ta aza diyar ta dashi take tashi,duk halin su daya da Inna,ta riga ta azata akan kwadayin abin hannun wani,wai yau Ma'u kece man kanwa magana cikin lalama,saboda kwadai.

Tashi tayi sai gata da wani gyale mai kyau,ta mikoman tace"saka shi kitafi,har mutunen ki nasaka maki(turare) kinji"har ta juya sai ta dawo tace"kada ki yadda ya bata maki rayuwa,kada kisakar mashi jikin ki,wallahi garin nan,idan mace tayi cikin shege korarta ake,kada ki yadda,kuma kada kice ma Inna nace maki wani Abu kinji"daga kai nayi.

Lalle ashe Uwa ke koyama diya hali marar kyau kenan,kila Ma'u ba halin ta bane,Innar ta ke koya mata.

Tare muka fito har bakin zaure ta rakoni tace"kinji abinda na fada maki,kila Inna so take a kore ki daga kauyen nan,shiyasa tayi haka ki kiyaye kodan Baba"kai na daga tare da murmushi,yau itace rana ta farko da Ma'u ta yiman abin arziki.

Na shiga cikin zaure ita kuma ta juya cikin gida.

Har kasa na duka nace ina wuni ya amsa yana washe hakora.

Laidar hannun shi ya mikoman yace"kici kaza ce kada ki sam ma kowa,kinga tasu Inna cikin bakar leda,gobe zan dawo,ya fiddo kudi yaban"

Na amsa nayi godiya yace "banson godiya nide burina kici kazar nan ke kadai "na daga kai.

Yanzu kam mamaki yaban,bai wani tsaya kallo na ba,ya juya yace sai ya dawo gobe.

Nace kila Allah ya shirya shi,tunda Allah shike shirya bayin shi.


Ina zuwa na kaima Ma'u duka nace a jema Inna tace "kidau leda guda mana tunda leda biyu ne"girgiza kai nayi,na wuce dakina.


Man tawa nayi ashe gyalen Ma'u ne,dakin su nayi,ina shiga na iske Ma'u ta bude kazar ledar da yace tawa ce tana chi.

Kallon ta nike tana chi hankali kwance sai kuma na tuna ai yace kada na sake naba kowa.

Innalillahi!! 

Cikin sauri na fizge ledar!


Inna ce tayi sallam ta shigo ta iskeni rike da leda, Ma'u dai kallo na take cikin mamaki!!

Shiru nayi tomi zance ma Inna??

Towai ma meyasa yace inchi ni kadai??

Mike cikin kazar ne??


Qurrah

Falalar Tasbihi, Da Godiya Ga Allah, Da Yin La ilaha Illal Lah, da yin Kabbara

Abu Huraira, Allah ya yarda da shi, ya ruwaito cewa; Manzon Allah tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, ya ce; wanda ya ce;
سُبْحَانَ اللهِ الْعَظِيمِ وَبِحَمْدِهِ. 
Subhanal-lahil azeem wabihamdih. 
(sau dari)
	... A rana, za a kankare masa zunubansa ko da sun kasance kamar kumfar teku ne (wajen yawa).

Abu Ayyub Al-Ansari, Allah ya yarda da shi, ya ruwaito daga Manzon Allah, tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, cewa ya ce: "Wanda Ya ce;
لاَ إِلهَ إِلاَّ اللهُ وَحْدَهُ لاَ شَرِيكَ لَهُ، لَهُ الْمُلْكُ وَلَهُ الْحَمْدُ، وَهُوَ عَلَى كُلِّ شَيْءٍ قَدِيرٌ. (عشر مرة).
La ilaha illal-lahu wahdahu la shareeka lah, lahul-mulku walahul-hamd, wahuwa 'ala kulli shayin

Babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai Allah, shi kadai; babu abokin tarayya a gareShi. Mulki ya tabbata gare Shi kuma Shi mai ikone a kan komai.
(sau goma).
	To kamar wanda ya 'yanta bayi hudu ne daga cikin 'ya'yan (Annabi) Isma'ila.


Abu Huraira, Allah ya yarda da shi, ya ce; Manzon Allah, tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare Shi, ya ce; "Akwai wasu kalmomi biyu masu saukin fada a harshe, masu nauyi a kan mizani (na ayyuka ranar kiyama), masu soyuwa ga (Allah) mai yawan jin kai (su ne).
سُبْحَانَ اللهِ وَبِحَمْدِهِ سُبْحَانَ اللهِ العَظِيمِ.
Subhanal-lahi wabihamdih, wasubhanal-lahil-'azeem.
Tsarki ya tabbata ga Allah, tare da Shi; tsarki ya tabbata ga Allah Mai girma.


Abu Huraira, Allah ya yarda da shi, ya ce; "Manzon Allah, tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, ya ce; Fadin:
سُبْحَانَ اللهِ، والْحَمْدُ للهِ، لَا إِلَهَ إلاَّ اللهُ واللهُ أَكْبَرُ.
Subhanal-lah, walhamdu lillah, la ilaha illal-lah wallahu akbar.

Tsarki ya tabbata ga Allah, kuma yabo ya tabbata ga Allah, kuma babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai Allah, kuma Allah ne Mafi girma.
	… Shi ya fi soyuwa gare ni daga dukkan abin da rana ta hudo a kansa".


Daga Sa'ad bn Abi Wakkas, Allah ya yarda das hi, yace; mun kasance a wajen Manzon Allah, tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, sai ya ce; "Yanzu dayanku yana kasa aikata kyawawan ayyuka dubu a kowace rana? Sai wani mutum cikin wadanda suke zaune tare da shi ya ce; "Ta yaya dayanmu zai aikata kyawawan ayyuka duub? Sai ya ce; "Ya yi tasbihi (ya ce;
سُبْحَانَ اللهِ.
Subhanal-lah.(3)

(Tsarki ya tabbata ga Allah)
	... Sau dari, saia rubuta masa kyawawan ayyuka dubu, ko a kankare masa zunubai dubu.

Jabir, Allah ya yarda da shi, ya ce; Annabi, tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, ya ce; "Wanda ya ce;
 سُبْحَانَ اللهِ العَظِيمِ وبِحَمْدِهِ.
Subhanal-lahil-'azeemi wabihamdih.

Tsarki ya tabbata ga Allah Mai girma, tare da godiya gare Shi.
	... Za a dasa masa bishiyar dabino a cikin aljanna.


Daga Abu Musa al-'ash'ari, Allah ya yarda da shi, ya ce; Manzon Allah, tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, ya ce mini: "Ya Abdullah bn Kais! Ba na shiryar da kai ba zuwa ga wata taska daga cikin taskokin aljanna ba? Sai na ce. Shiryar da ni. Sai ya ce: ka ce:
 لاَ حَوٍلَ وَلاَ قُوَّةَ إِلاَّ بِاللهِ.
La  hawla wala kuwwata illa billah.

Babu dabara babu karfi sai da Allah.


Daga Samura bn Jundub, Allah ya yarda da shi, ya ce Manzon Allah, tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, ya ce; "Mafi soyuwar zance a wajen Allah shi ne;
سُبْحَانَ اللهِ، والْحَمْدُ للهِ، ولاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللهُ واللهُ أَكْبَرُ.
Subhanal-lah, walhamdu lillah, wala ilaha illal-lah, wallahu akbar.

Tsarki ya tabbata ga Allah, kuma yabo ya tabbata ga Allah, kuma babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai Allah, kuma Allah ne mafi girma.
	... Babu komai a gare ka ko da wane daya daga cikinsu ka fara'.



Sa'ad bn Abi Wakkas, Allah ya yarda da shi, ya ce; "Wani balaraben kauye ya zo wajen Manzon Allah, tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gareshi, sai ya ce; koya mini wadansu kalmomi da zan rika fada. Sai ya ce; ka ce;
لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللهُ وَحْدَهُ لاَ شَرِيكَ لَهُ، اللهُ أَكْبَرُ كَبِيراَ والْحَمْدُ للهِ كَثِيراً، سُبْحَانَ اللهِ رَبِّ العَالَمِينَ، لاَ حَوْلَ وَلاَ قُوَّةَ إِلاَّ بِاللهِ العَزِيزِ الْحَكِيمِ.
La ilaha illal-lah, wahdahu la shareeka lah, Allahu akbaru kabeera, walhamdu lillahi katheera, subhanal-lahi rabbil-'alameen, la hawla wala kuwwata illa billahil-'azeezil-hakeem.

Babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai Allah; babu abokin tarayya a gare Shi. Allah ne mafi girma (Ina girmama Shi) girmamawa. Kuma godiya ta tabbata ga Allah da yawa. Tsarki ya tabbata ga Allah, Ubangijin talikai. Babu dabara babu karfi sai da Allah, Mabuwayi mai hikima.
	... Sai mutumin ya ce; "Wadannan na Ubangijina ne, ni kuma nawa fa/ sai yace ka ce;
اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لِي، وارْحَمْنِي، واهْدِنِي، وارْزُقْنِي.
Allahummagh-fir lee, warhamnee, wahdinee, warzuknee.

Ya Allah! Ka yi mini gafara, K aji kai na, Ka shirye ni, Ka azurta ni.

Tarik Ibn Ashyam al-Ashja'i, Allah ya yarda das hi, y ace; "Idan mutum ya musulunta, sai Annabi, tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, ya koya masa sallah, sannan ya umarce shi ya rika addu'a da wadannan kalmomin.
اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لِي، وارْحَمْنِي، واهْدِنِي، وارْزُقْنِي.
Allahummagh-fir lee, warhamnee, wahdinee, warzuqnee.

Ya Allah! Ka yi mini gafara, Ka ji kai na, Ka shirye ni, Ka amintar da ni daga bala'i, Ka azurta ni.

Jabir Ibn Abdullah, Allah ya yarda da shi, ya ce Manzon Allah, tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, ya ce; "Mafificiyar addu'a ita ce (fadin):
الْحَمْدُ للهِ.
Alhamdu lillah

Dukkan yabo ya tabbata ga Allah.
…. Kuma mafificin zikiri shi ne (fadin):
لاَ إِلَه إِلاَّ اللهُ.
  La ilaha illal-lah.

Babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai Allah.


Manzon Allah tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, ya ce; "Ayyuka nagargaru masu wanzuwa su ne (fadin):
سُبْحَانَ اللهِ، والْحَمْدُ للهِ، لاَ إِلَهَ إَلاَّ اللهُ واللهُ أَكْبَرُ وَلاَ حَوْلَ وَلاَ قُوَّةَ إلاَّ باللهِ.
Subhanal-lah, walhamdu lillah, la ilaha illal-lah, wallahu akbar, wala hawla wala kuwwata illa billah.


Tsarki ya tabbata ga Allah, kuma yabo ya tabbata ga Allah, kuma babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai Allah, kuma Allah ne Mafi firma, kuma babu dabara babu karfi sai da Allah.







 _Bissimillahir Rahmanir_ _Rahim_


---------------Cikin zafin nama Inna ta fizge laidar dake hannu na, tace, "ke har kin isa dan Ma'u na cin abinda Lado ya kawo ki fizge, to kinma kan ki, ba zaki ko lashe ta ba!".

   T ajiye laidar a gaban Ma'u dake zaune, baki a sake tana kallona.

Nace; "wallahi Inna haka yace, wannan laidar naci ni kadai kada na ba kowa, ga laidar da yace ita ce taku".

Wani kallo tayi min, tace;

"Idan baki fita dakin nan ba, idan na ingiza ki sai bakin ki ya fashe!!".

Cikin sanyin jiki na bar dakin.


A dakina na koma na zauna, yunwa nake ji sosai, saboda yau sau guda naci abinci, shi yasa wani lokaci ma nafi aon zuwa tallar, dan zanci na koshi.


Fitowa nayi a hankali nake tafiyar dan kada Inna taji motsi.

Daman ni a duhu nike kwana banda fitila, har nakai Madafa, a hankali na bude tukunyar dake bisa murhun, na lalubi luddai na motsa naji akwai dumamen tuwon jiya.

Cikin sanda na koma daki na, na dauko roba ta da nake cin abinci, kalar ta almajirai na dawo na dauki guzurai biyu na koma.

Na zauna na saka hannu cikin tuwon, naji kamar ya lalace, ina kaiwa baki na zubo shi kasa.

Miyar kuka ce tayi tsami, zafin da aka yi jiya da daddare tuwon dawa ne, kila hakan yasa ya saki.


Nayi zugun tare da tagumi ina hango kajin nan gaban Ma'u, jin cikina na juyawa yasa na nasa hannu naci haka nan ina chi, ina runtse ido har na gama.

Ina gamawa naje nasha ruwa na dawo nayi addu'a na kwanta, tare da tunanin Baba.


            *************

Yinin ranar da Kursim da Hanan a tare suka yini part din Kursim, sai da magriba suka je wajen Umma suka yi fira har dare yayi sosai kana suka dawo a gajiye.

Duk Hanan ta tsara haka ne dan kada Kursim taje wajen Prince.


A bisa kujerun parlon shi yake, ya aza kafafun shi bisa center table, yana rike da remote a hannu, ya rasa me zai kalla tunda ba kallo yake ba.


Wani tunani yayi sai yayi saurin naimo Baby tv, murmushi yake yana kallon cartoon din ciki, lumshe ido yayi tare da rungume pillow din kujerun shi, a hankali yace"MANAL", tare da kara rungume pillown a kirji.

Bude ido yayi ya ganshi rungume da pillow cikin sauri ya saki pillon, ya kashe tv, ya zauna ya rasa mike mashi dadi.

Tashi yayi ya koma bedroom ya zage labulen dakin shi yayi tsaye bakin window.


Sani ne ya shigo tare da sallama, Prince ya jiyo yana kallon shi yace; "Allah ya taimaki magajin Sarki ga abinci nan daga Gimbiya Kursim".

Hannu ya nuna mashi alamar ya fita, ya juya tare da cewa; "godiya nike, Allah ya kara lafiya da daukaka jikan Sarki". Bayan ya fita yana nan tsaye sai kuma ya tuna wani Abu, cikin sauri ya fito parlo.

Wajen abincin ya nufa yaga wani Jug, yayi saurin budewa, kamshin furar ya bugi hancin shi.

Lumshe ido yayi tare da sakin murmushi,yace; "Thank you Kursim".

Yayi maganar a fili tamkar Kursim na gaban shi, haka yayi maganar.


Sani ya iso cikin sauri ya matso kusa dashi, yace, "idan Yarima yayi man izini,zan zuba mashi furar". Da hannu ya nuna mashi ya dauko mashi Jug din, ya dauko Jug.


A bedroom ya zauna, Sani ya zuba furar a cikin cup ya mika mashi.

Ya amsa tare da yima Sani murmushi.

Cikin sauri Sani yace; "Allah ya tabbatar da farin ciki a rayuwar Yarima Anuwar".

Ya juya cikin mamaki. Anya ba tare da mai furar nan zasu koma ba, zanyi magana da martaba yace cikin ranshi.


Furar yake sha yana lumshe ido, lokaci guda Kursim ta shiga zuciyar shi, saboda ta bashi abin da yake so.

Sai yanzu yaga bai kyauta ba, tsaki yayi yace a fili; "ya kamata ta tsaya inda na ajiye ta, ban son shishigi, ba sai na kira Mai martaba ba zan zauna saboda fura nan, ina son furar". Ya lumshe ido.



A gajiye suke hakan yasa kowa na naimi wajen bacci, Hanan tace; "dazu fa ina jin ki, kyale ki nayi saboda Umma kina ba Lantana umarnin hada kayan abincin Prince".

"Mtwss!".  Tayi tsaki tace; "ai da kin bar shi baki kai mishi sauran furar ba ai dole yaci abinci, wallahi dan ina tare da Umma kada tayi zargin wani Abu yasa na kyale ki".

Dariya Kursim tayi tace; "ban son Doctor's da daukar zafi ba, masu hakuri aka sanku, wallahi na san idan ba furar aka kai mashi ba bazai ci komi ba, kuma ai kinga bako ne".

Hanan tayi tsaki tace; "ni fa yanzu nafi tsanar mai furar nan akan Manal, saboda Manal dan ya sota 'yar uwar shi ce, kuma kin ce an daura masu aure, kin ga ita matar shi ce, da sauki dan ya sota, amman ita fa wannan ace baicin komi sai nata, ni wallahi baccin kin hana ni, da tuni nasa an ruguje barandar an kore su".

Kursim tace; "lallai Hanan akwai daukar zafi".

Nan suka cigaba da fira har dare yayi nisa, kana sukayi bacci.


Haka a bangaren Anuwar ya yita tunani iri-iri har yayi bacci.



        *************

Tun asuba na fara aiki, saboda yau da ta jiya data yau zan hada, haka nayi aiki zuwa karfe goma na gama komai.


Har lokacin Ma'u bata tashi daga baccin da take yi, ina ji Inna na cewa wannan wane irin bacci ne haka, har rana ta fito baki tashi ba, sai wani murmushin banza kike.

Ni dai bance komai ba nayi wanka, na fito na dauki kaya na, nace; "Inna na tafi".

 Tace; "kada ki daukan min kudi". Na daga kai nayi gaba.

Na bar ta tana ta masifar wannan wane irin bacci ne haka Ma'u keyi??

Yau inda muke haduwa babu su Shatu, ga kayan sunfi na kullum nauyi, da kyar na iso nan din, ina ga ace naje birni dasu.


Ina nan zaune sai ga Shatu tare da Jabe a amalanken su.

Na mike nace yau na riga wasu, ina dariya.

Shatu tace; "lallai kam wannan furar fa ina zaki kaita? To taya ma aka yi kika daukota bisa kai?".

Dariya nayi nace; "haka nan na dauko ta amman fa da kyar na iso, har da ta jiya da ban yi tallah ba, shi ne ta hade min, shi yasa tun kamin ayi kiran sallah na fara aikin furar".

Jinjina kai tayi, fuskar ta kamar zata yi kuka tace; "Jabe sauko mu kama mu daura bisa".


Muna tafe muna fira, tana ban labarin yan uwan Maman ta dake burni, wai sun ce bata isa aure ba, dariya muke yi, nace;

"Basu san duk rugar ku babu mai girman ki ba, lallai kam, kin san birni sai sun girma sosai suke aurar da yaran su".

Haka dai muka yi ta labari na ban dariya har muka isa.


        ************

Yau ko da Kursim suka tashi, Hanan da kanta tayi mata Makeup kana tasa aka hada mata abin Break, suka ci Kursim ta dauki na Prince.


Tana zuwa ta iske a parlo zaune cikin kananun kaya yayi kyau sosai, hankalin shi yana a tv.

Ta kai kallon ta a tv, cikin mamaki take kallon shi.

"Me yasa yake kallon cartoon??".

Jim tayi tana tunani sai tace; "MANAL".

Juyowa yayi cikin sauri.

Tsuru-tsuru tayi kamar marar gaskiya, ashe a fili ta fada.

A hankali ta shigo ta ajiye mashi kayan Breakfast din.

Tace; "ina kwana, an tashi lafiya?". Ya amsa da kai.

Ta zuba mashi ta tura a gaban shi.

Ta tashi ta nufi bedroom din shi,ta iske a gyare, ta leka toilet din shi, nan ma a gyare, tayi tsaye tana tunanin mezata yi mashi ta burge shi??


Waya tayi Hanan, tace a aiko mata burner da turaren wuta masu kamshi.

Jim kadan ta fito ta ganshi zaune ya gama ci, fita tayi ta amso ta dawo.

Ta jona a bedroom din shi ta zuba turaren wuta, nan fa kamshi mai dadi ya fara tashi.

Ta dawo falo ta jona ta saka turaruruka masu kamshi, kamshi sai tashi yake mai dadin gaske, yana zaune rike da waya a hannu.

Bayan ta gama ta dawo ta zauna tace; "ko kana bukatar wani abu?"

   Kai ya girgiza tace; "fura ta kare muko ma ko musiyo?".

Mikewa yayi yana kallon ta.

Murmushi tayi tare da mika mashi hannu ya mikar da ita.

Juya wa yayi ya fara tafiya, ta mike tabi bayan shi.


Suna isa suka shiga mota babu mai yima kowa magana.

Kursim tana tunanin mezata yi ta burgeshi.

Shi kuma yana tunanin ya za'ayi idan ya koma gida babu fura?

Haka har suka isa wajen, idon shi a lumshe sai da yaji mota ta tsaya ya bude ido.

Ta fito ta bude mashi suka fito.


       ****************
Na fara sabawa da 'yan makaranta, hakan yasa yau hadda ni ake saida furar.


Shatu tace; "amso mana kudin mu wajen mutumin can dake tafowa". Na duba banga kowa ba.

Nace, "yana Ina?".

Tace, "gashi can wajen inda mata da mijin can suke zaune, daga nan kiyi masu tallah kinga yanzu suka iso".

Nace; "to".

Na doshi wajen ina tafiya tare da tsalle, zuciya ta cike da farin ciki.


          ***************

Kama hannun shi Kursim tayi, tana murmushi, hannun su sarke cikin nata, lumshe ido tayi tana jin sanyi a zuciyarta.


Jin ya saki hannun ta cikin sauri, yasa ta bude ido.

Kallon shi tayi yana kallon yarinyar dake saida fura, tana tafowa da dan gudun ta.

Tace; "lafiya me ya faru?".

Bai ko ji me tace ba.

Yace, "kalar tafiyar Manal". Cikin sauri ta kalle shi.

Ta zaro ido! tare da cewa; "Innalillahi!!".


Qurrah

Page-23-24





Daga Cikin Ayyukan Alheri Da Kyawawan Halaye

Jabir bn Abdullahi, Allah ya yarda da shi, ya ce; Manzon Allah, tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, ya ce; "Idan duhun dare ya gabato – ko kuma almuru ta yi – to ku hana 'ya'yanku fita, domin shaidanu suna bazuwa a wannan lokacin. Idan sa'a ta gota a cikin dare, to sai ku bar su. Ku rufe kofofi, kuma ku ambaci sunan Allah (yayin da kuke rufewa), domin Shaidan ba ya bude kofa rufaffiya. Ku zuge bakin salkunan ruwanku, kuma ku ambaci sunan Allah (yayin da kuke zugewa), kuma ku rufe kwanukan abincinku, ku ambaci sunan Allah (yayin da kuke rufewa); ko da ku dora wan;i abu ne a kan kwanukan. Kuma ku kashe fitilinku na a-ci-balbal.

Allah ya yi tsira da Aminci da albarka ga Annabinmu Muhammadu, da alayensa da Sahabbansa baki daya.



 _Bissimillahir Rahmanir_ _Rahim_



-------------------Tafiya nike inajin nishadi a zuciya,haka kawai na rasa minene dalili??


"Nana! Nana!! Nana!!".

Na juya cikin sauri jin yadda Shatu ta kirani.

Shanu ne na hango sunfi su dari sun nufo barandar mu, na yadda abinda ke hannu na na juya a guje.


Ina zuwa na dauke kwaryata, muka haye bisa dan tudun barandar.

Zasu shigo Shatu tace; " ajiye min inzo". Daga kai nayi, saboda har yanzu cikin tsoro nike, ina tsoron kada su fasa ma Inna kwarya.

Wajen fulanin dake tare dasu ta nufa, suna tafe suna chin awara sun saki shanu bisa titi.

Cikin fada take maganar da fulatanci, hakan yasa ban gane me take cewa ba.

Nan naga kamar suna bata hakuri, ta juyo ta dawo inda nike tace; "kai wallahi mutanan mu sai a hankali, yanzu barcin da baki yi nisa ba, ai da sun mana barna". injina kai nayi nace;

"Allah mungode maka da sun fasa kwaryar Inna".


           *************

Ganin yarinyar ta juya a guje yasa Yarima yin azama, domin chimmata.

Hannu Kursim tasa ta rike mashi hannu, tana cewa; "please Anuwar kada kaje, kaga ga Shanu kuma yaran chan kauyawa ne, zasu iya guduwa idan suka ganka, ko ni sai da nayi masu dubara, tsoron mutane suke tsabar kauyancin su".

Kamar ma baiji me ta ce ba turo ta yayi baya ta fadi kanta ya bugi mota.

Yayi gaba cikin sauri ita kuma ta buga wata irin kara.

Saurin dafe kanta tayi tare da mikewa cikin azama.

A guje taje tasha gaban shi.

Tace; "yanzu idan ka tsorata su, baza su kara zuwa ba kuma furar nan ita kadai kake sha, gashi yarannan nan suke samun ciniki, idan kayi haka baka kyautaba, hakan ba soyayya bace, ka kyale su mana idan har kana son Manal, ko dan kalar tafiyar MANAL da mai furar chan tayi".


Kallon ta yake kamar mai son ganin wani abu a fuskar ta.

Daga kai tayi alamar tabbatarwa, ga hawaye na bin fuskarta.


Hannun shi taja suka koma inda suke zaune.

Tace; "Bari naje na siyo maka furar yau da wuri zamu koma, gobe akwai wani nadi da za'ayi, bazamu zoba zan amso mai yawa sai a saka a Freezer dake dakin ka".


Rike kai yayi idanun shi jawur kamar garwashi.

 Kai kawai yake rikewa tare da girgiza shi, ganin yanayinsa yasa tayi gaba.


Fura mai yawa ta siya ta dawo, ko da ta dawo yana cikin mota.

Kulle motar tayi tare da zagayawa ta shiga.

Ta kalle shi fuskar nan tashi ta chanza, yatsun hannun shi yake kallo.

Tada motar tayi, ttayi hanyar fita, kanshi na kasa har suka isa gida.

Da karfi ya bude motar ya fita ko kamin ta fito har ya bace.


Bata tsaya kulle motar ba, tabi bayan shi cikin sauri.


Har gudu take tana isa dai-dai lokacin da ya banko kofar dakin shi.

Cikin azama tayi sauri kaiwa.

Banko ta yayi ta dawo, jarab ta fadi kasa.

Ya rufe dakin, cikin sauri ya isa bedroom tare da fadawa bisa gado.

Rike kai yake tare da buga kai.

Me yasa kowace mace take mashi kama da Manal??

Cikin filo ya saka kanshi dan bai son ganin gudun da yarinyar nan keyi.


Hanan ce take tabi da hannayen ta, tare da iso wa inda Kursim take kwance.


Kallon ta tayi ranta a bace tace, "yanzu princess din mu ce a kasa, because of Dah' namiji!". Tsaki tayi ta kama ta suka tashi.

Kursim kasa magana tayi dan ko kadan bata ganin laifin Anuwar.

A wannan lokacin ita tausayi ma yake bata.

Shiru tayi tana tunanin yanzu menene mafita??



          **************
Yau zuciyar ta fal da farin ciki.

Haka sukai ta ciniki har yamma furar ta bata kare ba saboda tafi ta kullum yawa.


Hakanan suka tashi yau babu tsoron Inna ko kadan a zuciyar ta Shatu ce ma keta cewa Allah yasa kada Inna ta bigeta.

Ni kam zuciya ta Yau tamkar wata wadda akayi ma wani Albishir.

Haka suka kwashi kayan su suka nufi. magama bakin hanyar kauyan su.

Nan suka iske jabe na jira Shatu ta siyo mana awara.


Muka hau mukaci muka sha ruwan cikin kokon ruwan jabe.

Muna isa Shatu tace, "nifa gobe ban zuwa". Zaro ido nayi, tare da cewa; "innalillahi! toni yanzu ya zanyi ni kadai". Dariya tayi tace;

"Zan turo jabe ya kai ki hadda nono ki saida man, Baffa ne ya hana ni zuwa tallah, 
wai tunda sati mai zuwa ne". Shiru nayi duk farin cikin da nake ciki sai naji ya gushe.

Jiki na babu kwari nace; "to Allah ya kaimu". Haka mukayi bankwana mu duka kowanne kamar yayi kuka.

Jiki ba kwari nayi sallama.

Na iske makwabtan mu tsaye cikin gidan mu,kusan gidan cike, sai maida magana ake.


Ga hawaye shabe-Sabe a fuskar Inna.  


Amman abun mamaki Ma'u tsaye take,idon ta babu ko alamar kuka.


Tace tomi ke faruwa??

  
          ************

Kursim tace"miya kaiki wajen part din Anuwar??"

Hanan tace"to sarkin 'yan kishi yanzu na shigo na amso sako,ina fitowa mota na hange ki,ko rufe mota bakiyi ba kinyi ciki a guje,ni na'aza ma wani abu ya faru shine na biyo ki"girgiza kai tayi.


Kursim ta kalle ta tace"Maganin minene mugani??"cikin sauri Hanan ta dauke hand bag dinta.

Tace ba ruwan ki,kada ki tabaman jaka.

 Kursim ta kalle ta cikin mamaki!!

To wane magani ne wannan Hanan take buyewa??


Anuwar ya kasa samun natsuwa jin shi,yake tamkar yau abin ya faru.


Ganin halin da yake yasa Sani buga kofa ya bude,amman ya kusa awa babu ko motsin Anuwar.


Daya bude ido fuskar MANAL ke dawo mashi.


Idan ya runtse ido  karamar yarinyar nan mai fura yake gani,tana nufo su a guje.



Bai ga fuskar taba, amman ya kasa natsuwa ganin abin yayi tamkar yau ya faru.


Shiru yayi tare da rike kanshi yana tunanin anya zai kara koma wajen nan??


Meyasa suke tsoron muta ne, Yana bukatar shawarar Sani.



Yana so ya koma kallar mutanan garin su ko dan ya samu kusan chi da ita.


Mikewa yayi cikin sauri yazo bakin kofa.

Yana budewa yaga duk fadawan shi a tsugunne bisa gwuiwowowin su, a bakin kofa.

Kowanne fuskar shi tamkar an mashi mutuwa.


Suna ganin shi suka yi saurin mikewa tsaye.

 
Suka ce tare da dukar da kansu kasa; "tuba muke idan mun bata ma Yarima rai,yayi mana duk hukun cin da ya dace damu"tare da kai gwuiwoyin su kasa.


Sani ya kalla,ya taso cikin sauri ya iso inda yake tsaye.


Da kyat maganar ta fita,ya nuni da su tashi,yace Sani ya biyo shi.



Suka tashi tare da godiya.


A falo ya zauna yana tunanin taya zai fara mashi bayani.


Me zai ce mashi ne?

 
Yace; "ina son a samo min kalllar kayan da kauyan garin nan ke sakawa".


Cikin sauri Sani ya kalle shi!!.



Qurrah

FALALAR ZIKIRI
       Allah Madaukakin Sarki ya ce:
فَاذْكُرُونِي أَذْكُرْكُمْ وَاشْكُرُواْ لِي وَلاَ تَكْفُرُونِ 
Fazkuriniy Azkurkum Washkuruliy Wala Takfuruni.
Ku ambace ni zan ambace ku, ku gode mini kada ku butulce mini" [Bakara, aya ta 152].

يَا أَيُّهَا الَّذِينَ آمَنُوا اذْكُرُوا اللَّهَ ذِكْراً كَثِيراً
Ya'ayyuhallazina Amanuzkurullaha Zikran Kathiran.

Ya ku wadanda suka yi imani ku ambaci Allah ambato mai yawa". (Ahzab aya ta 41).
وَالذَّاكِرِينَ اللَّهَ كَثِيراً وَالذَّاكِرَاتِ أَعَدَّ اللَّهُ لَهُم مَّغْفِرَةً وَأَجْراً عَظِيماً
Wazzakirinallaha Kathiyran Wazzakirati A'addanlahu Lahum magfiratan Wa'ajran Aziyman.
Da masu ambaton Allah da yawa maza, da masu ambatonsa mata, Allah ya tanadar musu gafara da lada mai girma". [Ahzab, aya ta 35]
وَاذْكُر رَّبَّكَ فِي نَفْسِكَ تَضَرُّعاً وَخِيفَةً وَدُونَ الْجَهْرِ مِنَ الْقَوْلِ بِالْغُدُوِّ وَالآصَالِ وَلاَ تَكُن مِّنَ الْغَافِلِينَ
Wazkurrabbaka Fiy Nafsika Tadarru'an Wakhiyfatan waduwanaljahri Minal kawli Bil'guduwi Wal'asali Walatakun Minalgafiliyna.
Kuma ka ambaci Ubangijinka a cikin zuciyarka, kana mai kankan da kai da tsoro, ba da daukaka murya ba (saka-tsaki) tsakanin asirtawa, da bayyanawa, da safe da marece, kuma kada ka zamo daga cikin gafalallu". [A'araf, aya ta 205].
	Kuma Manzon Allah, tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata a gare shi, ya ce; "Misalin wanda yake ambaton Ubangijinsa da wanda ba ya ambaton Ubangijinsa, kamar misalin rayayye da matacce ne". (Duba Sahihul Bukhari tare da Sharhinsa Fat'hul Bari (11/208). Muslim ya rawaito shi da lafazin; 'Misalin gida da ake ambaton Allah a cikinsa, da wanda ba a ambaton Allah a cikinsa kamar misalin rayayye ne da matacce". (1/539).)
	Kuma ya ce: "Shin ba na ba ku labarin mafi alherin ayyukanku ba, kuma mafi tsarkinsu a wajen Srkin da yake mallakar ku, kuma mafi daukakarsu ga darajojinku, kuma mafi alheri gare ku daga ciyar da zinariya da azurfa kuma mafi alheri gare ku da ku hadu da abokan gabanku ku rika dukan wuyayinsu, suna dukan wuyayinku? Suka ce ka ba mu labari. Ya ce: Ambaton Allah Madaukaki". (Tirmizi (5/459), da Ibn Majah (2/1245). Duba Sahih Ibn Majah (2/316). Da Sahihul Tirmizi (3/139).)
	Kuma ya ce: "Allah madaukaki yana cewa: Ni ina tare da zaton bawana da ni, kuma ina tare da shi idan ya ambace ni idan ya ambace ni. Idan ya ambace ni a cikin ransa zan ambace shi a cikin raina, idan ya ambace ni a cikin jama'a zan ambace shi a cikin jama'ar da ta fi su alheri, in ya kusance ni taki daya zan kuance shi tsawon gaba guda, in ya zo min ya na tafiya zan zo masa ina gaggawa".( Bukhari (8/171), da Muslim (4/2061). Lafazin ruwayar na Bukharine.)
	Daga Abdullahi Ibn Busr, Allah ya yarda da shi, ya ce; Ya Ma'aikin Allah! Shari'o'in Musulunci sun yi yawa a gare ni, saboda haka ka nuna wani abu da zan yi riko da shi. Ya ce: "Ku harshenka ya gushe face yana danye daga ambaton Allah". (Tirmizi (5/458). Da Ibn Majah (2/1246). Duba Sahihul Tirmizi (/139), da Sahih Ibn Majah (2/317).)
	Kuma mai tsira da amincin Allah yace; "Wanda ya karanta harafi daya daga littafin Allah yana da (ladan) kyakkyawan aiki daya saboda shi, kuma dukkan kyakkyawan aiki daya yana da (ladan) misalinsa goma. Ba ina cew Alif Lam Mim harafi ba ne. A'a, Alif harafi ne, Lam harafi ne, Mim harafi ne".( Tirmizi (5/175) Duba Sahihul Tirmizi (3/9), da Sahihul Jami'us Sgir (5/340).)
	Kuma daga Ukbata ibn Amir, Allah ya yarda da shi, ya ce; "Manzon Allah tsira da aminci su tabbata a gareshi, ya fito alhali muna cikin rumfa, sai yace; "Wane ne daga cikinku zai so ya fi kullum da safe zuwa koramar Budhan ko Akik, ya dawo da taguwa biyu masu manyan tozuna, ba tare da yin wani laifi ba ko yanke zumunta?" Muka ce: muna son haka. Ya ce; "Dayanku ya je masallaci ya nemi sani, ko ya karanta ayoyi biyu daga cikin littafin Allah Mabuwayi Mai Daukaka, ya fi masa taguwa biyu, ayoyi uku sun fi masa taguwa uku, ya fi masa taguwa biyu, ayoyi uku sun fi masa taguwa uku, ayoyi hudu sun fi masa taguwa hudu, da kwantankwacin adadinsu na rakuma". (Muslim (1/553))
	Kuma mai tsira da amincin Allah ya ce; "Wanda ya zauna a wani waje bai ambaci Allah a wajen ba, wannan zai zamanto masa abin bibiya a wajen Allah. Wanda ya kwanta a makwanci bai ambaci Allah a wajen ba, shi ma zai zamanto masa abin bibiya a wajen Allah. (Abu Dawud (4/264), da waninsa. Duba Sahihul Jami (5/342).)
	Kuma Mai tsira da amincin Allah ya ce: "Babu wasu mutanen da suka zauna a wani majalisi da ba su ambaci Allah a cikinsa ba, kuma ba su yi salati ga Annabinsu ba, face ya zamo musu abin bibiya, in Allah ya so ya yi musu azaba, in ya so ya gafarta musu".( Tirmizi, duba Sahihul Tirmizi (3/140).)
	Kuma Mai tsira da amincin Allah yace; "Babu wasu mutane da zu su tashi daga wani majalisi da ba su ambaci Allah a cikinsa ba, face sun zama kamar sun tashi daga kan mushen jaki ne, kuma zai zamanto musu abin da-na- sani".( Abu Dawud (4/264). Da Ahmad (2/389). Duba Sahihul Jami' (5/176).)




 _Bissimillah Rahmanir_ _Rahim_



----------------Ganin basu ji sallama ta ba yasa na kara wata sallamar, cikin daga murya.


Cikin sauri Inna ta kalle ni da jajayen idanun ta da suka rune saboda kuka.

Kaina tayo cikin zafin nama, kasa motsi nayi daga inda nake tsaye, sai ma kyarma da kafafu na suke.


Ganin zata iske ni yasa nayi saurin ajiye kwaryar kai na da nufin juya.


Ganin haka yasa ban son yadda akayi ba, sai dai naji Inna ta damko gashin kaina.


Wata irin kara na saki saboda yadda naji wani irin zafi tamkar ana diban naman jiki na.


Ta fara jibgata tana cewa; "Na fi son na kashe ki da naga wannan bakin cikin, duk sanadin ki ne hakan ya faru, tunda kika zo gidan nan farin ciki na ya kau, sannan yanzu kuma shine zaki saka diyata cikin wahalar rayuwa!".

Jibgata take tamkar kayan tsumma, su kuma mutanan gidan suna jin yadda nake ihu, amma anrasa wadda zata amshe ni.


Tun muryata na fita har tadena fita,kakari kawai nike,gashin kaina duk ya watse,sai damkar shi take.

Babu bakin kuka sai de hawaye.


Larai ce Aminiyar Inna tace; "Rakiya barta haka, ai itama ta wahala, kada ki kashe ta ki shiga bakin duniya, ni na san abinda zakiyi mata, kyale ta haka". Tazo ta janye Inna data danne min hannaye da kafafun ta, sai haki take dan ko ita ta gajiya.


Duhu-duhu nake gani, yunkuri nike na tashi amman na kasa tashi.

Gashin kaina cike da kasa, hakan yasa kan yayi min nauyi, ga jiki na ya saki, a haka na ja jiki na koma kusa da katanga, na jingina da katanga na zauna tare da maida numfashin da yake fita da kyar.



Ina kallon su Inna dake maida magana.


Larai na cewa; "tsabar kiyayyar da take ma Ma'u saboda Allah ya daukaka ta akan ta yasa ta bata kazar, babu wani Lado da ya bata kaza, ita taje wajen na Kan Tudu, dan shi kadai ne zai iya wannan aikin, kuma ai hanyar ta ce".


Inna dai banda hawaye babu abinda take.


Ma'u tsaki tayi ta bar wajen, suka bita da kallo dakin Inna ta nufa, ta shige duk suna kallon ta.


Saude tace; "Nifa abinda naga yafi kiyi amfani da shawarar mu ta farko, ki azabtar da ita, taje ko karuwanci zata yi ta samo kudin karya samun nan, dan kinsan Nakan tudu akwai son kudi, shi kudi kadai ne matsalar shi".


Inna taja majina tare da goge hawayen ta tace; "wallahi ko zan karar da abinda na mallaka sai na karya shi, tunda na samo mata Lado, take cewa a barta ta samu kalar mijin Ma'u ganin babu irin shi duk kauyan nan yasa tayin haka".

Su Indo makwabtan mu suka ce; "mun wuce Rakiya Allah ya saka ma Ma'u, komu munji bakin ciki, diyar ka tasamu miji kamar Kabiru, a maida ta da Lado, gaskiya babu dadi". Kasa magana Inna tayi sai dai tayi masu nuni da kai.


Larai tace; "Wai ita bacin ita da abunta, ina ita ina Kabiru, Ma'u ko kowa ya san asalin ta, kuma jikar masu kudi ce, kowa yasan Baba Rakiya da yawan gonaki, kowa yasan Baban Rakiya a kauyan su, kefa??". Ta nuna ni.

Tace; "kila ma talauci ya hana dangin uwar ki su rika ki, to dan haka me ya kaita son shi, shi ai ba sa'ar auren ta bane". 

Nan dai suka cigaba da goranta min.



Saude tace; "to yanzu ya zakuyi? Ba zaku je wajen gaisuwar ba yayan mijin kine, kuma kinga ya aiko yace kuje".


Larai tayi saurin tarar numfashin ta tace; "Allah ya jikan shi, daman dangin mijin ta ba son ta suke ba, ana cikin wannan hali da kudin motar da ya aiko mata zata tashi muje wajen na Kan tudu, kuma cikin kwana ukun zata famshi kudin ta, dan tallah tun asuba zata ringa fita".


Saude tace; "Wai daman dan naga kamar Liman nada tsauri, kuma naji tace Uwar su daya Uban su daya da marigayin".

Tsaki Larai tayi tace; "ai ba shi kadai bane ya rage mashi ba".

Shiru Saude tayi, Larai suka mike ita da Inna suka nufo hanyar waje.


Ganin haka yasa ni dai na tashi a hankali na fara bin bango ina jan kafata, tare da rike ciki na da Inna ta buga.


Na kwanta bisa gadon kara ta, hawaye na kara zubowa daga ido na.


          *************

 Cikin ladabi Sani ya tashi ya nufi abinda Anuwar yasa shi.


Tashi yayi ya koma bedroom.


 Hanan tace; "fada min best drink din Prince?".

Kursim dake gefen ta tana chart da wayar ta, ba tare da wani damuwa ba, saboda tasan Hanan tana kokari, a tunanin wani abu zata hada masu.


Tayi dariya tace; "wallahi best drink din shi idan na fada maki sai kinyi mamaki, niko shan shi ban iyawa, saboda babu dadi,shima zaman Saudia yasa ya Saba dashi, nan ta fadi mata".


Dariya Hanan tayi tace; "naji dadi da baki shan shi, nima ban sha saboda babu dadi, gobe akwai nadi, da angama na nasan za'a fara kide-kide kuma nasan mutunan ki baison hayaniya shi yasa zan yi maku order abinci daga hotel da kayan sha, sai kuje gidan ki na cikin estate yana da dadin zama".


Mikewa zaune Kursim tayi daga kwance da tayi tace; "kai amman fa kin kawo dabara, wallahi ina tsoron kada kuma ya koma wajen nan, abin har ya kai ga cewa tana mashi kalar tafiyar MANAL".


Dariya Hanan tayi tace; "sai ki kula gobe zuwa ten haka, sai nayi maku order din komi, zan ma aika a kara gyara gida, zan amshi makulli anjima".



Cikin jin dadi Kursim ta rungume Hanan tace; "ban san dami zan saka maki ba,to kema zan maki addu'a Allah ya mallaka maki Fahad".


Cikin jin dadi tace; "kai Amin my dear".


Nan suka ciga ba da firar su har dare ya tsaga, kana Kursim ta aika ma Prince kayan abinci.



Yana zaune parlo rike da remote yana juyawa, sai kuma ya koma Baby tv, yana kallo har ya kai ga yana murmushi.


Yana zaune parlon har dare yayi nisa, kana ya jawo wayar shi ya kira number Dady.

Shiru bai dauka ba tana ta ringing harta katse, ya kara kira sai da ya kira sau uku ba'a daga ba.


Dan karamin tsaki yayi yace; "shima Dad ya bar Sona kenan? Gashi ina ta kira yaki dagawa, kuma time din baccin shi bai yi ba, kowa ne kallar meeting yake idan na kira shi yana min message, amma yau ko message din babu".

Haka yayi ta magana shi kadai.




         *************

Da kyar nayi sallah na koma na kwanta, jin gidan shiru alamar ni kadai ce, sun fita kenan, gashi babu fitila ga wani irin duhu yau akeyi.


Nace gobe jummu'a yan makaranta da wuri suke fita, to ya zan yi gashi Shatu bata zuwa, da mun shiga cikin gari". Haka dai nayi ta tunani iri-iri.


A haka har bacci ya dauke ni, lallai ranar nayi baccin wahala.



Asubar fari naji an kwaraya man ruwan sanyi gashi akwai dan sanyin asuba cikin sauri nayi saurin tashi.


Inna ce kaina tace; "Bakar munafuka, sai ki tashi lokacin baccin ki ya kare ki fara daka, kuma harda zogala zaki fitar man".


Yanzu tsoron Inna nike kamar mi.


Cikin kyarmar jiki na mike tsaye Ina daukar dankwali na.



Nayi hanyar fita sai rakubewa nike, nazo fita ta turani na fadi, tace, "Bakar munafuka!".


Kamin ta fito nayi saurin mikewa tsaye nayi hanyar bandaki.


Tace; "uban mi zaki binne cikin bandaki? Ba'ayi sallah ba". Na dawo jiki na na kyarma.


Wani uban gero na gani cikin kwarya tace; "duka za'a yi amfani dashi". Nayi saurin daga kai.


Haka na fara aiki, koda akayi kiran sallar asuba har na kusa gamawa.



Naje bandaki wajen alwala na cika katon bokiti, nasa omo na wanke kaina, na sake shi dan ya bushe, na dawo nayi sallah na ida aiki.


Koda rana ta fito na shirya fura.


Na dauki buhu, nayi gona na babu kowa har na dawo.


Na gyara na aza sanwar zogala.


Wanka naje nayi nasa kayana masu kyau saboda yau jummu'a, daman kala daya duk jummu'a nike sakawa, da daddare sai na wanke.


Na fesa turare, na bude kaina don inason ya ida shan iska.



Na fito daga daki na, ina fito Ma'u ta fito tana tsaye bakin kofar dakin su.


Tace min, "Nana zo na aike ki wajen Lado, kice mashi mu hadu bayan gari wajen gonar shi dan Allah ki Fadi mashi, kinji? Idan ban ganshi ba zan iya mutuwa".


Inna ashe tana jinta tace; "wallahi Ma'u sai dai ki mutu babu idan zaki, kuma wajen Na kan tudu zamu".

Ta kalle ni tace; "bace min da gani, munafuka". Naje na kama da kyar na aza.


Ina azawa naji kaina yayi kasa.

Nayi saurin fita gidan.

Ina zuwa na iske babu jabe na zauna zaman jiran shi.


Na dade zaune sai ga jabe,na hau muka tafi,yaban wani abu cikin leda yace gashi inji Shatu.  


Na amsa nayi godiya, yau har bakin baranda ya kaini, yace tace"kiyi hakuri ba'a matsi nonon ba jiya sai dai gobe" .

Nace; " babu komi ai na gode", ya juya ya tafi.



Nayi tagumi ina tunanin ta ya zan saida fura babu nono, gashi yau jummu'a.

 

        ***************

Tashi yayi da tunanin miyasa Dad bai dauka ba??


Tun safe yakejin gidan haya niyya,tsoki kawai yake.

Ya shirya yau a cikin kayan saurata,haka Sani ya shirya shi.


Bayan yayi break yana zaune falo.


Sai ga Kursim ta shigo cikin shiga ta alfarma, ita yau hadda Alkyabba.


Murmushi tayi tace; "Morning", bai ko kalle ta ba ya juyar da kai, saboda bai son ta kura, gashi gidan yau hayaniya, gashi Dad yaki daukar wayar shi.


A hankali yace; "Me yasa yau gidan suke takura min bana son hayaniya".


Cikin taushin murya tace; "Idan baka so zamu bar gidan, za'ayi nadin sarautar kanin mahaifi na, kuma kaine a madadin Abie, sai one za'a gama, ana gamawa zan daukeka mu bar gidan".


Ta mike tsaye tace; "ya kamata ka fito hakanan", ta juya taje kusa da Sani tayi masa magana ta fice.


A wajen taro Anuwar sai kara hada rai yake, yana dan guntun tsaki, a  ganin shi an takura mashi yana da wajen zuwa.



Haka ya zauna har karfe daya, cikin sauri ya nufi part din shi.


Wanka yayi yasa kananun kaya jin yadda gidan keta kide-kide yasa ya fito suka ci karo da Kursim.


Ta kalle shi tace; "muje", ba musu yabi bayan ta.



Suna fitowa gida ya cika anata bushe-bushe da kide-kide, sai kirari ake masu.


Suna isa ta bude mashi ya shiga ta tada mota, suka bar gidan.



A gidan da Hanan ta shirya masu ta nufa.


Yau bai ce komi ba, tana bude mashi yayi ciki.


Tana shiga parlo bai nan, ta leka bedroom ta ganshi kwance.


Murmushi tayi tace; "shi komi wahalar shi yake ji Wai nan har ya gaji". Parlo ta dawo tana kallo.


Tayi sallah taci abinci, ta zauna ta fara chart.


         **************


Yau kam babu chiniki, gashi babu nono kuma yau babu mutane sosai zogalan mane nayi dan ciniki.


Har yamma tayi nace; "nikam yau ya zanyi?".



         **************

Kursim jin shirun yayi yawa yasa ta dan fita ta mike kafa.



Anuwar na tashi yayi sallah ya fito parlo Babu kowa, hamma yayi tare da rufe baki da dan guntun tsaki.


Ganin kayan abinci yasa yayi murmushi yace; "Thank you kulsoom". Ya nufi dinning ya zauna ya zuba yaci ya koshi,ya kalli lemon da aka aje mashi, yayi murmushi.


Ya zuba ya fara sha, cup guda ya shanye, ya zuba wani ya sha yace; "I Missing You", tare da kiss din kwalin.


Ya koma parlo ya zauna, tv ya kunna,m yana nan zaune yaji yanayinshi lokaci guda ya chanza.

Lokaci guda yana yin shi ya sauya,sai wani yam yake ji a jikin shi.

Tun abun yana a hankali har ya fara matse kafafu.


Tsaki yayi, key din Kursim na mota dake bisa center table ya dauka.


Ya shiga motar ta ya tada, Mlmai gadi ya bude mashi get ya fita.


A hankali yake Driving din  saboda bai kware ba.


Tafiya kadan ta kai shi wajen zaman su, daman wajen babu wasu motoci, hakan yasa driving bai mashi wahala.


Yana zuwa masu aiki sun tashi har mai gadi.


Kashe motar yayi yana rikon cikin shi.


Tsaki yayi yace; "me na sha? Daman nazo nan dan na rage, Amman karuwa yake". Bude mota yayi ya dawo baya, tare da rike ciki, yadda yake ji tamkar ranshi zai fita, saboda yadda marar shi ke ciwo.


Zufa kawai ke kwararo mashi, yana ganin kiran Kursim amman ya kasa dauka.

Sai kara matse kafa yake, tare da rike ciki.


Wata irin sha'awa ce yake ji,l wadda tunda yake a rayuwar shi bai taba jin haka ba.

Gani yake idan bai kusanci mace ba zai iya mutuwa.


Yanzu anan ina wata mace??


Bai taba tunanin aika ta Zina ba amman yau bai iya jurewa.

          ************

Na rasa abokin magana, na dauki ledar da Shatu taban naga kwali nayi saurin budewa, ina budewa naga turare duk bakin cikin da nike ciki naji ya rage.


Na fara fesawa a jiki na ko ina, ina jin kaunar Shatu a raina.


Ina nan zaune shiru har duhu ya fara gashi ba jabe, sai turare da nike fesawa.


Ganin motar su ta wuce nace; "Alhamdulillah", tare da fesa turare na dan nasan matar shi nada son tsabta, mikewa nayi cikin murna.

Na nufi wajen inda motar take.


Zuciya ta cike da farin ciki na Isa wajen.




Qurrah
*_JARABTAR MU*_
