Addu'ar Sanya Tufafi:
الحَمْدُ ِللهِ الَّذِي كَسَانِي هَذَا الثَّوْب ورَزَقَنِيهِ مِنْ غَيْرِ حَوْلٍ مِنِّي وَلاَ قُوَّةٍ"
Alhamdu lillahil-lazee kasanee hatha aththawb warazakaneehi min ghayri hawlin minnee wala kuwwah.

Dukkan yabo ya tabbata ga Allah, wanda ya tufatar da ni wannan (tufa), kuma ya azurta ni da ita, ba tare da wata dabara daga gare ni ba ko wani karfi.




 _Bissimillah Rahmanir_ _Rahim_



------------Gurnani yake tamkar zaki tare da rike ciki yana fadin; "Ya Allah kada ka nufe ni da aikata zina, ya Allah ka taimakeni, bana da niyyar haka a zuciya ta, Allah ka debe min abinda nake ji".


Jin kamshi a bayan shi yayi saurin juyowa duk da duhu ya sauko, baka ganin fuska.


Daman Addu'a yake Allah ya kawo mashi mai taimakon shi, ya kira mishi Kursim ta kaishi asibiti, ganin mace kuma karamar yarinya kallon dirin ta kawai yayi ya gane mai furar nan ce 


Magana yake son yi amman ya kasa, saboda yadda marar shi ta rike.


Nuni yake mata da ta matso ta dauko mashi waya.


Ganin mutum duke cikin mota yasa nayi tsaye, na san bashi da lafiya, na waiga banga matar shi ba, na matsa domin taimaka mashi.



Cikin tsoro na matsa ina kallon abinda yake nuna min tana haske, gashi wayar na gaban mota, ban san ya ake bude kalar wannan motar  ba, to ya zanyi, gashi ina jin yadda yake gurnanin azaba.

Ban san shi ba amman duk na rude na rasa ya zanyi na taimaka mashi.


Zuciyata ta karaya sai hawaye, shi kuma kara nuna min yake.

Na matso kusa dashi yadda muke jin numfashin juna, na kai hannu na domin daukar wayar nakasa.

Na san matar shi bata son kazanta hakan yasa na cire takalmi na, na shiga cikin motar inda yake da nufin dauko wayar.

 
Yarima na kwance rike da ciki a bayan motar yaji shigowarta.


Hannu ta kai ta dauko wayar ta kasa, hannun ta bai kaiwa, matsawa tayi, ta dauko wayar, hannun ta kyarma yake saboda bata taba ganin kalar wannan abu ba.
 
Ta manta ba kalar motar garin su bace mikewa tayi kanta ya buge tare da fadowa baya.

Jib!! sai kan jikin yarima.

Lumshe ido yayi tare da kara sunsunar jikinta, lokaci guda ya kara rudewa.

Saurin tashi tayi daga jikin shi, mika mashi wayar  tayi itama ta kasa magana saboda faduwar gaban da take gashi jikin ta sai kyarma yake.


Yana son amsar wayar amman ya kasa, bai ko iya motsi, a hankali ya kulle ido.


Ganin idon shi a kulle yasa na kara jin wani tsoro, ko ya mutu ne??


Na aje abun hannu na dake haske, nasa hannu na a hankali cikin rigar shi, don insan yana numfashi ko ya mutu.


Hannun ta na kaiwa jikin Yarima su duka suka ji wani yarrrr...


Cikin sauri Anuwar ya rike hannun ta, saboda duk zufar da yake tana saka hannun ta cikin jikin shi yaji wani faduwa gaba da sanyi.

Ido na zaro ganin ya rike min hannu, ban ida tunanin da nike ba naga ya fara sunsunar hannu na.


Fara lalubar ta yayi, har a zuciyar shi yana jin tausayin ta, ya san karamar yarinya ce, amma yadda yake ji tamkar  zai mutu dan azaba.

Jin ta fara kuka yasa yayi saurin saka bakin shi cikin nata yana kokakin rabata da zanin dake jikin ta.


Hawaye yake sosai yana kara danne ta, saboda yadda take yunkurin tashi.


Sun kusa minti goma a haka kana Anuwar ya raba ta da budurcinta.


Sun kai minti ashirin yana abu daya kana ya sake ta, lokacin tayi laushi ko hannu bata iya dagawa.


Maida numfashi yake tare da runtse ido ganin abin yake tamkar a mafarki, ace yau shi ne ya kusanci macen da babu aure tsakanin su, kenan Zina yayi, runtse ido yayi tare da buga kan shi.

 
Kan shi wani irin ciwo yake mishi, hakan yasa ya rike kanshi.

Saurin sakin kanshi yayi tare da kai kallon shi ga yarinyar da yayi ma fade.

Cikin sauri yasa hannu ya dauko ta daga kasan da ta fadi.


Bisa kirjin shi ya azata yana shafa gashin kanta, yana jin yadda zuciyar ta ke bugawa da sauri da sauri.

Cikin zuciyarshi yace; "sorry baby ni ma ba hali na bane, ban san yadda aka yi hakan ya faru dani ba, ammaa zan maki komi kike so bazan bari ki wulakanta ba zanje naga iyayen ki, wannan ita ce Jarabtar mu".


Zoben hannun shi na azurfa wanda Abban shi ya bashi a matsayin shi na Yarima ranar da ya cika shekara sha takwas, shi ya cire daga dan yatsan shi, wanda daga shi sai karamin yatsa, ya saka mata a babban dan yatsanta.


Ya kara gyara mata kwanciya bisa kirjin shi, tare da lumshe ido sai bacci.


A hankali na bude idona naga kamar yayi bacci jin ya daina shafa jiki na, na kalli dan yatsa na duk da duhu ne, amman dutsen cikin zoben na haske.


Tashi nayi daga jikin shi, na dauki zani na da Hijab dita, na fito daga cikin motar babu kowa.


Ga duhu ga tsoro gashi da kyar nike tafiyar, ina isowa wajen barandar mu, naga mota tayo wajen mu.


Nayi saurin boye, sun wuce banko tsaya daukar kwaryata ba, nayi cikin daji ga duhu ga zafin da gabana keyi.


Tafiyata ta chanza, ji na nike tamkar wadda akayima rauni a wajen fitsari na, ina son yin tafiya dai-dai amman na kasa.


Zuciya ta ta bushe na kasa kuka, tafiya nike har na isa tudu.


Ina kallon yadda ake walkiya, amma babu tsoro ko kadan a fuskata, saboda yadda nake jin zuciya ta tamkar ta fito dan bakin ciki.


Me zan cema mijin da zan aura??

Yanzu kenan maganar Inna zata tabbata gaskiya, nike kai kaina birni karuwanci??


Waye wannan yayi min haka?.

Faduwa nayi dai-dai lokacin da ruwa ya sauko.


Sai yanzu baki na ya bude har zan iya yin kuka.

Cikin kuka da daga murya nace; "waye shi da zai yi min haka?? Na tsane ka Allah sai ya saka min".

Haka nayi ta surutai cikin daji, ga ruwa na duka na ga walkiya ana yi.


Kuka nike tamkar wata ma haukaciya ga ruwa na saukowa.

Yadda nake jin zuciya na zafi, ji nake ina ma Allah ya dauki raina a lokacin.

A cikin duniyar nan bani da kowa sai Baba, bansan  komi ba sai wahala, kullum cikin wahala nike da kaskanci.

Wannan wace irin jarabta ce?

Bansan dadin rayuwa ba sai wahala.

Ina tunanin a hutu a gidan miji na sai kuma akasin haka, wane ba miji zai aure ni ya yadda dani.

Kenan haka zan rayu cikin bakin ciki??


Mikewa nayi zan cigaba da tafiya, amma ina, jina nayi nayi kasa.


Bude ido nayi na ganni bisa gado, wanda ba nawa ba.

          ***********

Tun kamin Hanan ta tsaya Kursim ta bude mota ta fito a guje.

Wayar hannun ta ta kunna fitilar ta.

Ganin Anuwar kwance bayan mota tamkar bai motsi.

Tayi saurin shiga motar tana girgiza mashi kai tana fadin sunan sa.


Hannan ta iso tace; "baza tashi ba, har sai nan da awa daya, saboda nasa mashi hadda maganin bacci".

Kuka sosai Kursim keyi tare da rungume Anuwar.


Haska shi tayi da wayar hannun ta.

Innalillahi! 


Hanan tace; "lafiya??Me ya faru?", tare da matsowa kusa.


Kursim tace cikin kuka; "jini kuma babu rauni a jikin shi, ga kayan shi duk sun yamutse".


Hanan tace; "wacece Prince ya fitar ma da budurci?". Cikin sauri Kursim ta kalle ta.


Kursim tace; "me yasa baki yi shawara dani ba, da zaki aikata haka, yanzu me za mu cema su Abba?".


Hanan tace; "na fada maki komai, kuma kinsan bawai nayi domin cutarwa ba, wallahi nayi hakan domin Prince yayi nadamar abinda yayi maki, saboda ina jin takaicin abin".

Kursim tace; "to yanzu me zamu ce mashi idan ya tashi, gashi dole mu fadi mashi gaskiya akan Dad din shi".

Hanan tace; "kina nufin bai san komi ba? Nayi tunanin ya sani shi yasa kika ji nace mu fara zuwa airport neman shi". Girgixa kai Kursim tayi.

Tace; "mu tafi gida nan cikin daji ne, kada ya farka".


Rufe motar tayi ta koma gaba, Hanan kuma ta koma cikin motar da suka zo.


Haka suka bar wajen Hanan na gaba Kursim na baya, har suka isa.

Suna zuwa Kursim ta Kira Sani ya kama mata suka shigar da Anuwar dakin shi, ta kwantar dashi tare da cire mashi takalmi.


A tunanin Sani Anuwar ya san halin da Dad din shi yake, shi ne dalilin da yasa ya zama haka, kila giya ya sha shi ne abinda Sani ke tunani.


Kursim tace mashi ya fita bata son kowa a part din.


Haka ta zauna gefen Anuwar yana bacci har kusan awa guda.


A hankali ya bude idon shi, ganin shi a dakin shi yasa yayi saurin tashi.


Rike kan shi yake, ya kalli Kursim ya kalli dakin yace; "Kulsoom ina Baby take?". Kursim kallon shi take, ganin ya yunkura zai tashi.

Tace; "dare yayi yanzu, kuma wacece Baby?".


Tsaki yayi tare da sauka bisa gadon, kallon kayan jikin shi yake.


Bakin mirror yaje ya tsaya yana kallon kan shi.

Cije leben shi yayi, hakan yasa har yayi jini, tare da wata kuwa, yake cewa; "No!No!!".

Kayan bisa mirror ya zubar tare da saka hannu ya naushi mirror, mirror ya tarwatse, shi kuma hannun shi jini kawai ke fita.


Da kyar Kursim tayi motsi daga inda take duk ta tsorata.


Juyowa yayi yana kallon inda ta daskare yace; "Ina key din motar ki". Cikin tsoro da kyarmar murya tace;


"Prince baka iya mota ba sosai, shi yasa hankali na ya tashi dana zo na iske bakanan babu mota, nayi tunanin airport kayi dan chan nayi, na san kana iya cewa yau zaka je Abuja sai dai na kai ka, amma bazan baka mota ba ina tsoro".

kallon ta yake cikin mamaki.


Me zai je yi Abuja?? Bai gane maganar ta ba.

Yace; "Babu Abujan da zani, kizo ki kaini garin su yarinyar nan, idan bazaki ba ki ara min motar ki, idan kina gudun na bata maki, ita zan lunka maki kudin motar, kiban ita, yanzu zan maki transfer, bana bukatar wata magana".


Wayar shi ya gani bisa bed site yaje ya dauko, bai yi mamakin ganin Miss calls ba.


Message din Nifra yayi mamakin gani yana buda wayar ya shigo.


Tsaki yayi yace kila ma shirmenta ne, sai kuma yayi wani tunani yace bari ya duba.



Ganin message din yasa yayi wurgi da wayar shi yana kallon Kursim, hankali shi a tashe yace; "daman kin san halin da Dad yake kika ki fada min!".

Girgiza kai take tana kuka.

Tace; "wallahi ban sani ba, sai dazun bayan na fita daga gidan nan, na nufin zagayawa cikin unguwa, nan ne Nifra ta kira tace na tura ayi maka booking din jirgi, gobe kaje gida Dad bashi da lafiya kuma sosai, shi ne na kira Hanan ta kaini nayi booking din jirgi, muna dawowa muka iske baka nan, nan hankali na ya tashi nayi tunanin ka sani airport zaka, shi ne muka koma airport nemanka".


Bakin bed din shi ya dawo ya zauna tare da rike kai.

Yanzu ina zashi wajen yarinyar da yayi ma barna??

Ko Kuma wajen Dad da bai san inda yake ba, rai hannun Allah.


Gashi sunan shi Dad ke fadi, shi yake son gani.


Runtse ido yayi yana tunanin aikin da yayi dazun na yima karamar yarinya fade.

Sai kuma ya dunga hango kalar soyayyar da Dady ke nuna mashi, ko wanda ya haife shi bai mishi ita ba.


Yanzu gobe da safe ina zai nufa kenan??



Qurrah
*_JARABTAR MU*_

*~So magical,Our creative golden pen✍,behold our words.~*

*~A product of our pen🖊,savour our words, for it will cause you no pain.~*

DEDICATED TO....MY SISTERS😘


Page 29-30



 _Bissimillah Rahmanir_ _Rahim_



-----------------A hankali nake bin dakin da kallo,lalle nan ba dakina bane, kuma ba gidan mu bane.

Jin an rike man hannu yasa nayi saurin kai kallo na wajen.


Shatu ce rike da hannu na kanta na bisa gadon da nike kwanec, sauran jikin ta na kasa hannunta cikin nawa,tana bacci ga hawaye nan a fuskar ta duk sun bushe.


Runtse ido nayi na tuna abin da ya faru da dani.

Ban san lokacin dana fizge hannu na cikin nata ba.


Kallon zoben da ya saka man nike,lalle ba mafar ki bane.


Wani irin kuka ne mai cin rai da sauti ya kubu ceman.


Cikin sauri Shatu ta bude ido,ta ganni zaune bisa gado ina kuka.


Kallo na take hawaye na zuban mata,itama daman da alama tasha kuka.


IYA ce ta shigo dakin(mahaifiyar su Shatu)tace;

"Subhanallah! me nike gani haka Shatu?keda ya dace ki bata baki kuma shine kema kuka kike,tun dazun nace kibar wannan kukan haka"


Cikin sauri Shatu ta goge hawayen ta tace;yanzu Iyah miza muyi mata,bata iya tafiya fa dai-dai,kada Inna ta sani,kin san idan ta sani zata fadama mai garin su,ba zasu yadda da ita ba haka zasu ce tana sane,dan haka sai an mata bulala".

Cikin kuka Shatu ke cewa;"Inna dan Allah kiyi wani abu,kada Inna ta sani,kin san bata son ta, kuma daman yanzu ta tsane ta,tabi ko Ina a gari tana cewa wai ta yi ma Ma'u asiri dan kada ta auri Kabiru,kin ga ta samu dama kenan,shi yasa kika ga ina kuka"

Shiru Iyar su Shatu tayi,lalle maganar Shatu gaskiya ne, tomi zata yi kenan?


Zuwa Iya tayi inda nike zaune ta kamani,tace;"muje ga ruwan zafi chan ki shiga,daga nan kiyi tsarki"daga kai nayi.


Sai da ta kama ni na tashi,kana ta kaini bandaki cikin daren nan,haka na shiga ruwan zafi.

Har lumshe ido nike saboda yadda naji dadin su,na danji sauki,ba kamar dazun ba,nayi wankan tsarki na fito.


Ta kamani muka nufi daki, taban kayan Shatu na saka na fara rama sallar da ake bina.


Ina gamawa naga ba kwaryar Inna da ta nono.

Nasan na baro su baran dar mu.

Shatu na kallah ina neman karin bayani.

Tace;"Jabe ya dawo yace bai gan kiba,amman kayan ki na nan,shine Iya tace karya yake muyi sauri muje mu dauko ki baki iya daukar kayan nan ke kade ga hadari,koda muka je babu kowa mukai ta kiran sunan ki, baki nan shine, nace mu dauki kayan mu duba hanya,shine muka iske ki kin suma"


Ganin hawayen Shatu sun ki tsaya wa, yasa na tsaida nawa,Iya ta kawo man tuwo,naci nasha ruwa na zauna, banajin bacci ko kadan saboda dana runtse ido sai intajin tsoro.


           *************

Jinin dake zuba daga hannun shi, yasa Kursim mikewa,zata kama hannun ya fizge hannun shi.


Tare da cewa;"No need" cikin tsawa ganin jinin yaƙi tsayawa gashi bai son taimako nata, tayi saurin barin dakin cikin sauri.


Su Sani ta iske bakin kofar dakin sun yi zugu dum su duka,ko wanne yayi ta gumi.


Sani tace ya biyo ta.

Saida suka dan yi nisa kana ta tsaya ta kalle shi tace; "ina son kaje yanzu ka gyara part din Yarima,sannan inason ka shirya mashi kayan shi,gobe karfe takwas zaku tashi".


Kai ya ɗaga tare da cewa;"Angama Gimbiya Kursim"ya juya cikin sauri ita kuma ta koma daki.


Ta iske Hanan tayi zugun da hannu,tana ganin ta tayi saurin mikewa ta iso inda take 


Tace;"bai tambayi abinda ya kawo mashi wannan matsanan ciyar sha'awa ba?"yadda taga Hanan duk ta rude yasa tayi mamaki.

Daman Hanan na tsoron wani?

Kursim tace "bashi ne a gaban shiba yanzu,yasan halin da Dad din shi yake ciki"

A jiyar zuciya Hanan tayi tare da cewa "thank God".

Kursim tace;"daman duk jarumtar taki kina da tsoro haka?"


Murmushi Hanan tayi tace;"kinfi kowa sanin waye Prince,a kasan su muke,ina tsoron kada wani abu yasamu kujerar Baba na next tenuwa"dafa ka fadar ta Kursim tayi tace;

"Kada ki damu babu abinda zai faru insha Allah,ai yanzu hankalin shi na wajen Dad din nan"


Murmushi Hanan tayi tace"bari na shirya maki kayan ki,dake na shirya tafiyar"zaro ido Kursim tayi.


Hanan tace;"ya kamata ki kasan ce tare dashi, ina tare da ke a koda yaushe, ki nuna kema kin damu a kan ciwon Dad din shi,ance yana Spain ko?"

Daga kai tayi alamar eh.


Murmushi Hanan tayi tace;"yarinya ki farka daga mafarkin da kike,kamar ba mace ba,komi sai an saka kissa a ciki,kina abu kamar ba Lowyer ba"

Dariya Kursim tayi tace;"wallahi duk rudewa nike yi, kuma kinsan idan Dad din nan yace ya aure ni,shikenan matsalar mu ta kare, dan zaiyi ko kaɗan baison bata ma Dad din nan"


Hanan tace;"lalle kiyi amfani da wannan damar wajen sanya soyayyar ki a zuciyar Dad, kiyi amfani da damar ki, zan zo ko daga baya ne".


Haka suka cigaba da shirya kayan Kursim,duk abinda tasan zata bukata ta dauka,har saida dare yayi nisa kana suka kwanta.


Tun asuba suka tashi suka shirya Kursim ta nufi wajen iyayen ta.


Ko kaɗan baiyi bacci ba yana yin sallah ya shirya ya dawo falo.


Har wajen Baban Kursim suka je sukayi mashi ban kwana shidai Prince ba magana.


Kowa yayi mashi uzuri akan rashin lafiyar Dad,da ka ganshi kasan baiyi bacci ba.


Haka suka bar gida,shida Kursim a mota guda.


Suna zuwa airport ba bata lokaci suka bar garin Katsina.


Zuciyar Anuwar da tunani iri-iri.



          **************

Bayan munyi sallar asuba,ni dai a zaune nike inbanda hawaye babu abinda nike.

 A haka har rana ta fito ina nan zaune.

Saiga Shatu ta shigo da zazzafen tuwo.

Tace;"sabko kici,ga ruwa chan an dafa maki na zafi idan kin gama kije kiyi wanka"kai na daga.


Naje na wanke baki na na dawo,na zauna tare da rintse ido,koya nayi motsi sai naji zafi,daure wa kawai nike dan babu yadda zanyi.


Tuwon nike chi amman jin shi nike tamkar ina cin madachi.

Jin yadda nike jin tuwon yasa na cire hannu na.

Naje na wanke na dawo na kwanta.

Ita ma Shatu tashi tayi ta kai tuwon ta wanko hannun ta ta dawo.


Kusa dani ta zauna, inda nike kwance.


Tace;"kiyi hakuri ki dauka hakan itace Jarabtar ki,kowa fa da irin jarabar da Allah ke mashi,ke kuma haka taki take, insha Allah watarana zai zama labari,amman dan Allah ki saki jikin ki,ki tashi kije ki gasa jikin"


Tashi nayi ina goge hawayen da suka zubo man.


Haka naje nayi wanka na nadawo,na shafa mai na saka kayan Shatu.


Ranar anan na yini ba tare da na koma gida ba,haka suma basu ceman naje gida ba.

Duk abinda Shatu tasan zai faranta man ranar tayi man ita da Iyarta amman da yake zuciya ta babu dadi,sai dai naita binta da kallo.


Ban sanin lokacin da hawaye ke zubo min ba,nan Iya tai ta man nasiha,tare da kwantar man da hankali.



             *************

Suna isa tace suka bar kasar sai Spain inda aka kwantar da Dad.


Koda suka isa Anuwar kamar yayi hauka, duk ya rude,gashi ba'a bari a shiga wajen Dad din.


Ranar haka ya yini baici komi ba,haka ita ma Kursim dake gefen shi, komai bata ci ba 

Saida Daddare kana Momyn Nifra tace,su wuce su tafi,sai zuwa gobe za'abari a ganshi.


Anuwar yace nan zai kwana, Kursim ma tace nan zata kwana.


Momy tace a'a su dai wuce gida ita mace ce, haka ta hakura ta bar Anuwar suka wuce gidan su Nifra dake nan.


Tana zuwa tayi wanka taci abinci,tana zauna ta kira wayar Anuwar bai dauka ba.

Daga karshe ma kashe wayar yayi.

Zaunawa tayi tare da tagumi,tana tunanin anya Anuwar zai sota??


Tsaye yake a cikin parlo yana kallon Dadyn shi da ke kwance yana barci.


Dawowa yayi ya zauna cikin daya daga cikin kujerun parlo,idan kaga wurin baka cewa asibiti ne,parlo ne wanda aka shirya kamar ba asibiti ba ya tsaru sosai.


Daga parlon kana kallon wanda ke jinya ta gilas,amman an kulle,babu mai shiga sai Doctors ko kuma sai idan su sukayi maka izini.


Lumshe ido yayi yana tuna abinda ya faru jiya.


Yana bukatar ya koma Nigeria kodan yaje wajen yarinyar, ya sanya mata laifi Amman babu yadda zaiyi kaddara ce.


Yana bukatar yarinyar zai ne mota ,itace mace ta biyu da yaji wani abu akan ta,bayan Manal sai ita.

Zata iya maye gurbin Manal??


A haka har dare yayi nisa,yana zaune.

Chan cikin dare ya mike domin ya ga Dad koya farka.


Yana zuwa ya ganshi idon shi bude,yana kallon wajen parlon shi.


Cikin sauri yayi kofar shi,ya ganta rufe.

Cikin tsawa yake kiran Security.


Jin tsawa yasa duk suka rude,cikin kyarmar jiki suka iso wajen.


Kofa ya nuna masu,cikin sauri suka bude.


Yana zuwa rungume Dad din shi yayi yana murmushi tare da sakin ajiyar zuciya.

Ya ɗago yana kallon Dad din nashi na da yake hawayen farin ciki.


A hankali yace;"I missing you so much my Son"tare da manna mashi kiss a 
goshi, yana shafa sumar kanshi.

Murmushi kawai Anuwar yake yana son Dad dinsa duk abinda zaima Abie shima zai iya yima shi.


Koda ku Momy suka zo asibiti da safe.

Zaune suka iske Dad da Anuwar bisa gado, suna fira su duka sunyi wanka sun ci abinci kamar ba marar lafiya.


Matsowa Kursim tayi ta gaida Dad, ya amsa cikin sakin fuska da jin dadi, Anuwar kuma ko kallon ta baiyi ba saima latsar waya da yake.


Ta gaida da shi, kai kawai ya daga mata.


Haka a bangaren Momy sai jan Kursim take a jiki, Nifra ce ma dai da bata shiga lamuran ta ba,dan Nifra jinta take kamar itace President din, daman Fawad na makaranta banda shi aka zo, baiko san Dad bai lafiya ba.


Fira suke sosai amman Anuwar bai saka masu baki.


Tashi yayi zai bar wajen Dad ya rike shi, kallon Dad yayi da alamar tambaya??


Da ido Dad ya nuna mashi waje ya zauna.

Koma wa yayi ya zauna, amman jin shi yake tamkar bisa ƙaya yake zaune , yana son ya kebe yayi tunani miya kamata yayi akan yarinyar nan.



           *************

Yau koda na tashi na kasa, jiki na naji sauki sosai, haka nayi ta zama duk da koda yau she muna tare da Shatu.


Shatu na kokari wajen gusar man da damuwa.


Haka itama Iyarta duk abinda suka san ina so saida aka aika Jabe ya siyo man.


Muna zaune nida Shatu,tana ban labarin yadda suke bikin su, hakan yasa na saki jiki amman kasan zuciya ta na man zafi, saide wani labarin dole ka dara.


Jabe ya shigo yace;"Adda wai kinji anata cigiyar 'yar gidan Malam, wai ta bace"mamaki nike daman Inna zata iya nema na??


A haka har dare yayi na kwanta, sai bacci saboda jiya sama-sama nayi bacci.



            **************

Juya wayar dake hannun shi yake, kowa yana sha'anin shi a parlon, da Momy da Kursim da Nifra, Dady na zaune suna ta bashi labari.


Kadan-kadan yayi tsaki tare da runtse ido.


Daddy na kallon duk abinda yake.


Ta shi yayi ya koma dakin shi ya rufoshi da karfi.


Bakin bed din shi ya zauna yana buga kan shi.


A hankali Daddy ya bude kofar yayi tsaye yana kallon Anuwar.


Matsowa yayi ya zauna kusa dashi.


Ya rike hannun shi guda, kallon shi Anuwar yayi.


Dady yace;"nasan kana cikin matsala minene fada man"



A hankali yace;"ina son zuwa Katsina gobe"kallon shi Dady ya ke cikin mamaki.


Yace;"miza kayi ne a Katsina bayan gamu nan tare da Kursim"gir giza kai yake yace;

"Please Dad kabar ni na tafi mana "yayi saurin rike hannayen Dad.

Kallon shi Dad yake yana nazarin wani Abu.

Miyasa yake son zuwa katsina??


Kuma daga ance bai zuwa duk yabi ya rude lalle akwai dalili.


Daddy yace;" sai ka fada man gaskiya me zaka je  kayi?"


Shiru yayi ya kasa magana.


Dady yace idan ba zaka fadi ba shikenan babu inda zaka Ina bukatar ka kusa dani.


Shiru yayi yana tunanin kode ya fadi ma Daddy abin da ke faruwa.

Bazai iya daure wa ba idan baiga yarinyar nan ba.


To yanzu menene mafita??


Qurrah

DEDICATED TO....MY SISTERS😘


Page-31-32

 _Bissimillahir Rahmanir_ _Rahim_ 



-----------------Shirun da yayi yasa Daddy yace;"Anu dina idan ba ka fada man damuwar ka ba zan iya zargin wani abu, sai inyi zargin kamar ban kai matsayin da zaka fada man damuwar kaba"

Cikin sauri Anuwar yayi saurin rike hannayen Dad din shi, tare da gir giza kai.


Murmushi Dad yayi tare da kara rike hannun shi yace;"Anu dina fada man ina jin ka, kada kaji komai zan fahimce ka"


Shiru yayi sannan ya juyar da fuskar shi, daga kallon Dad yace"wata karamar yarinya nayi ma fade..."sai yayi shiru.


Shiru Dad yayi yana kallon shi, yarasa me zaice, bai taba tunanin haka daga Yarima ba.

Yasan Yarima yana da tausayi a tunanin shi taimako zai maida shi Katsina.


A jiyar zuciya Daddy yayi kana yace;"fada man ya akayi hakan ya faru??dan nasan halin ka ba zaka iya aikata wani abu marar kyau ba saida dalili"


Shiru Anuwar yayi, murza hannun shi Dad yayi.

Kana yace"wallahi Daddy I don't know how to explain, kawai na tsinci kaina cikin wata irin sha'awa...."sai kuma ya kara juyar da fuskar shi, tare da yin shiru.


Gyaran murya Daddy yace"okay, no need to explain, my Son I understand".

Shiru ya biyo baya kana Daddy yace"ya sunan ta? kuma a wane unguwa take a Katsina??
Ya kuma kaman ninta suke??"


Shiru yayi tare da dukar da kan shi kasa;"ban san komai game da ita ba, saide nasan tana saida fura, kuma bansan fuskar taba,saide nasan tanada Funny attitude"

Kallon shi Daddy keyi cikin mamaki.

Yace;"to taya za'a gane ta Anu, ko dai Aljana ta aure ka?? ko kuma sammun da aka man ya fara shafar ka?"


Anuwar girgiza kai yake yace"please Daddy ba aljana bace, tun da naje Katsina abincin ta nike ci, furar ta ko zogalan ta, please Daddy zan nemo ta a kauyan Katsina take, kuma ban son kowa ya bini, saboda kauyen su na tsoron mutane"


"Subhanallah! Anu kana cikin hankalin ka?? Kaman ce kokai waye?? taya za'ayi nabar ka kaje cikin kauyen da suke tsoron mutane tsabar kauyan ci, ina! ba zai yiyu ba lalle aljana ce wannan yarinyar"


Cikin sauri Daddy ya saki hannun shi, yayi saurin tashi yabar dakin, ya fita ranshi a bace.


Yana fitowa yadda Momy ta gan shi yasa tayi saurin tashi tace;

"Lapiya Daddyn Manal??mike damun Anu, wani abu yasa meshi ne??"


Cikin hawaye yace;"makiyya sun taso ni gaba wallahi aljana aka turama ma Anuwar, so suke su halaka man shi wai yanzu duk halin da yake ciki wai akan wata mai fura ce,mun raba shi da Manal da kyar yanzu kuma ya rasa inda zai fada wai sai akan mai fura, ke kin san idan ba Aljana bace ba, to babu yadda za'ayi Anuwar ya koma haka  saboda ita ba"


"What..! Mai fura fa kace?"daga kai yayi.

Jan hannun shi tayi ta zaunar dashi bisa kujera.

Kursim hawaye kawai take, meyasa wannan mai furar zata shigo masu rayuwa??

 
             ************

Ina zaune ni da Shatu munata ta tsar nono tana ban labari ina dariya.


Saiga Jabe a guje, haki yake ya kasa magana.

Shatu tace;"Jabe lafiya?"

Yace;"ga Baffa nan tare da Baban ta"ya nuna ni. 

Ban san lokacin da na mike ba.

Nace;"Da gaske"

Yace;"wallahi ko"

Kama hannu na tayi muka wanke hannun mu tace;"ki natsu nasan zai fahimce ki,kuma ko Baffa bai san abinda ya faru ba dagani sai Iya dan haka kiyi shiru, kinga kin samu lafiya"

Kai na daga amman duk da haka ina jin tsoro gani nike kamar zai gane.

Runtse ido nayi ina tuna lokacin da nike bisa kirjin shi.

Nayi saurin bude ido na lokaci guda hawaye suka fara zubar man.

Sai ga Iya tace"muzo ga Baba na yazo"Shatu ce ta ce to,nikam kamar an daskarar dani wajen

Kama hannu na Shatu tayi muka bar wajen.

Na saka hijab din Shatu muka fito nan inda suke zaune.


Yana bisa tabarma, muna tafiya kaina a kasa jiki na sai kyarma yake, kara gyara tafiya ta nike dan kada ya gane.

Muna zuwa nida Shatu muka zukunna muka gaida shi, Shatu tabar wajen.

Ya kalleni yace;"sannu Nana Allah ya yi maki albarka, makwabcina ya fada man duk irin dukan da akayi maki, kuma Baffan su Shatu ya fada man ashe yaran shi suka tsinto ki cikin daji kin Suma, kiyi hakuri kinji Allah zai maki sakayya"hadda hawayen shi.


Nima hawaye nike,yanzu yakenan idan Baba ya gane abinda ya faru dani wane hali zai shiga??

Na goge hawaye na,da suke bin fuskata.

Baba ya mikama Baffan su Shatu hannu yace;"Nagode Malam Garba Allah ya saka da Alkhairi, naji dadi da ta fada hannu na gari".

Yace;"kada kadamu Mallam ai Nanah diyata ce, Allah ya kara karemu"

Yace;"Ameen"

Ya mike nima na mike nace;"zan dauko kwarya ta"na juya na koma ciki.

Shatu na labe jikin dannin su.

Tayi saurin rungume ni tace;"Allah mun gode maka, yanzu muje ki dauki kwaryar ki"dariya nayi nace;

"Muje na cire maki kayan ki, tunda an wanke nawa"

Bata fuska tayi tace;"wallahi banso kije nabar maki, zan ma kara maki wasu, tunda aure zanyi, kuma tun nan ki cire wannan abin na hannun ki, ki boye daga gani yanada daraja"

Cikin sauri na kalli hannu na, nayi ajiyar zuciya.

Nace;"nagode Shatu, da Baba ya ganshi dana bani"na cire ta saka manshi cikin dan abin sarkar ta da mijin ta ya siyo mata daga birni,kana ta saka cikin bakar leda ta sakaman cikin kayan da taban.


Ina jin dadi sosai yadda Shatu ke nuna man soyayya ban san lokacin da hawaye suka zubo man ba.

Nace;"nagode sosai Shatu"bata fuska tayi 
tace;"kada ki damu ba komai wallahi da ina da dan uwa babba wallahi daya aure ki kodan ki huta amman mu cigaba da fadama Allah zai mana magani"


Hawaye nike ban san dame zan gode ma Shatu ba.

Iya ta shigo tace;"Kubar kukan haka, addu'a ya kamata kuyu kuma yanzu Baban ta na jiran ta, sannan Nanah duk lokacin da kike bukatar taimako kizo wajena koda lokacin Shatu tayi aure kinji?"

Kai na daga saboda ban iya magana.


Na dauki kwarya ta da ƙullin kayan da Shatu taban muka fito.

Baba ya amshi kayan yace;"sun maki yawa, muje"nabi bayan shi na tafi ina dagama Shatu hannu dake tsaye tana hawaye.

Har muka bace muna dagama juna hannu.

Tafiya kadan ta kaimu kauyen mu.

A hanya banda bani hakuri ba abinda Baba keyi sai ban hakuri, ni dai sai dai nace ba komai. 


Muna shiga gida da sallama.

Inna ta fito ta tsaya bakin kofar dakin ta.

Tunda na shigo take kallo na, hakan yasa jikina kyarma ina addu'ar Allah yasa kada ta fahimci komai a tare dani. 


Ma'u ta fito daga daki tazo ta amshi kayan hannun Baba tace;"Baba sannu da zuwa"

Yace;"yawwa Ma'u"

Tace;"Nanah kin dawo, muje na share maki dakin ki"zata amshi kayana Inna tace;

"Wallahi kul kika amshe su, idan kuma kika amshe su sai na bata maki rai, don tabar maki Lado kike wannan biyayyar to dama kin daina, har abada ba zaki auri Lado ba"tura baki Ma'u tayi tace;

"Amman Inana ai 'yar uwata ce dan zan taimake ta, wani abu ne"


Ta amshi kayan hannun nata  suka nufi daki.

Muna zuwa ta ajeman kayan tace;"akwai biredin da Lado ya kawoman jiya kinaci, dan jiya burni yaje"na girgiza kai.

Nace;"naci abinci gidan su Shatu"ta zaunar dani bisa gadon karana.

Ta rike hannu na tace;"wai mikike ma Lado, komai sai yace kalar naki yake so, yaki sona Nanah kuma ni shi nike so banko san ganin Kabiru, shi kuma Lado sai ya kama min  wulakan ci, jiya ma biredin ke ya kawo mawa yayi tunanin kin dawo, hadda lemon kwalba".

Na rike mata hannu nace;"kada ki damu 'yar uwata Lado zai soki kinji"tayi murmushi tace;"nagode".


Ta mike ta fita nabita da kallon tausayi, nida ita ban san wacece abin tausayi ba, mu duka muna cikin jarabawa Allah ka yaye Mana imanin mu .


Haka na yini ni kaɗai cikin daki sallah kaɗai ke fidda ni waje.


               ***********

Momy tace;"Kulsoom fada mana mike faruwa, da gaske Aljana ta auri Anu"

Nan ta fada masu komai. 

Su duka suka hau yin sallallami.

Momy tace;"Lalle maganar ka gaskiya ce, baicin komai sai nata sannan kuma dole kullum a kai shi wajen ta, hadda cewa tana mashi kalar tafiyar Manal, lalle zasu yi amfani da Manal dan yaudarar shi, ya kamata Mai martaba yasan halin da ake ciki"shiru Daddy yayi.


Ya rasa abinyi duk ya rikice jin yadda labarin yake.

Lallai Aljana ce ke son yaudarar shi.


Riƙe zuciyar shi ya farayi tare da runtse ido.

Cikin sauri Momy tace;" lafiya Daddyn Manal??"

Ganin zai fadi yasa Momy tayi wata kara tayi kanshi.


Anuwar na zaune bisa bed dinshi rike da kai.

Jin karar Momy yasa yayi saurin zabura!!

Yayo waje cikin sauri.

Ya iske Momy ta girgiza Daddy su Nifra na kuka.


Cikin tashin hankali ya iso wajen.

Kursim ce tayi saurin kiran Security.

Dan danan aka kira Doctor's sai gasu sun iso.


Suka shiga dashi suka rufe daki.


Momy tace;"Anu meyasa baka jin magana meyasa kake son kashe Daddyn ku, kayi masa biyayya mana, meyasa shin baka son Daddyn naku ya cigaba da rayuwa?".

Shiru yayi idanun shi cike da hawaye saide basu zubo ba.

Tace;"yanzu sai ka zaba, ko rayuwar Daddyn ku ko yarinyar kauye, zabi ya rage naka".

Daga haka tayi hanyar d'akin ta tana kuka.

Nifra tabi bayan ta tana kuka.

Kursim ta taso tazo inda yake.

Itama hawaye take tace;"Haba Prince rayuwar mutun nawa kasa cikin kunci, hadda Daddyn masoyi yarka, kasa mahaifiyar ta kuka, kasa kanwar ta kuka, shin da tana da rai kana tunanin zata yi alfahari da kai? kayi hakuri kayi ma iyayen ka biyayya mana"


Kallon ta yayi yace;"itama fa waccen mutun ce, an shiga hakkin ta tana bukata ta" 


Kara matsowa tayi ta rungume shi tace;"Yanzu kodan lafiyar Daddy kayi hakuri da zuwa Katsina kabari hankali ya kwanta nayi maka alkawarin idan muka koma zan taimaka maka wajen gano yarinyar".

Cikin sauri Anuwar shima ya kara rungume ta jin zata taimaka mashi.

So sai Kursim taji dadin rungumar yau itace rana ta farko da Yarima ya rungume ta a jikin shi.


Hakan yasa hawayen ta suka kasa daukewa.

A bangaren Anuwar ya shagalta jinta yayi tamkar mai furar shi,hakan yasa ya kara manneta da jikin shi, yana sakin numfashi.


A haka Momy ta iske su, jin tafiya yasa Kursim ta dan zame jikin ta.

Anuwar kuma sai lokacin ya lura ashe Kursim ce.

Dan ja da baya yayi, Momy na kallan shi.

Ganin kallon da take mashi yasa yabar wajen.


Ya koma dakin shi, ya zauna tare da dafe kai.

Yanzu idan yayi mata haka bai mata adalci ba.


Amman babu yadda zaiyi.

Tunda ya kusan ceta ya rasa meyasa yanzu jin shi yake na daban, yana bukatar ta.

Zan aure ta kenan? Sai kuma yayi murmushi.


Bayan Daddy ya farfado Momy ta fada mashi yadda ta iske su Anuwar yaji dadi sosai yace idan akayi mashi aure zai rage wasu abubuwan.


A parlo Anuwar kusa da Daddy anata fira.

Sai tattalin Daddy yake, idan Daddy yace yana son abu jikin shi har kyarma yake da yayi mashi shi.



          *************

Tunda asuba haka Inna tazo ta maka man ruwa tace;" tashi kiyi dakan fura".


Baba dake tsaye bakin kofa yace;"kin kyauta to bari kiji, daga yau ta gama tallar komai ko aiken ta kinbar yi, ai kema kin haifa, sannan babu ruwan ki da ita, ta gama yimaki komai a cikin gidan nan kina da diya tayi maki, idan kuma kika saka ta a bakin auren ki, sai idan ni nasakata, kin ban kunya Rakiya wallahi, banyi tunanin zalin cinki yakai haka ba"


Tace;"lalle kam yarinya kinyi nasara a kaina amman kisani ki guji ranar da zan kamaki da laifi"daga haka ta juya tabar wajen Rai bace.



Baba yace;"kinji abinda nace, wahalar ta isa haka"ya juya yayi masallaci.



      **************

Kursim ta samu shiga wajen Daddy da Momy kullun cikin yabon ta suke.


Anuwar kullun yana aikin yin zanen fuskar mai fura.


Kullun sai ya runtse ido yana zanata, yana tuna lokacin da yake shafar fuskar ta.


Ganin zanen fuskar MANAL na fitowa yasa saida ya yaga papers din da yake drawing din.


A haka har sukayi sati biyu a Spain.


Momy da Daddy kullun cikin waya suke yi dasu Abie da Ammi yadda za'a tsara bikin idan sun dawo.


Momy ita tayi order komai na laifen Anuwar.


Anuwar ko kullun cikin zane yake, amman yakasa sai fuskar MANAL da yake zanawa.


Saida yayi murmushi ya ce;"Ina sonki Manal nakasa son kowa ce mace sai ke, ita ma wannan ashe ba sonta nike ba, kawai dai ina tausayin tane tunda na kasa zana fuskar ta sai taki"


Nifra dake tsaye tana kallon shi ta juya cikin sauri 

Ta tsaya a kurido tana kuka.

Ta yaya Anuwar zai amshi wata mace a matsayin mata.

Ita kam ba zata iya fada mashi sakon Momy ba.

Shiru tayi tana tunani

Kodai taje ta fada mashi Amman tana tsoro.


         *************


Haka muke zaune ba ruwa na da Inna sai gaisuwa itama bata amsawa.

Lafiya kalau muke zama da Ma'u har muyi fira.

Inna ko kullun kara tsanata take, kullun addu'ar ta Allah ya kawo abinda zai sa nabar mata gida, duk silata ce komai ya faru, na raba ta da mijin ta gashi kullun Ma'u bata da magana sai Lado.

Sunyi neman abinda zai karya asirin amman shiru.


Anyi auren Shatu amman banje ba saboda Baba ya hanani fita, wai kada a cutar dani ganin abinda ya samu Ma'u nima kada aban nacii.

Amman yace shida kan shi zai kaini naje dakin ta, duk randa ya samu lokaci.


Kwance nike bisa gado ina sun sunar rigar atamfata ranar da abin ya sameni, Ina matukar son kamshin kayan, har wanke su akayi amman kullun kamar ana samasu turaren, naji cikina kamar ana mani motsi.

Daman kwana biyu ina jin motsi.


Gashi kwana biyu kullun cikin kwanciya nike kasala taman yawa.

Nace komai ke damu na??

Kwana biyu kullun cikin kasala bari Baba yazo na fada mashi ya samo man magani.


        *************

Nifra dake tsaye tana hawaye sai ga Momy.

Tayi saurin goge hawayen.


Momy tace;'mike damun kine?kin fadama Anu yazo yaga kayan sunyi?"


Nifra ta goge hawayen ta tace;"Momy kina ganin Yaya Anuwar zai amshi wata mace a matsayin mata?? Momy ina ga kamar ba'a yi mashi dai-dai ba ya kamata ayi shawara dashi"wani kallo Momy tayi mata yasa tayi shiru.

Momy tace;"daman naga yadda kike ma ma Kulsom tamkar kishiryar ki, na lura baki sonta ko kema son Anuwar din kike? Idan son shi kike ki fito ki fadi yafi wannan magan ganun naki".

Cikin kuka tace;"Momy har abada babu aure tsakani na dashi, kana kin manta 'yar uwata fa ya aura kuma ita yake so, Allah ya kyau ta naso mijin 'yar uwata, ina jin kunyar ta koda bata duniyar amman ina jin kunyar ta"


Tsaki Momy tayi tace"idan bazaki iya Fadi mashi ba bani waje ni naje na fada mashi ke kuma kita kuka,ko ince ki chigaba da taya 'yar uwar taki kishi, tunda ita bata duniya kinga sai ki taya ta".

Daga haka tabar Nifra tsaye tayi hanyar dakin Anuwar.

Nifra tayi murmushi takaici ta yi sannan tace naga yanda wannan Al'amarin zai kasan ce, lalle naso naga yadda Yaya Anuwar zaiyi.

DEDICATED TO....MY SISTERS😘

Page 33-34


 _Bissimillah Rahmanir_ _Rahim_



-----------------A hankali Momy ta tura kofar ta shiga.

Hankalin shi yana wajen wayar shi, murmushi yake tare da manna ma wayar kiss.

Gyaran muryar da Momy tayi ne  ya dan jiyo yana kallon ta.

Murmushi ta sakar mashi tare da shigowa ta zauna bisa daya daga kujerun dake a bedroom din shi.

Ta sowa yayi yazo ya zauna kujerar dake kallon ta.

Yace;"Momy kun fidda ranar da za'a koma Nigeri  ne?"

Girgiza kai tayi tace;"Babu rana amman cikin satin nan insha Allah, daman wata magana ce zamuyi, nasan zaka fahimce ni, hukunci Daddyn ku ya yanke akan ka, ba dan komai yasa yayi hakan ba saidan yana ganin ya isa da kai"


Shiru Anuwar yayi yana nazarin maganganun ta.


Kara maida hankalin shi yayi yace;"Momy inaji, kada kidamu fadi koma menene".


Murmushi tayi tace;"Nagode Anu daman mun yanke hukunci a matsayin mu na iyayen ka, idan mun koma da sati biyu za'ayi bikin ku kaida Kulsoom, komai su mai martaba sun riga da sunyi kafin mu iso, sunyi magana da iyayen ta sun baka, damar kayan lefe ne aka kawo shine nace muje kagani sunyi, idan basuyi ba zaka iya karowa, koma a chanza baki daya idan basuyi ba".


Tunda ta fara maganar yake kallon ta, tana gamawa ya juyar da kanshi wani gefe.


Tasowa tayi ta kama hannayen shi tace;"Haba Anu nasan kana da tausayi, musamman ma akan mace to miyasa baka tausayama Kulsoom itama fa mace ce, ka duba kaga duk irin wahalar da tasha, miyasa baka tausayin ta? wallahi Anu Kulsoom ita kaɗai ce wadda zata iya zama da kai saboda tana matukar sonka".


Kara damke hannun shi tayi tace;"yanzu ni 'yar sako ce, Daddyn ku na jiran ka, Amman ina rokon ka alfarma kada kace a'a kodan lafiyar shi, please Anu wannan itace alfarma ta ta farko dana rokeka, a matsayina na Uwar ka kuma tsohuwar sirikar ka"ta ida maganar da hawaye.


Tamkar dutse haka Anuwar ya koma ko motsi ya kasa.

Haka kuma fuskar shi bata nuna wani alamun yadda ko akasin haka ba.


Momy ta saki hannun shi ta mike tana goge hawayen ta tayi waje.


Mikewa yayi tsaye yaje bakin window ya tsaya hannu cikin aljihu.


Kallon lanbun dake waje yake, tsallen da take lokacin da ta nufosu rige da kara a hannunta, murmushi yayi yana kallon yadda take tsalle.



          ***************

Ina jin  shigowar Baba nayi saurin fitowa.

Lokacin Inna ta fito tana tsaye a bakin kofar dakin ta.

Wata harara data sakar man hakan yasa na tsaya daga inda nike.


Baba ya jingine keken shi a bango.

Yace;"Nana barka da gida ya gajiya"nayi saurin karasawa.

Nace;"Baba sannu da zuwa, andawo lafiya"ya mikoman wata leda.

Tun kafin tazo hannuna miyau dina ya tsinke.

Na amsa nayi godiya na juya cikin sauri na isa daki.


Soyayyan kifi ne a ciki, cikin sauri nike chin kifin dan danan na chinye.


Na fito na wanke hannu na, na zauna  ina zama   naji zuciyata na tashi.


Lokaci guda naji cikina ya murɗa.

Zuciya ta sai tashi take, jin amai yazo man lokaci guda yasa nayi saurin mikewa nayi waje a guje.


Ba kowa hakan yasa nayi bandaki.

Ina zuwa sai amai duk abinda naci saida na juye shi.


Nayi mamaki sosai yadda akayi hakan ta faru saboda jikina ba zafi ko kaɗan. 

Gashi inacin kifi sosai to amman ya akayi hakan ta faru??

Na wanke bakina na fito dai-dai lokacin da Inna ta shigo rike da kwaryar nika a hannunta.


Ta kalleni tun daga sama har kasa.


Tace;"idan tayi wari munji"nide bance komai ba, naje na aje butar nayi dakina na kwanta.


Har dare ina kwance babu abinda ke ban sha'awa balle inci


Haka na kwanta jiki na babu kwari, ina son inje wajen karatu amman ban iyawa, kuma ni ban masan mizan cema Baba yana damuna ba, saboda yanzu ban jin dadin komai. 


Haka na kwanta tare da addu'ar bacci a bakina rigata na bisa hanci na ina sun sunawa har bacci ya daukeni.


            **********

Gajiyar da yayi da tsayuwar yasa ya dawo ya zauna.


Wayar shice  ta fara kara yasa hannu ya dauka.

Muryar Ammi ce a dayan ban garen.

Kara gyara zama yayi yasa Bluetooth a kunnen shi.

Yace;"Ammi kin mantani kinbar kirana ko?"

Murmushi tayi wanda saida yaji sautin shi tace;"Ban manta da kai ba Ɗana, hidimar Ƙanwata akeyi Rahama kasan tana nan gida munsha bikin sunan Manal".


Yace;"Ohh haka ne aba Maman Manal hakuri ina nan zuwa,mun kusa dawowa, ashafa man kan Manal dina, ace mata Uncle dinta na nan zuwa ganin ta".


Dariya tayi tace;"abunda diyar taku bata da kyau, diyata Manal ta fita kyau"

Shima murmushi yayi yace;"lalle Ammi babbar diyar tamu kike cema bata da kyau, duk garar da muka yi maku?"

Dariya itama tayi sun dan dade suna fira kana tace;"Daman akan maganar auren kane na kira ka"


Jin haka yasa wutar Anuwar ta dauke.

Ammi tace;"Yanzu Daddyn ku suka gama waya da Mai martaba yace saboda an maka magana fushi kake da kowa, baka fita chin abinci ba sannan kai ke bashi magani amman yau baka zo ba har time ya wuce".

Shiru yayi yana sauraren ta tace;"Haba Yarima kada kamanta kalar kaunar da yake maka mana, saboda kaunar da yake maka yasa ya dauki 'yar shi 'yar shekara tara ya aura maka, duk saboda kaunar da yake maka, saboda farin cikin ka, wahalar da yayi dakai mahaifin ka baiyi dakai ba, sannan yanzu ka kasa mashi biyayya akan zai kara inganta rayuwar ka, taimakon ka zaiyi fa, kayi tunani bai kamata kayi mashi haka, kyau ace ka nuna mashi yafi karfin komai a wajen ka, kayi hakuri nasan ba'a shiga rayuwar ka kullun abinda kake so kake yi saide a matsayina na mahaifiyar ka naga ya dace na baka shawara wannan shawara ce ba umarniba"

Daga haka ta kashe waya.


Shiru yayi yana tunani, duba time yayi ganin lokacin shan maganin Dad ya wuce yasa yayi saurin tashi ya fita.

Parlo ya fito babu kowa ciki, kasa ya nufa.

A hankali yake sabko wa daga matakalan benan.

Daga Daddy sai Momy ne a parlon kasan.

Kaya ne a gaban Momy tana dagawa Daddy na zaune bisa kujera.


Bai iya kirga jikun kunan dake gaban Momy gasunan zube a kasa.


Daddy ya dago yana kallon shi.

Ganin inda Daddy ke kallo yasa itama Momy tabi wajen da kallo.


Murmushi Daddy ya sakar mashi shima haka ya maida mashi.


Baiyi magana ba ya zauna kusa da Daddy.


Kallon Daddy yake yana kallon maganin dake kusa dashi.

Daddy ya gane abinda yake nufi yayi murmushi yace;"bazan sha magani ba sai Anu dina yazo yaban, Momyn ka ce ta dauko zata ban nace ban sha, saboda ba ita ke ban ba, zangani idan Anu yana san lafiyar Daddyn shi"


Murmushi yayi tare da tashi ya dauko ruwa ya mika ma Daddy maganin ya mika mashi ruwa.


Har saida ya shanye kana Daddy yace;"kana fushi dani har yanzu, saboda zan maka aure shine kake fushi dani, wallahi Anu hankalina ya tashi ganin yadda ka kasa control din kan ka har saida ka kusanci km karamar yarinya yanzu a lokacin da kana da mata ita zaka jemawa, kayi hakuri bazan fasa auren nan ba, ina gudun kada asake samun irin wannan matsalar, kasan ada ban ba maganar karfi ba har rokan maka Mai martaba nike ya kara maka lokaci amman ganin abunda ya faru nima ina goyan bayan shi"


Shiru Anuwar yayi ya rasa mizai cema Daddy.


Daddy yace;"kaga kayan da Momy tayi order sunyi ko a kara wasu?"

Kai ya daga Daddy yace;kaga yanzu next week zamu koma ko akwai abinda kakeso?"girgiza kai yayi.


Daddy yace;"saura naka laifen Momyn ku bata iya zaben kayan maza ba, shiyasa zan sa Usman yayi maka"yana maganar yana dariya.

Murmushi Anuwar yayi tare da mikewa yace;"Daddy zan fita"kallon shi Daddy keyi cikin mamaki yasan bai zuwa ko ina, kuma baida friends, towai miyasa Anuwar keson zuwa Spain, kuma bai taɓa jin ya ambaci sunan wani dasunan abokin Shiba.

A Nigerian Turkish yayi Primary, secondary dinshi yayita a Dubai babu wanda yasani a Spain, amman yana yawan zuwa.


Ganin Daddy na tunani yasa yace;"Daddy please kabarni zan kula da kaina".

Dariya Momy tayi tace;"lallai a Spain zaku yi Honeymoon dinku, duk kasa shen da muke zuwa baka fita sai munzo Spain lalle dole a yabama Spain"


Dariya Daddy yayi yace;"nima hakan ya sani dogon nazari"

Shima Anuwar murmushi yayi yace;"wai Dad kamanta pack din nan da ka taba sawa aka kaimu ranar Birthday din Manal na five years, to ai chanzani yau shekarar ta sha bakwai kenan, ina son naje".


Murmushi Dad yayi yace;"sai ka dawo"ita kuma Momy bata kara magana saima shiru tayi tana kallon Anuwar.


Dad yace;"Amman ai yanzu dare yayi baka bari sai gobe".


Bata fuska yayi yace;"Dad kamanta karfe Daya na dare aka haifeta da minti tara, Momy please kice yabarni na tafi zanje Hospital din da aka haifeta, daga chan na wuce pack ba gida zan kwana ba"

Murmushi Momy tayi saboda idan tayi magana hawayen dake makale a idon ta zasu zubo.

Ganin haka yasa Daddy yace;"ka kula saika dawo"ya juya zai fita kenan yayi kicibis da Kursim dake tsaye tana hawaye a bakin kofa.


Kallon ta yake yana mata kallon tuhuma yace;"mikikeyi a tsaye bakin kofa, yanzu idan maganar sirri muke fa".

Ta goge hawayen ta tace;"nazo shigowa naji kana maganar Birthday din Manal shine na tsaya ka gama, dan Allah zan raka ka ina. son na kasan ce dakai a wannan daren kodan muhimmancin shi a wajen ka"



Saboda yadda yake jin yau zuciyar fes yasa ya daga mata kai.


Cikin sauri tace;"one minute please"tare da hada hannayen ta biyu.

Daga Kai yayi cikin sauri tayi ciki shi Kuma yayi waje.

Yana tsaye sai gata sanye da wando da riga, sai ta aza Dubai Abaya(after dress)a sama Amman bata saka maballen taba, tayi rolling din gyalen rigar ta saka wata jakka a bayanta tayi kyau sosai dan surar jikin ta ta bayyana.


Tun kafin ta iso kam shinta ya iso inda yake.


Tana isowa ta sakar mashi murmushi tace;"da wane motor zamu fita?".


Baiyi magana ba ya nufi wata 'yar karamar mota fara.


Cikin sauri ta nufi wajen Driver ta amso makullinta.


Ta bude mashi ya shiga ta kulle.


Motar a bude take saman ta, duk budewar idon Kursim bata taba ganin kalar motar ba.

Ta burgeta sosai hakan yasa take driving din cikin nishadi.


Shike nuna mata inda zasu har suka isa.

Ga mamakin ta a asibitin tunda suka shiga har suka isa office din wani Doctor duk wanda suka hadu dashi inde ma'aikaci ne to yasan Anuwar, sai sungaisa.


Suna shiga taga Doctor din ya taso cikin fara'a ya rungimi Anuwar.

Bayan sun gaisane yake ce mashi matar shice wannan mai kyaun.


Jin an ambace ta maikyau a gaban Anuwar yasa tayi murmushi mai kayatar wa.


Tayi saurin amsawa da eh, kana suka gaisa.


Bata kara shiga mamaki ba saida taji Anuwar yace;"ya fitar da abinda za'a ba da ga yaran da za'a haifa Yau"

Murmushi Doctor din yayi yace;"ai tun kafin kazo munyi komai kamar yadda aka saba"bata gane maganar ba hakan yasa take son karin bayani.



Anuwar ya mike zai zagaya asibitin, wata Nurse ce ta shigo ganin Anuwar yasa ta saki murmushi, suka gaisa tana cewa shekarar ta dawo kenan tunda mungan ka shima dariya yake.


Ta juyo tana kallo na cikin mamaki tace matar shice tace eh.


Da Anuwar da Doctor din suka fita daga ita sai wannan baturiyar Nurse din


Nace mata meyasa naji Doctor yace za'ayi kamar yadda aka saba me Anuwar keyi a irin wannan ranar tace;

"A wannan asibitin aka haifi MANAL, tunda ta mutu duk ranar zagayo war haihuwar ta, Prince na bada kyauta mai tsoka ga duk yarinya ko yaran da aka haifa irin ranar, shine yau yazo, muna da masu ciki duk wadda ta haihu tana da kyauta mai tsoka, ko bai nan za'a aje sunan wanda ta haihu a ranar duk shekara haka yake".


Wata irin raunanniyar ajiyar zuciya Kursim tayi.


Nurse ta dauki file din da tazo dauka tayi ma Kursim ban kwana ta fita.


Tana kallon series fim na kasashe kala-kala amman bata taɓa ganin irin wannan soyayyar ba.

Anya idan ta biye ma son zuciyar ta ba zata wahala ba??

Tana tsakar tunani sai gasu sun dawo suka yi ban kwana.


Suka bar wajen basu yi tafiya mai nisa ba suka isa wani haɗaɗɗen pack.


Wajen mai ice cream suka nufa.


Shima cikin fara'a ya tarbi Anuwar yana cewa sannu da zuwa Prince.

Shima abin mamaki maganar MANAL yake.

Miko mashi wani ice cream yayi.

Bata fuska Anuwar yayi.

Murmushi mai ice cream yayi yace;"wannan yana da dadi Madam za'a ba , ga ice cream din dana san kana ba yaran nan kalar wanda MANAL kesha, dan nasan yau zaka zo shiyasa gashinan da yawa aka ajema, kuma koba ka zoba yau saina bada shi ga yara"


Dariya Anuwar yayi ya mikama Kursim ƙin amsa tayi.


Kallon ta yayi murmushi ta saki tace;"nima kallar wanda MANAL ke sha zan sha".


Murmushi yayi da yake da turanci tayi maganar yasa mai ice cream baiji abinda TACE ba, yayi murmushi ya miko mata.


Ta amsa ta juya tana goge hawayen dake zubo mata.

Wani waje ta samu ta zauna.


Tafi minti talatin tana tunani.

Koda ta juya ta hangi Prince shi da yara suna dariya yana rungumar su yana mika masu Ice cream.


Dariya tayi ta mike, ta nufi inda suke photuna take mashi shida yaran.


Haka har kusan awa guda suna abu daya.

Tana mashi photuna tana mashi video da wayar ta.


Cikin nishadi yake har karfe Daya.


Bata yi mamaki ba saida taga yaje wajen mai ice cream ya amso abin sallah.


Ya fara sallah a wajen tana zaune har asuba yana abu guda.


Tun tana hawaye har ta fara tausayin shi har bacci ya fara daukar ta daga  zaune, sai wajen asuba ta bude ido tace wannan wace irin Jarabta ce haka.


Saida akayi asuba kana suka nufi hanyar gida.


Suna zuwa ta wuce daki saboda a gajiye take ga bacci ta naji. 


Dakin shi ya shiga yayi wanka ya fara karatun Al-qura'ani cikin murya mai sanyi.


A haka har rana tayi kana ya fito.


Koda ya fito ya iske anata kallon Videon da Kursim tayi mashi.

Yana isowa Daddy yace;"Nayi maka laifi nasha magani, dan nasan kana bukatar hutu nama yi tunanin bacci kake".


Nifra tace;"Morning Yaya Anu"da kai ya amsa.

Kursim ta gaishe shi Daddy yace;"photunan jiya muke kallo sunyi kyau sosai, kaima Allah ya baka yara da wuri, yadda kake son yara Allah ya kawosu nan da wasu watanni"

Ita dai Kursim kunya taji amman shi Anuwar yace;"Amin Dad ina son yara sosai ina son yara masu yawa kayi man addu'a Allah yaban Baby nan kusa"dariya suka yi 

Momy tace"to Allah ya bada mai Albarka Allah ya nunaman yaron ka Anuwar"dariya Nifra tayi tace;

"Idan kana so kaji maganar Yaya Anuwar to kayi masa maganar yara, dan haka Auntie Kulsoom saiki shirya"tana maganar tana dariya.


Kursim ta dukar da kanta kasa"Momy ta da fata tace kyaleta Kulsoom"nan suka yi Breakfast harda Anuwar yau itace rana ta farko daya shiga cikin su.


Haka suka cigaba da rayuwa a Spain kullun cikin tunanin mai furar shi yake.


Gashi ya kasa zana fuskar ta.


Haka sukaci sati guda yau watan su guda kenan Dad ya samu lafiya ya gama shan magun gunan shi.


Suka nufo Nigeria baki daya kowanen su da irin abunda yake sakawa a zuciyar shi.


Kursim murnar take lokacin auren ta yayi.

Shi kuma Anuwar murna yake zaiga mai furar shi.

Su Momy kuma yadda za'a kayata bikin wanda ba'a taɓa yin shi a Nigeria ba suke magana.


Haka jirgin su ya iso Nigeria


             *************
Haka muke rayuwa a gidan bani wata wahala amman babu ruwana da Inna sai gaisuwa

Ina zaune saiga Ma'u ta shigo da langa a hannu.

Dumame ne ta aje a gabana, nace nagode tace to ta juya.

Ina saka hannu cikin abincin naji zuciyata na tashi nayi saurin toshe hancina ban son kamshin miyar.



Yaune ranar da nike saran jinin al'ada yazo man shiru sai na kara kwana biyu nan ma shiru babu alamun shi


Ina jin motsi a cikina amman ban kawo komai a raina ba,gashi ba komai nike ciba


Nakara kwana biyu Amman shiru babu al'ada karatun fikihu ya dawo man.


Zaunawa nayi bakin gado na ina nazarin alamomin masu ciki.

Gaba na ya fadi gashi sai wani haske nike, kullun Inna cikin kallona take daga sama har kasa tana cewa idan tayi wari munji.


Mikenan take nufi?? innalillahi!! Abinda zai bani tabbacin ina da ciki shine rashin ganin al'ada ta.


Wani irin faduwar gaba tare da wasu zafafan hawaye suka zuboman.


Me zai faru ne dani?? Wannan wace irin jarabtar rayuwa ce nagama da wahalar duka da zagi da zangwama na Inna da mutane yanzu kuma wani tabo zai shafeni?


Kenan yanzu ne zan fara ganin wahalar rayuwa ada chan sulmin tabu ne.


Dafe kirji nayi nace;"yanzu idan Inna ta sani zata kaini gidan Mai gari kenan?daman jiran laifi na take"


Mikewa nayi tsaye yanzu me zan yi kenan inje na tambayi inda aka kai Shatu idan akwai abinda zan sha ya zube.


Wata zuciyar tace a'a nace to idan ba haka ba idan Inna tasan da wannan cikin ya kenan??

DEDICATED TO....MY SISTERS😘


Page-35-36


 _Bissimillah Rahmanir_ _Rahim_


----------------Cikin takon Isa da kasaita yake sabkowa daga bisa matakalar jirgin da ya kawosu.


Ta ko ina daukar su ake da Camera, airport din cike take da manema labarai da security.


Black din glas ne a fuskar shi, sanye yake da light blue din yadi kalar na jikin Daddy daka gansu kaga Uba da Ɗana.


Fuskar nan kamar ta kullun ce yau ma babu murmushi ko kaɗan a fuskar shi.


Sosai airport ɗin ya cika da mutane hakan yasa Security sai kara tura mutane baya suke masu daukar rahoto da sauran mutane.


Daddy ne ke daga masu  hannu sai Momy sai Nifra itama da take sanye da Suit pink color itama Black din glas ne a fuskar ta.


Sai Kursim dake kusa da ita cikin doguwar riga, su duka sunyi kyau.


Motar Daddy da Momy ne a farko sai motar Kursim da Nifra.

Anuwar motar shi shikaɗai ne a ciki.


Suna zama Kursim ta kalli Nifra tace;"Meyasa Prince ya shiga mota shi kaɗai ?nayi tunanin a tare zamuje"

Kallon ta Nifra tayi tare da murmushi tace;"ko dasu Dad bai shiga mota tare da su, shi kaɗai yake shiga motar shi ya zauna, kafin Sister Manal ta rasu motar su guda, amman tun daga lokacin da ta rasu idan zamu yi tafiya shi kaɗai yake zama a mota guda, mun taɓa zuwa wata Umara mu duka motar da zai shiga nayi saurin shiga lokacin da yake kokarin shiga, wallahi juya wa yayi ya nufi wata ya shiga, Manal Kaɗai ce a duniya ke juya shi"

Murmushi Kursim tayi cikin ranta tace sai mai fura.

Shiru Kursim tayi tana tunanin da wannan MANAL din nada rai da bata san wane kalar gata da so za'a nuna mata ba.

Idan mutum ya mutu ana mantawa dashi cikin watanni amman ita gata tayi shekaru da mutuwa amman mutuwar ta kamar yau akayi ta.


Jin shirun yayi yawa yasa Nifra tace;"zan baki wani sirri game da Yaya Anuu idan kina son kiga murmushi to kusan ci wajen da yara suke yana son yara sosai, ina maki fatan samun su da wurii"


Mamaki Kursim take yadda lokaci guda Nifra ta chanza.


Nifra ta fahimci Kursim sai tace;"kiga na chanza ko? Na chanza ne saboda yadda kika nuna murna ranar Birthday din sister Manal"


Murmushi Kursim tayi cikin ranta tace,lalle yau taga karshen so, shi family din idan kana so a soka saika nuna kauna ga Manal waccr bata duniya, to wannan MANAL da tana duniya da kila ko ganinta ba zasu samu damar yiba saboda matsayi.


Dai-dai lokacin motar su ta tsaya a cikin sauri aka bude masu tare da aza masu red din carpet a kasa.


Nan ma wajen cike yake da yan jarida da muta ne, banda daukar su photo babu abinda ake, har suka kai wajen kofar shiga.


Tana waigowa taga Yarima tafe shi da Daddy sun jera Dad na rike da hannun shi.

Wani kyau da annuri ta ga ya kara.


Juyawa tayi tabi bayan Nifra ta shiga ciki.



              **************

Zirga-zirga nike a cikin dakina na kasa fidda shawara.


Hannuna nasa cikin rigata na azashi bisa ciki na, jin motsi hakan yasa hawaye masu zafi suka zubo man. 


Nayi saurin gogewa na koma na zauna nayi ta gumi.


Jin motsin Baba yasa nayi saurin fitowa.


Lokacin ya juya da keken sa zai fita, nayi saurin isa kusa dashi nace;

"Baba fita zaka yi?? wai daman haka nace ina son zuwa wajen Shatu idan kasamu lokaci" 


Murmushi yayi yace;"yi hakuri Nana na manta shab wallahi abun na raina kullun ina son ince maki kishirya mantawa nike"


Nayi murmushin karfin hali nace;"ba komai Baba daman nayi kewar tane".


Yace;"kada ki damu dauko mayafin ki muje, daman kwana biyu ina son in fadi maki ina ta kokarin zuwa Gubi ne wajen tsohuwar matar Yaya bata san ya rasu ba, yana da kyau ta sani duk da sun rabu, ina kokarin inbar maki kudi masu tsoka waɗan da zasu isheki har indawo".


Murmushi nayi na dauko Hijab dina na hau bayan keken shi, muka fita.


Muna tafe yana ban labarin Gubi a chan ciwon ya kama dan Uwan shi, shi yaje ya taho dashi, shima malami ne yan uwan shi malamai suka yi mashi sammu shekara tara kenan yana kwance, a chan ya auri matar shi Rabi tana da kirki ba su taɓa haihuwa ba daman tana da yaran ta daga baya ya aure ta mijin tane itama ya rasu uban yaranta, anta surutu a kauyen daga zuwan malami ta aure shi ba'asan asalin shi ba.


A lokacin shi ba ƙone a garin amman lokaci guda yayi kaurin suna aka san shi a kauyuka na kusa dasu da kauyan su, hakan yaja kiyayya daga malam garin shine wani yayi mashi sammu yana da son zumunci kuma yana zuwa wajen iyalin shi, jin shirun da suka ji sai hankalin iyalin shi ya tashi gashi ni kaɗai nasan garin saboda ni kaɗai ne naje daurin auren shi, hakan yasa na shirya naje.

Koda naje na iske shi duk ya rame nan nasan abinda ke faruwa nace zan dauke shi mu koma gida dangin Rabi suka ce saidai araba auren daman suke taimaka mata kuma sun gaji, suka ce saidai a raba auren tunda ba zuri'a a tsakanin su, ba zasu yadda subada 'yar uwar suba taje wata uwa duniya, daman wai saida suka yi yar jejeniya, kuma gata da manyan & ƴaƴa, haka yayi mata saki guda muka kama hanya, ina yawan zuwa garin harma dai cikin rani, suna da wani Gulbi(korama)nan kusa dashi nike noman rani.

tana da mutunci mun saba da ita sosai, bata taɓa ɓataman ba haka nima ina mata biyayya.

Nayi ajiyar zuciya, yace;"ni kaina ban san barin ki saboda yadda na sameki wannan tafiyar, shiyasa nike so na baki kudi wadan da zasu isheki, munyi zaman mutumci da ita ya kamata tasan komai, wallahi yanzu idan naganta kunyar ta zanji, saboda tun lokacin da yabar garin sau biyu naje tsawon shekara tara, gashi ta nuna ma dan uwana soyayya, kiyi hakuri dole tasa zan barki, tafiyar kwana biyu an azabtar dake ina ga kusan sati biyu zanyi"shiru nayi nima Rabin nan taban tausayi nasan yanzu ta kara tsufa.


Haka yayta bani labarin kirkinta har muka isa wasu gidaje na fulani.


Yace; " a yadda akayi man kwatance nan ne, zanje kasuwar kauyen Mahuta sai na dawo zan biyo na dauke ki"

Murmushi nayi nace;"Allah ya taimaka Baba Allah ya bada sa'a"


"Amin Nana"ya juya ta tashi na kalli wajen gida guda ne amman kwai bukkoki kalar na fulani a ciki.


Ina shiga naga yara suna wasa ga iyayen su chan ƙarƙashin inuwar bedi.

Nayi sallama suka juyo suna kallona.

Duk kauyen cinmu sai naga nafi wadan nan lalle Allah mai iko haka duniyar take.


Suka amsa da Hausar su wadda bata fita dai-dai suna kara bina da kallo.


Har na fara tunanin kodai ba nan bane gidan?

Sai kuma naga wani gini ya banbanta da sauran dakin kasa su kuma sauran duk bukkoki ne, kuma an shafa mashi farar kasa, nayi ajiyar zuciya nace kila gidan ne.

Na gaida su nace;"Dan Allah nan aka kawo Shatu".

Budar bakin su suka ce;"kece Nana ta gidan Liman?"cikin mamaki na girgiza Kai.


Ai kafin nayi wani Abu yaran dake wasa sun ruga suna fulatanci suna ambatar sunana.

Sunyi dakin nan na kasa mai fentin farar kasa.

Sauran matan suka washe baki daya tace;"ai kullun sai Shatu tayi maganar ki,  tai ta aiken Jabe yazo dan ta aike shi wajen ki yaki zuwa"murmushi nayi.


Dayar tace;"ga ma tanan zuwa"

Nayi saurin juyawa na ganta ta rugo a guje tana dariya.


Nima na ruga muka rungume juna muna dariya.


Tayi ma sauran magana da fulatanci ta kama hannu na muka nufi dakin ta.


Muna tura kofa naga  wata 'yar baranda ansaka gadon karfe ƙarami,wajen zama ne, sai kuma bandaki, muka shiga dakin ta daya sha kayan jere na kauye da gadon karfe mai rumfa, lalle an kashe mata kudi dakin cike yake da tasoshi.

Muka zauna bakin wata katifa dake kasa itama ansa mata zanen gado.


Ta mike ta kawo mani ruwa cikin kofin silver ta zuba min ta zuban cikin karami, ta dauko wasu kwanoni ta ajeman.

Tace;"kai lalle ba sai na tambaya ba duk kin chanza kin kara fari da kyau lalle addu'ar mu ta amsu yanzu baki cikin wahala sai addu'ar miji na gari"

Nayi murmushi nace;"bar abincin nan wata magana nike son yi ina zargin wani abu"

Ɓata fuska tayi tace;"wallahi sai kinci abinci na, kuma kalar abincin yan burni ne nasan baki taba cin taba, yarinya taliya ce kalar ta leda kinsan neman kudi mijina yake zuwa duk rugar nan ni kaɗai ke chinta, kema sai kinci kinji, munyi maganar daga baya kuma na rantse".

Nayi murmushi karfin hali nace;"ina mijin yake?"tace;"jiya ya koma tun bayan auren mu baije ba sai jiya ya koma"


Ta zuba man ina dan danawa naji da dadi nayi saurin ci, dan danan na cinye nasha ruwa naji cikina ya cika.


Nace;"wallahi na rikece Shatu"tace;

"Kamar ya bayan naga har kyau kin kara ina ta murna naga kinyi haske"

Nan na fada mata abinda ke faruwa.

Kasa motsi tayi sai sakin baki da tayi tana kallona.


Na matsa kusa da ita ina hawaye nace;"ki taimaka man Shatu ki fadaman cikin ne da gaske?"


Ta goge hawayen da suka zubo mata tace;"lalle da gaske ciki ne dake, yanzu ya zamuyi?"

Na fadi mata muzubar da cikin idan tasan wani abu da ake sha.


Tace;"wallahi ban sani ba kuma ance mutuwa ake,ta taɓa jin wata suna magana kinsan yaran tudu basu da kunya daya tayi ciki aka je zubar mata da cikin ta mutu, suma kenan da suke kusa da asibiti kuma a birni akaje amman ta mutu, kuma ance haramun ne".


Yanzu ya zanyi kenan?

Tace;"kawai ki gudu wajen dangin mahaifiyar ki"cikin kuka nace;"na fada maki ban san komai game dasu ba, ko wajen dangin Baba bani zuwa dasu Ma'u Kaɗai ake tafiya, yanzu ya zanyi kenan?"


Tace;"Kuma Baba bai taɓa fada maki yana da yan uwa wani gari ba da sai ki gudu a chan".

Na girgiza Kai,tace;"ki dai yi tunani"nayi shiru Ina tunani.

Sai chan nayi shiru Ina tunani nace;"yawwa saidai wata basu da zumunci saidai alakar mutunci kuma yace tana da mutunci amman fa da nisa kuma chan zashi"

Tace;"yawwa ya sunan garin,koma dai minene indai ba zata cutar dake ba"

Cikin sauri nace;"Gubi"

Tace;"to minene sunan babban garin kamar nan katsina Batagarawa kinga Katsina ne babban birni Batagarawa kuma mai bi mashi "

Nayi shiru ina tunani nace;"cikin labarin bai ambaci sunan Babban binnin ba"


Tace;"toki tambaye shi cikin hikima"na daga kai duk jikina ya mutu.

Tace;"lafiya naga kamar baki amince da shawarata ba?"

Nace;"Ina tunanin anya zan iya guduwa inbar Baba? Nidai nafison nima ya zube ai ina jin ana cewa wasu cikin su ya zube"

Tace;"Ina tsoron kada ki rasa ranki kinji fa abinda ya faru da yarinyar tudu wallahi bazan baki goyan baya ba, kije ki rasa rayuwar ki kuma kinyi ma Allah laifi ina maki fatan ki cinye jarabawar ki Allah zai taimaka maki"nayi shiru nidai kam bazan iya guduwa ba.

Tace;"kinga idan Baba ya tafi tunda mijina bai nan kizo nan ki ringa yini tunda kina amai ga Kuma kasala,tunda baki yadda da guduwar ba, kinga Koda Inna zata gane idan Baba na nan abin zai zo maki da saukii"

Na daga kai alamar amincewa



Haka muka yini sai yamma Baba yazo muka tafi muna dagama juna hannu.


Muna tafiya ina bayan keken shi nace;"wai Baba ya sunan Babban birnin garin su Inna Rabi?"

Yayi murmushi yace;"kin taba jin sunan Bauchii...!"yaja maganar.

Nayi shiru kamar Maison tuna wani abu, na girgiza kai alamar a'a 

Yace;"to sunan Babban birnin su kenan"

Nayi shiru tare da  kara mai-maita Bauchiii.....


            *************

Har dare bata ga Anuwar ba hakan yasa hankali ta ya tashi ina yaje kada ace katsina ya tafi.


Yana dakin shi a bakin window hannu cikin aljihu.


Yana kallon sararin samaniyya.

Yanzu me zaiyi kenan??

Wata dabara ta fado mashi ya juya yayi hanyar fita.

Part din Momy ya shiga.

Yana zuwa ya isketa tana magana Daddy na kusa da ita waya take.


Shima kusa da Daddy yana zaune remote a hannun shi.

Bayan ta gama ta maida hankalin ta wajen Anuwar tace;"Anu ya kake ne?"

Yace;"Momy yaushe Kulsoom zata koma?"

Daddy dake kallon News ya juyo yana kallon shi cikin Mamaki.

Daddy yace;"miyasa ka tambaya?"

Kai tsaye yace;"ina son zuwa ne"

Daddy yace;"Babu inda zaka, saboda aljanar yarinyar nan kake son zuwa kuma ba zaka ba gobe zamu wuce Bauchi, ita kuma zata wuce Katsina"


Shiru yayi ya kara hade rai Momy tace;"kada kadamu kabari agama biki kana kaji Anu na, zan taimaka maka nima saboda kace karamar yarinya ce zuciyata ta sosu"

Kallon ta Daddy yayi cikin mamaki yace;"kina hauka ne biye mashi zakuyi ku bata man siyasa ace dana yayi ma karamar yarinya fade, bazai yiyuba, ba'a gidan nan ba"

Nan ya cigaba da fada yana daga murya har Nifra da Kursim suka jiyo tare suka shigo


Cikin fushi Daddy ya juya idon shi har ja suka yi. 

Zuciyar Kursim tayi fari tas taji dadi amman bata bayyana ba.

Shiru falon Nifra ta matsa kusa da Momy tace;"Momy wallahi nima yarinyar taban tausayi haka yace fa iyakar ta girma na, Amman ace an mata fyade abun tausayi ce please Mom kiyi wani Abu"

Kallon ta Kursim take cikin mamaki lalle maganar Daddy Aljana ce wannan yarinyar taya za'a yi ace har ta saye zuciyar su bayan zuciyar Anuwar data saye.

Maida numfashi tayi ta juya 


Nifra ta tashi taje inda Anuwar yake zaune ta kama hannun shi tace;"zan taimaka maka Yaya please kasaki ranka nima ina son yarinyar kodan kalar tafiyar sister na"

Murmushi yayi suka hade hannun su Momy ma ta aza nata bisa nasu.

Su kalli juna sukayi dariya.

Momy tace;"Nayi alkarin zan taimaka maka da duk karfina Anu dina"

Nifra ma tace kamar yadda Momy tace

Anuwar yayi murmushin nasara, tareda lumshe ido ya hango gudun ta.



Qurrah
