بِسْمِ اللهِ ، تَوَكَّلْـتُ عَلَى اللهِ وَلاَ حَوْلَ وَلاَ قُـوَّةَ إِلاَّ بِاللهِ.

Bismil-lah, tawakkaltu alal-lah, wala hawla wala kuwwata illa billah.
Da sunan Allah (nake fita), na dogara ga Allah, kuma babu dabara, babu karfi sai da Allah.

اللّهُـمَّ إِنِّـي أَعـوذُ بِكَ أَنْ أَضِـلَّ أَوْ أُضَـلَّ ، أَوْ أَزِلَّ أَوْ أُزَلَّ ،  أَوْ أَظْلِـمَ أَوْ أَُظْلَـمَ ، أَوْ أَجْهَلَ أَوْ يُـجْهَلَ عَلَـيّ .

Allahumma innee a'uzu bika an adilla aw udalla, aw azilla aw ozall, aw azlima aw uzlama, aw ajhala aw yujhala alayya.

Ya Allah! Ina neman tsarinka daga na bata ko a batar da ni, ko na zame ko a zamar dani, ko na yi zalunci ko a zalunce ni, ko na yi wauta ko a yi mini wauta.


DEDICATED TO....
MY SISTERS😘


Page-37-38



 _Bissimillah Rahmanir_ _Rahim_



--------------Muna shiga ya jin gine keken shi a bango, na dauko buta na zuba mashi ruwan alwala  na juya nima na zuba na fara alwala.


Duk abinda ake a kan idon Inna amman bata tanka ba, sai harare-harare da takeyi.


Na shiga daki na shimfida kallabin da nike sallah bisa nayi sallah kana na zauna Ina karatun Al-qura'ani har akayi isha'i kana nayi sallah na juya na hau gado na kwanta.



             *************

Goyan bayan dasu Momy suka bashi yasa yaji zuciyar shi cikin farin ciki.


Har mantawa yayi da wani auren Kursim da za'ayi.


Haka yayi bacci zuciyar shi cikin farin ciki.


A bangaren Kursim tana daki abin duniya ya isheta game da supporting din da yasamu wajen su Nifra da Momy.


Yanzu ya zatayi kenan??

Cikin sauri ta dauki wayar ta ta kira Hanan.


Tace;"Hanan akwai matsala fa"shiru Hanan tayi.

Shirun da tayi yasa Kursim kara mai maita maganar.

Hanan tace;"nasan matsalar ki ina tunani ne, matsalar bazata wuce shegiyar mayyar mai furar nan ba, koba ita bace matsalar?"

A jiyar zuciya Kursim tayi tace;"wallahi itace yanzu kinsan matsalar guda ce Momy?"

Nan Kursim tabata labarin yadda akayi yanzu.


Hanan tace;"kibari gobe kina iso kiyiman waya inzo musan abinda zamuyi tun kamin asamu matsala dan Momy tana power zatayi amfani da Power dinta wajen taimaka mashi sati guda yayi yawa sungano kauyan su, dan haka gobe kina iso kiyiman waya"


A jiyar zuciya tayi tace;"Nagode Hanan saina iso wallahi da kyat zan iya yin bacci"

Hakade Hanan taita kwantar mata da hankali.



          *************

Tun da asuba Baba ya kwan kwasaman kofa na mike na bude na gaishe shi.


Yace;"tafiya zaiyi a Katsina ake hawa motar Bauchi kuma tunda safe suke tafiya"


Ya mikoman kudi masu yawa yace;"gashi nan ki rike nayi shawara natafi Yau saboda jiya wani abokina da muka hadu kasuwa yake fadiman akwai mota daga Katsina har Bauchi kuma cikin saukin kudi dan na gwammanati ne shine zanje ya kaini, kinga nasamu sauki basai nayi wahalar tafiyar nan ba daga nan sai naje Kano kana na hau ta Bauchi ga mota cin basu da kyau amman yace man ita wannan motar bamu ko kwana, kinga kenan bazan dadeba kila iyakata kwana ukku saboda banson na dade saboda ke, ki kula kinji"


Nace;"to Baba Allah ya tsare adawo lafiya, zan kiyaye insha Allah, kuma daman inason in tambaye  ka zan dan je gidan Shatu da yake mijin ta bainan yana bida birni"

Yace;"yawwa nima hankali na zai kwanta amman kidinga dawowa nan kina kwana kuma kada ki kai dare"

Cikin jindadi nace;"to Baba adawo lafiya"

Ansar jikar hannun shi nayi, dakin Inna ya nufa ya kwan kwasa ta fito.

Ta kalleni duk da akwai duhu amman akwai fitila hannun Baba.


Bata ce mashi komi ba saide tayi tsaye a bakin kofar dakin.

Ma'u ma tafito tana murzar ido tace;"Baba lafiya de?"

Yace;"lafiya kalau Ma'u tafiya zanyi Bauchi...."

Bai karasa zancen ba Inna tace;"Bauchi! Wallahi na tsani garin nan saboda yarinyar nan yanzun ma Bauchin zaka kara zuwa ka kwaso mana wasu shaggun?"


Kallon ta yayi cikin mamaki yace;"su waye shaggu Rakiya?"

Kai tsaye ta nunani tace;"ai daga zuwa Bauchin ne ka kwaso ta yanzu wasu zaka kwaso"sai ta aza kuka tace;"wallahi Malam kwara ka fadaman gaskiya idan kanada mata a garin nan akan kaita zuwa kana kwaso man yara"shiru yayi.


Yace;"Amman Rakiya kinban mamaki waike wace irin mace ce? Mijin ki yazo yana fada maki zaiyi tafiya mai nisa irin wannan amman kina maganar kada kwaso maki shaggu, idan inada wata matar zan dauki dogon lokaci banje ba?? nide nazo ne dan na rokeki kada ki azabtar da Nana Allah yana kallon ki, kisani shi Dah idan ka rike shi bakasan mi zaya zama ba kilama itace zata rikeki, kila itace zataji kanki, dan haka kiji tsoron Allah kwana ukku zanyi ga Amana nan nabar ki, ke yanzu bai isheki ishara ba yadda kikaso ki dur kusar da rayuwar ta da auren Lado sai Allah yayi mata sakayya wannan ya isa ya zama darasi a gareki"

Daga haka ya juya mukabi bayan shi nida Ma'u.


Muna zuwa muka gaida abokin shi malam Habu dake bisa babur dinshi yana jiran Baba.


Yace;"Ma'u banson yawon nan naki ki kula kinji duk da ita 'yar uwar ki banjin ta nasanta da tsantseni da kula kuma kema inason ki kula da mutumcin ki kinji"


Tace;"insha Allah Baba zan kula sai ka dawo Lado yace zaizo jin magana Allah ya kiyaye Baba"

Baiyi magana sai girgiza kai yayi.

Jin yadda mahaifina ke yabo na akan zan kula da mutunci na yasa nayi sumar tsaye hawaye suka taru ido lalle idan yasan abinda ke faruwa akwai matsala nayi sake wani wanda bansani ba ya bataman rayuwa.


Malam Habu ya tada mashin jin banyi magana ba yasa Baba haskani yace;"lafiya zan wuce Nana"

Ganin hawaye yasa yace;"kiyi hakuri kinji saboda ke bazan dade ba ga 'yar uwar ki nan ku hada kanku kinji kuyi hakuri insha Allah zan dawo"


Hannuna Ma'u ta rike tace;"kibar kuka kinji yace bazai dade ba"

Daga kai nayi na daga mashi hannu.

A haka mukai ta daga mashi hannu har ya wuce muka koma cikin gida Ma'u tayi dakin Inna ni kuma nayi daki.


Kasa komawa bacci nayi na zauna bakin gado Ina shafa ciki na inajin motsi a cikin.


Kuka nike sosai tare da rokon Allah yayi man mafita gashi cikin nawa ya fara kun burowa da alama kato zaiyi.


Kullun Baba yana yabona yanzu idan yasan haka ya zanyi??


Shiru nayi inatunanin yanzu mizan fadima shi inama tun farko na fada mashi da yanzu an wuce wajen.


Amman kuma idan dana fadi mashi ai bansan ya zai dauki al'amarin Allah de ya kawoman Mafita.



           ***************

Tunda safe Kursim ta shirya taje tayima Momy ban kwana.


Hadda Daddy saida ya fito yana mata godiya akan kokarin da tayi sannan ya bata Dollars tana ta godiya haka wajen Momy tasha kyau.


Daddy ne yace baiga Anuwar ba ya kamata ace zai mata rakiya har airport itade Momy bata ce komi.


Ya kalli Kursim yace;"Kulsoom kije dakin shi kitado shi bai kamata ace kin wuce bakuyi ban kwana ba, duk dade kin kusa zama 'yar gida"


Tace;"to Daddy"cikin jin dadi tayi part din shi.


Yaune rana ta farko data shiga parlon shi.

Tunda take a rayuwar bata taba ganin parlon da yayi tsaruwar na Anuwar ba wannan wane irin sone Daddy kema Anuwar lalle idan ta samu shiga wajen Daddy ta gama komi.


Yanayin yadda akayi ginin benen dake cikin parlon shi kanshi abin kallo ne dan bata taba ganin kalar shi.


A hankali take takawa har ta hau sama inda ta iske wani kayatattacen parlon shida na farko ma ta rasa wane yafi kyau.


Kallon wajen take tanaso ganin inazata nufa duk ta rikice.


Sai chan ta hango wani korido tayi chan shikan shi wajen an hada shi da wani kalar kujeru kamar na kaba tana ganin su a Indian Fim jikin su hadda katifa kamar gado kamar kujeru haka suke.


Mamaki take taya za'ayi ace duk wannan wajen na mutum guda ne ace mutum guda ke rayuwa lalle antakura mashi zaman shi na Katsina.



Ganin kofofi yasa ta tura guda a hankali bedroom ne amman babu kowa sai wani kalar gado da yaune rana ta farko da ta taba ganin kalar gadon ko a fina finen kasashen waje bata taba ganin waje mai haduwar part din Anuwar ba.


Dakin dake kallon ta ta tura a hankali ta ganshi kwance yana bacci hankalin shi kwance cikin bargo.


Shiga tayi ta rasa miza tayi mashi.


Aje dan karamin gyalen ta tayi da hand bag dinta.

A hankali ta hau gadon ya dan motsa saita kame.

Ganin ya ciga da baccin yasa ta daga bargon ta tura kafafun ta ta matsa kusa dashi na rungume shi ta baya tana shafar bayan shi.


A hankali ya bude idon shi ganin abun yake kamar a mafarki wanene zai shigo mashi daki hadda rungume shi ta baya.



Wannan ai kalar rumgumar Manal ne ita ke rungume ni ta baya idan tana so intashi.


Murmushi yayi kila Nifra ce.


Kara lumshe ido yayi yana tuna yadda take mashi.


A guje take shigowa idan ta tashi bai tashi ba sai ta rungume bayan shi tare da manna mashi kiss tana dariya dole inde ta tashi to shima sai ya tashi.


Murmushi yayi yana taba kumatun shi.


Jin shiru yasa Kursim kara rungume shi jitake inama ace an daura auren ta zama matar shi.


Ganin time yana tafiya yasa tayi saurin tashi.


Jin an sake shi yasa yace;"Nifra ki gyale dan Allah bacci nikeji bana son takura bana hanaki shigoman part ba, idan kika kara saina maki duka kuma nace maki banason ana shigo man bedroom ko, wannan shine last warning idan kika kara nidake"

Daga haka ya kara lumshe ido yana matse filo a kirjin shi yana murmushi.


Ganin haka yasa Kursim tashi taje ta dauki gyalen ta da hang bag dinta tayi waje.

Kenan ita bata da matsayin da zai tunata yadda yake maganar kamar yasha wani abu ko har yanzu yana shan giya??


Wata zuciyar tace mata giyar soyayya ko.


Tana isa Daddy yace;" ankira wayar ki baki dauka ba kada jirgi ya tashi nasan Anuwar ne yakeson shiriritar dake kila yace sai yayi wanka ai kwara da kika baro shi"


Nan suka rakota har wajen mota ta tafi.


A dakin Anuwar yana koma bacci banda mafarki babu abinda yake sai mafarkin ya sake kusan mai fura.


Cikin sauri ya bude ido tare da addu'a a bakin shi.


Tashi yayi yaje yayi wankan tsarki kana yayi wanka ya dawo.


A bakin gado ya zauna yayi tagumi shiyama rasa gane kanshi.


Shin sha'awar karamar yarinyar nan yake koko sonta yake ko tausayin ta??


Yarinya tasa shi ya koma yin wankan tsarki duk yayi bacci.


A main parlo ya sabko ya iske Nifra kwance tana Chart yau kamar kullon kananun kaya ne a jikin ta.


Ita kade ce a parlon jin kamshin turaren shi yasa ta dago ta ganshi zaune yana latsar wayar shi.


Tace;"Yaya Anuu yaushe kashigo banji ko takon tafiyar kaba saide kamshin ka yasa nasan ka iso, mikake so nayi maka order kaci" tana maganar tana kallon shi.


A hankali yace;"No need, i need to see My Papa"wani lokaci yana kiran Daddy da Papa saboda haka MANAL ke cewa to duk ya ambaci haka akwai matsala yana son yayi maganar MANAL.


Murmushi tayi tace;"Let me call him"

Yace;"okay, Thank you".


Da wayar ta ta kirashi bai dauka ba tana ta Kira amman saide a latse wayar.

Ganin haka tace ya kira shi da wayar shi.

Yana kira ko ya dauka yace;"Anuu mikake so katashi kenan?"

Yace;"yes Dad i need to see you"

Cikin sauri yace;"Am coming now"daga haka Anuu ya kashe waya yayi zaman jiran Dad.


Ba sufi ten minute ba saiga Dad ya shigo.


Cikin sauri Anuwar ya mike yaje wurin da Dad din.


Kama hannun shi yayi yace;"fadaman mike damun ka ina meeting ka Kira amman nace su bari mu ida gobe lafiyar ka kalau de ko?"

Bata fuska yayi yace;"Eh Dad kawai inason na fita kuma nasan baza'a barni naje nikade shine nikeso Dan Allah kabar ni nafita nikade"


Kallon shi yake cikin mamaki.

Yace;"Anuu dina wai miyasa duk ka chanza yanzu kafison zama karshen gari inda ba mutane amman nanfa ba Katsina bane kuma ba Spain bane yanzu idan ka fita kai kade yan jarida kade sun isheka dabi bayadda za'a yi kafita kai kade"


Bata fuska yayi baice komi ba Daddy yace;"idan kana bukatar zama bayan gari Mai martaba yace a Bauchi zaku zauna da Kursim sai kunyi wasu watanni kana yabar ka kabar kasar yau zansa aduba katon fili mai kyau wajen karshen gari a Bauchi a gina maka lambu wajen kada kadamu kaga zuwa anjima zaku bar Abuja kilani sai zuwa next idan abubuwa sunyi sauki in iso kayi hakuri Anuu dina dole na kare rayuwar ka, kodan tsoron kada a maimaitaman 'yar gidan jiya"


Ganin yadda yanayin Dad ya sauya yasa yace;"bakomi Dad zanje nasa a shiryaman abinda nikeso"


Yace;"No Anuu kaima ai nasa ammaka laifen ka na kishin laifi"

Yana maganar yana murmushi shima Anuwar murmushi daga haka yayi gaba.

Nifra dake kallon su tace;"why don't picking my call Dad?"dariya yayi yace;

"Nasan kiran naki bai wuci kice zaki wajen Friend dinkiba shiyasa na jira in fito ko kema kina bukatar laifen ne?"

Yayi maganar yana murmushi dariya tayi tace;"Eh Dad kaga daman inason naje Dubai nayi Shopping din kayan da zan saka bikin Yaya Anu nida su Walida"


Kama baki Dad yayi yana cewa;"sarkin 'yan yawo ba kinje Shopping ba a Spain keda Kursim ba"

Cikin shagwaba take maagana tana cewa;"please Dad"tana buga kafafu.

Dad yace;"ki tambayi Brother dinki idan yabar ku sai kuje keda Walida amman two days zakuyi saboda bikin".

Tsalle tayi tace;"thank you Dad"


A guje ta isa dakin Anuwar yana sama bedroom dinshi ta shiga cikin murna da tsalle.


A dakin Anuwar yana tsaye a bakin window saide yaji kaf an bude dakin.


Yasan Nifra ce shiyasa bai waigo ba.


Cikin sauri ta rungume shi tace;"please Yaya Anuu kabar ni nida Walida muje Dubai please don't say No"rike hannunta yayi yace;"Sakar ni sarakan yawo kuma ku kade zakuje?"

Tace;"a'a da friend din Momy zamuje"


Ya cireta daga jikin shi yace;"sai kundawo banda rawar kai"


Ta fita tana ihu tana tsalle.


Dawowa yayi  bakin gado ya zauna.

Nifra yarinya ce amman miyasa ita bata burgeshi??

Kodan ya dauke ta kamar Walida?

To miyasa ita kullun tana cikin mafarkin shi??

Kenan tausayin ta yake?


Idan yana tunanin ta yana mantawa da komi.



Zama yayi ya dafe kai yana tunanin taya zai iya rayuwa idan baigan taba??


            *************

Wanka nayi nasaka kayana na dauki Hijab nida naje nace ma Inna zan fita bata ko kalleni ba hakan yasa na wuce.


Ina zuwa yau ta baya nashiga gidan Shatu da yake bayan dajine babu Danni.


Kofar dakinta a bude hakan yasa na tura na shiga.


Tana zaune tana damun fura jin sallama na yasa ta dago tace;"yanzu nike shirin aika maki ince kitafo na dafa maki taliyar leda"


Da kyat na isa jin ta kawai nike amman har duhu nike gani, bisa kafitar dake tsakar dakin ta na fada ina kuka.


Shigowa tayi tace;"lafiya de ko miyafaru?"

Nace;"wallahi idan Baba yasan da ciki a jikina bansan ya zaiyi ba Baba na ya yadda dani Shatu fiye da yadda kike tunani, yana alfari dani da kalar tarbiya ta inama ace na fada mashi tun farko ya zanyi ki taimaka ga kudi muje idan akwai yadda za'ayi nide azubdaman dashi"


A jiyar zuciya tayi ta goge hawayen da suka zubo mata tace;"a kwai hadari wallahi zaki iya rasa ranki ina tsoro na fada maki da kunne na naji 'yan matan tudu na maganar, yanzu kinsan wani Abu?"

Na girgiza kai tace;"na taba jin kamar ance ganyen bedi na zubda ciki idan aka daka shi sai a tsaye ruwan asha amman fa banda tabbas"

Cikin sauri nace;"nide muje mu gwada"

Tace;"Babu tabbas abinda yafi mutafi wajen Iya zaifi, kila tasan wani Abu"

Cikin sauri nace;"a'a muje musamu bedin nide nasan Iya bazata barmu na zubda cikin nan ba, kinga haka zata ce ya fara girma"

Shiru Shatu tayi tace;"kuma haka ne fa, to amman banda tabbas kada kisha bedi azo ba haka bane kinga babu tabbas kada ya taba lafiyar jikin ki kona yaron ki"


Nace;"nide muje na tsinko ko mutuwa zanyi bazan iya zama na haife wannan cikin ba banson Uban D'aba sannan ace ni shegiya sannan na haifi shege Shatu ki tausayaman wallahi zanyi iya karfina saina zudda shi"


Shatu cikin sauri ta rike tace;"bamu san abinda Allah zai yiba Nana ina hakurin naki da juriya akan wahala duk suna Ina? Ya kamata na baki karfin gwuiwa ki chinye wannan jarabawar kada ki zubda cikin nan kilama Alheri ne a rayuwar ki kila shine sanadin hutun ki kada kifara".


Fizge hannu na nayi nayi waje ina kuka.

Nanufi wajen wani katon iccen bedi da ke bayan gidan su Shatu



Qurrah

DEDICATED TO....MY SISTERS😘


Page-41-42




----------------Cikin wani irin tashin hankali ya farka daga baccin da yake.

Ya rike kanshi daya ke sarawa ga zufa dake zubo mashi duk sanyin A-C dake cikin dakin.


A hankali ya furta;"mike samun wannan yarinyar? A kwai matsala kenan?".

Kasa komawa bacci yayi yatashi yayo wanka yayi alwala yazo ya fara sallah daya gaji yafara karatun Al-qura'ani.


           ***************

Tana isowa ta shakeni tace;" fadaman gaskiya ciki ne dake?".


Na girgiza kai nace;"wallahi Inna wani abu naci gidan Shatu shine ya kumburan man da ciki shine yasa cikina murdawa".

Shiru tayi tana kallo na haskani take karayi sai tayi murmushi ta fita.


Ma'u tace;"Inna baza'a kira Mai Amalanken ba? tanajin jiki"kallon ta Inna tayi tayi tsoki tabar wajen.

Ma'u tazo ta kamani ta maidani bisa gado.


Cikina kara juyawa yake haka nayita kwallah Ma'u naban baki sai chan da aka kusa asuba kana bacci ya daukeni.



         **************

Shagalta yayi dayima yarinyar nan addu'ar sosai har saida akayi asuba kana ya tofa ya tashi yayi masallaci.


Babu abinda yake a cikin sujadar shi inba addu'a ga yarinyar da yayi refing dinta.


Ana gamawa ya mikama Mai martaba hannu suka gaisa yayi part din shi.


Bacci yake son yi amman daya kulle ido sai yaji kukan ta.


Tashi yayi ya zauna tare da yin tagumi duka hannuwa biyu.




          *************

Sai da safe kana na farka na mike naje nayo alwala na dawo nayi sallah jikina zafi hakan yasa na koma gado na kwanta.


Ina komawa sai bacci mai nauyi ya daukeni.

Cikin baccin naji haya niyya ga kamshi Mai dadi har cikin dakina.

Nace bari naga suwaye kamshin yaman dadi sai kara shaka nike ina lumshe ido.

Tashi nayi nayi waje ganin takalma masu kyau kofar dakin Inna yasa na saki baki dan bantaba ganin kalar suba.


Kamshin dana fito waje yafi yawa hakan yasa nayi wajen dakin Inna Amman da mamaki an turo kauren dakin ta.




Jin kamshin yafi yawa wajen dakin ta yasa na matsa jin kamshin nike kamar abinci saboda yadda nikejin dadin kamshin







Hancina nakai wajen kauren ina sun sunar kamshin sainaji anfara magana

Na girgiza kai nace;"Babu kyau labe bari nabar wurin kada Inna taji motsina"

Har na juya naji sunce;"Muna son idan anzo neman mai furar nan kuce ta mutu".


Ido na zaro wace mai fura kenan hakan yasa na dawo.


Inna naji tace;"ku suwaye kuma mike tsakanin ku da ita? diyar mijina ce ko sata tayi a birni dan zata aika"


Daya daga cikin su tace;"bake kika haifeta ba kenan?".

Cikin sauri Inna tace;"Allah ya kyauta idan ma laifi tayi ku dauketa ku tafi daman ta tar watsaman farin ciki gida".


Jin wannan maganar yasa zukatan su yin sanyi bukatar su zatazo da sauki.


Kudi Hanan ta fiddo ta aje gefen Inna tace;"duk kudin nan nakine inde zakiyi mana Abu guda daya tak".

Cikin sauri Inna tace;"wallahi komi kukace inyi zanyi akan mayyar yarinyar nan gatanan ta hana diyata farin ciki, tama diyata asiri wallahi komi kukace zanyi".


Murmushi sukayi Hanan tace;"za'a zo neman diyar ki" sai kuma tace;"yi hakuri namanta diyar mijin ki, idan sukazo duk abinda suka baki kada ki amsa zamuzo mu baki wadda yafishi idan sukace kece mahaifiyar ta kice eh ki amsa masu cikin jimami ina nufin hadda 'yan hawaye mukeso idan kinada saurin kuka, idan suka tambaye ki ba'asi kice ta mutu" 

Cikin sauri Inna da Kursim suka kalli Hanan.


Kursim tace;"zasu tambaye ta miya kasheta".

Banji maganar da dayar tayi ba saboda muryar ta bata da karfi.

Murmushi Hanan tayi ta kalli Inna tace;"miza kice masu hakan zaisa nasan zaki iya wannan aikin, nasan kina bukatar kudi to bari kiji idan aiki yayi kyau kason ki saiyafi haka".

Cikin sauri Inna tace;"zance taje icce a daji maciji ya sareta ta mutu, dan ko diyar makwabtan mu haka ya faru da ita kinsan yaran mu na zuwa daji icce".


Murmushi su duka sukayi;"Hanan tace Amman gaskiya na yarda baki sonta hakan zaisa aikin mu yayi kyau to shikenan zamu tafi".


Babu abinda ke fitowa daga idona sai hawaye.


Har sun mike Kursim tace;"mijin ki ya rasune?".

Inna tace;"a'a yayi tafiya kila gobe ya dawo ko jibi badai zaiyi sati ba, kuma idan yana nan wannan aikin bazai yiyuba".


Su duka suka kalli juna Hanan tace;"kila suzo yau, insha Allah zamuyi nasara".

Jin zasu fito yasa zanyi baya naji Inna nacewa;"Amman baku fadiman laifin da tayi maku ba har za'azo neman ta".

Hanan tace;"kada kidamu wannan tsakanin mune kide kiyi aikin ki zamu fada maki amman sai mun dawo lokacin idan aiki yayi kyau amman babu tabbas din mu zamu dawo saboda yanzu muna cikin hidima Amman de nayi maki alkawarin kara maki kudin da sukafi na hannunki kila aikowa zanyi".

Inna tace;"ba komi nide inajiran Kudi".

Hanan tace;"kada ki damu"jin zasu fito yasa nayi daki da gudu.

Na zauna nakasa tuna komi su waye wadannan mutan nan??


Zufa nike sosai akan mi inada rai za'ace na mutu, waya turosu??


Haka nayita sabbatu har rana tayi hankali na ya tashi.


Jin ana kiran sunana yasa na fito.

Ma'u ce tsaye da wani yaro da sabuwar samira akan shi.


Yace;"Shatu tace akawo ma Nana".

Gani yasa Ma'u tace;"wai Shatu tace akawo maki injide ba irin na jiya bane?"

Nace;"Bari na duba ina Inna?"


Na amshi Samirar na bude cikin mamaki nike kallon samirar kaza ce ciki.


Nace;"kace Nagode kaji bari na baka"

Yaron yace a'a nayi-nayi ya ki amsa wai tace idan ya amsa bazata dan mashi ba idan ya koma, kuma zata gane cikin kudina naje na dauko mashi Muttala na bashi.


Nace;"Ma'u muci mana".

Tace;"Allah yasa Inna batanan tun da nadawo ban isketa ba kila tayi wajen yawon ta".

Tace;"nikam saide ki dan man tunda baki da lafiya".

Nace;"saide ki iyaba"

Tasa hannu ta dauki kadan nace ta kara.


Na zuba mata ganin bata dauki da yawa nayi daki miyau na har gudawa yayi na fara ci rabon da naci kaza ai har na manta 

Inachi amman babu dadi nakasa gane wake keson ace na mutu minene alakata dasu, hakan yasa banwani ciba saboda yadda gabana ke faduwa

             ************


Part din Momy yayi na cikin gidan su.

Jallabiya ce a jikin shi Milk daka ganshi kasan akwai matsala a tare dashi

A parlour dinta ya zauna masuyi mata hidima suka gaida shi baiko kalle su ba sukabar wajen yana nan zaune sai gata ta fito.


Tace;"lafiya nagan ka haka kamar ba ango ba gasu Walida da Nifra nan zasu wuce Dubai shine mukayi bankwana sai anfara biki zasu dawo sai Monday kenan, amman kai kana Abu kamar ba ango ba".

Jin bazata tsaya ba yace;"please Momy kiyi wani abu mana banfa yi bacci ba yarinyar nan cikin baccina tazo tanaman kuka tana cewa na taimaka mata, Momy wallahi kukan ta jinshi nike cikin kai jin shi nike tamkar na MANAL kinsan na tsani kukan mace".


Ya sabko daga bisa kujera yazo inda Momy take tayi mutuwar zaune ko motsi ta kasa ya kama hannunta yace;

"Momy idan bakiyi wani Abu ba a yau to zan batama iyayena zan gudu naje neman yarinyar nan bazan iya cigaba da rayuwa ba idan bata nan"


Ganin hawaye sun zobama Momyn yasa yace;"Momy ki yadda ba Aljana bace wallahi mutum ce ina kaunar ta na bata mata rayuwa Kuma karamar yarinyar idan banji kanta ba Allah bazai barni ba".

Momy ta goge hawayen ta tace;"na yadda mutum ce kuma na maka alkawarin zan kawo maka ita inde tana duniya insha Allah yanzu zansa ane mota".


Wayar ta ta dauka;"Hello Hajiya Fatima inason zanyi magana da Mai gidan ki yanzu idan da yana nan"


Wayar tana hannun Momy yana Jin abinda ake fadi tace;"Allah ya taimaki first Lady yanzu gashi ma yana gida".


Cikin girmamawa ya gaidata tace;"tanason suyi mata aiki cikin awannin nan zata aiko mutanen ta aje dasu, nan ta fada mashi inda suke zama tace tanason agano sunan garin su yanzu".


Cikin girmamawa yace angama yanzu zakiji komi game da ita.

Sukayi ban kwana ta aje waya.

Tace;"Kaji yanzu za'ayi komi zamusan sunan garin su sannan zuwa gobe zan tura amintattun mutane suje gidan gobe".


Murmushi yayi yace;"thank you so much Momy"murmushi tayi tana shafa sumar kanshi.


Ya danji sauki a ranshi kenan ko gobe ana iya tafowa da ita.


A dakin shi ya koma yayi wanka ya shirya ya fito.


Kamar kullun yana fitowa akayi busar ya fito.


Hakan yasa gidan ko ina kamshi ke tashi.


Hankalin shi a kwance yaje dakiunan abokan zaman Ammie suka gaisa ya wuce part din Mai martaba dan yasan zai iske Ammi achan.


Yana shiga Ammie tayi murmushi tace;"Ango ne a part dinmu sai kamshi kake"


Murmushi yayi ya isa inda suke suka gaisa.


Nan Mai martaba ke bata labarin Yarima ya koma san fura.

Mamaki take tace;"lalle kam ashe Iya Rabi ta shirya kenan"


Mai martaba yace;"ai ya huta dan Baban nashi kusa da garin su yasa ake Gina mashi Lambu gashi ga mai furar".

Dariya sukayi suna 
mashi dariya.

Kama hannun shi Mai martaba yayi yace;"Subhanallah! Yarima ina zoben ka na sarautar gidan nan banace kada ki cire ba idan ya maka kadan za'a kaishi a buda maka kasan Darajar shi kuwa?"

Kallon mahaifin sa yake ya lumshe ido ya tuna lokacin da yake saka matashi.


Mai martaba yace;"kayi magana mana"

            **************


Ina zaune saiga Ma'u ta kawo man wani tuwo tace;"ita ta gamashi yanzu na boye kada Inna ta gani"nace to na amsa na aje.


Ana sallar magriba naji shigowar Inna.


Na fito nayi alwala na koma daki nayi sallah ina nan zaune har akayi isha'i nayi na koma na kwanta nama manta da wani tuwo.


Sai da dare yayi kana naji yunwa na tashi naje na wanke hannu na dawo na dauki tuwon.


Ina bude langar naji banson kamshin miyar nade daure saboda yau kazar nan kade naci kuma rabawa dai-dai nayi naba Ma'u.


Haka naita tura tuwan saida naci sosai kana naje na wanke hannu.

Ga mamaki na sainaga haske dakin Inna gashi dare yayi sosai.


Daki na koma cikin raina nace komi Inna keyi haryanzu ohow?


Na koma na kwanta ina kwanciya naji cikina na juyawa zuciyata na tashi.


Bakina babu dadi abincin sai tukata yake.


Jin amai yazoman lokaci guda yasa nayi waje a guje.


Nan fa nafara kwaraya amai har saida na amayar da komi na cikina.


Ina dago zan samo buta na kuskure bakina naga Inna tsaye bakin kofa.


Tace;"Yau Allah ya toni asirin ki munafuka Yau gaskiya tayi halinta bakisan kyaleki nayi ba kada na kaiki gaban mai gari a duba aga ba ciki ace kazafi nayi maki daman banson ki".


Na mike na fara ja da baya.

Tace;"wallahi bazan barki ba ki bata Mana gari kila Bana aki ruwa saboda ke wallahi yanzu ko gidan Mai gari sunyi bacci sai sun tashi wallahi yau sai kinbar duniya bakar munafuka mai raba mata da miji sannan ki raba Uwa da diya, yau nasan mutanan garin nan sai sun jefeki daman bakisan saida akayi tashin hankali kana suka yadda kika zauna ba saida Ubanki ya rantse yace ke ba 'yar zina bace kana suka yarda kika zauna to yau asirin ki ya tonu idan ke ba 'yar zina bace to zaki haifi dan Zina kinga kuma garin nan ba shege".


Guda tayi tareda shewa.


Ma'u dake tsaye tace;"Inna amman kinsan korarta za'ayi kuma Baba yace yabaki Amana kullun idan muna karatu Baba saiya maimaita mana muhimmancin Amana sannan Hadisin da nike yana magana akan duk Wanda ya rufe asirin dan Uwanshi shima Allah....."


Wata fanteka  ta dauka ta jefama Ma'u hakan yasa bata ida maganar taba.

Tace;"wallahi zan iya tsine maki akan wannan yarinyar kinsan ta dalilinta bakin cikin dana shiga shekara tara kenan ina Kumar bakin ciki yau itace ranar da tabon bakin cikina zai goge"


Cikin sauri ta bankade Ma'u ta dauko mayafi tace;"ki jirani kiga sakamako" ta juya ta fita cikin sauri.


Ma'u tayi saurin isowa inda nike ta hawaye.

Tace;"bakida hankali zaki tsaya baki nemi mafita ba, yan kauyan nan wallahi jahilayya ne kuma kinsani mune masu ilimin su zasu iya komi, idan aka fara bugun ki saikin suma kana a koreki a garin kiyi sauri ki gudu, akwai wasu Kudi danaga Inna na kirgawa tun kamin inyi bacci Inna ke kirgasu har yanzu sunanan bari na debo maki, kije wani gari sai Baba ya dawo ko idan kin haihu sai kidawo saiki ce sharri Inna tayi maki shiyasa kika gudu zanji da Baba".

Ina kuka nace;"Ma'u nagaji da rayuwar nan su kasheni bama koraba idan suna iyawa su kasheni nagaji tunda na tashi nike cikin kunci da wahala ina rokon Allah yasa haka shiyafi Alkhairi"


A guje Ma'u taje daki saigata ta fito rike da kudi dakina ta shiga.

Ta dauko buhuna tace;ga kayan ki Bari na daukar maki kala daya masu kyau dan duk sun zama tsumma wahala kawai z daukar su zai zama"


Kallon ta nike ina hawaye zuciyata ta riga da ta bushe naji zan dauki mutuwar nagaji da wannan rayuwar bazan zan taba hutawa ba.

Ni kuma haka tawa Jarabtar take.


Jin kara yasa na maida hankali.


Dan kwankwanin dana saka zoben da mutunan nan ya bani ya fado daga buhun sai haske yake.


Dauka tayi tace;"minenen wanannan Nana?"

Nayi saurin amsa ina kuka jin hayaniyya da haske yasa tace;"mun shiga ukku Inna tana iya komi yanzu haka ihu tayi kowa ya tashi mutanan kauyan nan ne Nana ki gudu kibar kayan ga Kudi"


Taja hannuna tana kuka na fizge.


Nace;"kila mutuwa ta zatasa Inna farin ciki su kasheni babu inda zani"


Jin hayaniyyar ta doso gidan mu yasa na kara damke akwatin da zoben yake hannu na tare da fidda wasu zafafan hawaye masu zafi inajiran mutuwa ta

Page_43-44

 _Bissimillah Rahmanir_ _Rahim_



--------------Hannu na ta fizga saida nayi kamar zan fadi tayi hanyar bandaki dani.

Tace; "Idan har Baba yana da amfani a rayuwar ki to ki gudu idan kuma baida amfani ki tsaya su halaka ki, kinga sai ki saka Baban mu cikin wani hali kibar shi da kunci, kuma hakan zaisa na tsane ki a matsayin ki na 'yar uwata, saboda Uwata ce silar halaka ki  mahaifina kuma zai tsaneta idan hakan ta faru ba zai taba ya femata ba, idon Inna ya rufe amman naki a bude yake saboda ke kina da ilimi, ki ceci rayuwar gidan nan ki gudu kada tayi sadaniyar halaka ki, idan kika tsaya shikenan zaki silar tarwatsa mana rayuwa dan idan suka kashe ki mahaifina bazai kara zama da Inna ba kuma akwai zuri'a a tsakani".


Kalaman Ma'u sun karyaman zuciya jin hayaniyyar ta matso yasa tace; "ga kudin mota kiyi nisa damu ta yadda ba ɗan kauyen nan da zai ganki, har sai kin haihu sannan kidawo zanji da Baba".


Na amshi kudin Ina kuka na rike hannun Ma'u itama kuka take.


Na kama katangar ban ɗakin mu na dira daman karshen gari muke ina dirawa sai daji.


Banko waiga ba, nayi gaba da gudu ina kuka saboda yadda garin ko ina hasken fitila.


Cikin daji nike gashi tsakar dare gashi babu fitila sai addu'ar dake bakina inayi ina neman tsari.




               **********


Mai Martaba tsare shi yayi da idanu yana kallon shi.


Kallon hannun shi yake babu zoben sai murmushi yake.

Ammie tace; "ko Kulsoom kaba zoben shiyasa a gaban mu ka shiga shauƙin so?"

Shiru yayi da Mai Martaba da Ammie suka kalli juna sukayi murmushi.

Mai martaba yace;"to ai idan itace babu damuwa duk da zoben Mai daraja ne amman babu matsala ai itama ta shigo cikin mu duk randa ta haifa mana jika sai ya zama mallakin shi, kaga kenan na gada daga kakan nina, babban jikana shima zai gada, ina alfahari da haka amman fa kace ta kula dashi sosai".


Murmushi yayi ya mike yace;"Abbie Ammie bacci nakeji".

Nan sukayi bankwana Prince ya fita zuciyar shi fal da farin ciki.


A part din shi ya shiga yana zuwa an hada mashi ruwan wanka kamar kullun ya shige cikin bahon wankan ya lumshe ido yace;


"Lalle Abie zaka samu jika daga gareta nan kusa dan gobe za'a kawota sai a daura rana daya da Kursim"

Lumshe ido yake yana kara shigewa cikin ruwan masu kamshi yana murmushi jin shi yake tamkar tana bisa kirjin shi.


Ya dauki kusan awa ukku cikin shauki da tunani iri-iri na yadda zai maidata Queen.


Cikin sauri ya fito yana goge jikin shi yana murmushin sleeping dress din dake bisa bed din shi ya dauka ya saka yazo bakin window yana kallon samaniyya.


Jin shi yake tamkar yaune ranar da zai angon ce.


Har saida dare ya raba sannan ya hakura ya dawo ya kwanta.


Filo yasa a kirjin shi yana shafawa tamkar ita haka yake jin shi, sai faman kara rungume shi yake yana murmushi.



        ** ***************


Ina tafiya a wahalce har nakai tudu ina zuwa garin tsit babu ko motsi na wuce nayi hanyar birni.


Ina tafiya ina hawaye babu ko tsoro a idona, ko kaɗan saboda yadda duniyar ta fita raina sai addu'ar kariya da nike daga sheɗanu.


Koda na iso Batagarawa nan ma ba mutane na wuce na cigaba da tafiya saida na iso barandar mu sannan na zauna na jingina da bango.


Haka nayi zaune ina hawaye ina kara makyalewa a bango duk da ba motsi sai an dauki lokaci mota ke wucewa.



Ina nan zaune safiya ta fara waye wa.


Ganin gari ya fara haske yasa nayi saurin mikewa kada cikin 'yan garin mu wani yazo.


Nabi titi ina tafiya Yau itace rana ta farko da kafata zata shiga birni.


Jikina sai kyarma yake ina kalle-kalle har na iso wajen wasu gida je masu yawa kala daya.


Sakin baki nayi na tsaya Ina kallon su.

Karar Babur ya maidoni daga shagalta ina kallon gidajen.


Mai Babur ne yace;"Yarinya tafiya zaki yi kizo na wuce dake wajen nan yana  wahalar samun abun hawa da safe, idan kika ce zaki jira Napep('yar kurkura) anan zaki dade baki samu ba"


Ban ma fahimci mi yake nufi ba to kawai nace na hau Babur din.


Yace;"Ina zamuje?"


Nace;"inda ake hawan motar Gwamnati zamu".


Yace;"daman tafiya zaki yi banganki da Kaya ba ai, bari nayi sauri yadda zaki samu motar farko"

Nidai bance komai ba saboda bansan yadda abin yake ba.

Muna zuwa naga mutane ma tafiya ana sabke ni wasu dake tsaye bakin tashar suka zo suna tambayar yarinya ina zaki? Da kyar nasamu nace Bauchi saboda tsoro ya kamani wani daga cikin su yace na bishi. 

Na bishi ina zuwa yace ga ciko na samo shikenan motar farko ta cika a bude ta biyu.

Na shiga akace ina kayana za'a daure nace banda Kaya.


Hakan yasa aka rufe mota tare da fita muka dauki hanya.

Nide na kasa natsuwa sai satar kallon motar nike saboda ban taɓa ganin irin taba.


Matar dake kusa dani tace;"Yarinya ina zaki haka tafiya mai nisa ba kayan sawa?? Kamar ma baki wanke fuska ba".


Na dukar dakai nace;"eh ina sauri ne domin na samu mota"

Tace;"kin koyi sa'a dan wajen wani ne har an rubuta sunan shi ya biya kudi yace bari ya dawo shiru bai dawo ba saida aka bada kayan shi ga mota ta biyu"

Nayi murmushi sai kara damke zobe na nike.


Haka akayita tafiya tun idona biyu har bacci ya daukeni



              **************

Yau koda ya tashi da wuri ya shirya ya kira Momy tace sun tafi karfe goma jirgin su zai sabka.


Ya zauna yana jiran jin mizai faru.


Cikin hadiman shi yasa yana zaune bisa Bed din shi daya na warware mashi kayan dazai saka na tarbar ta, kayan da Daddy yasai mashi ne sai yanzu ya buda su.


Kayan ne gasunan amman ya kasa zabar wanda zai saka idan zata zo.


Wayar shi ya dauka ya kira Jawad kanan shi.

Cikin mamaki Jawad ke gaida Anuwar bai tsaya amsawa ba yace;"inason ayiman wasu order kaya a yau nikeso su iso dan gobe zanyi amfani dasu kuma da safe".


Jawad yace;"baka ga kayan da Daddy yasa akayi maka order ba? Duk kayan dake a wadirof dinka sabbin zuwa ne dan wallahi bazaka same su anan ba"


Jin shiru ya aza ya kashe Jawad ya maimaita;"Hello"

Tsoki Anuwar yayi yace;"kagama naka surutun idan bazaka yiba nasa Usman"

Jawad yace;"abinda kakeso shi za'ayi zanyi masu magana sai aturo maka ka zaba a waya"


Murmushi yayi ya kashe wayar saura kuma aka maida.


Baifi ten minutes ba saiga shi sun shigo.

Wani Indian Prince Suit ne ya gani ya dade yana kallon su Nevy Blue wunyi kyau sosai suya zaba.


Yana turama Jawad su yakira shi.

Jawad na dariya yace;"to bari kaji wallahi wani Prince din India ne zaiyi aure shine fa sukayi su amman bari naji idan zasu bada kalar su, idan abin yafi karfina zan hada su da Daddyn ka"


Anuwar yace;"okay"


Jawad yace;"please Broth kada ka kashe dan Allah wacece wannan da zakayi ma kwalliya ko kana neman auren wata Princess ne a wata kasar?   Dan nasan wannan bana Kulsoom bane"


Kashe wayar shi yayi yana kallon kayan yasan zasuyi mashi kyau.


Lumshe ido yayi yana addu'ar Allah yasa ta soshi kada ace ta tsane shi saboda abinda yayi mata.


Kallon photunan MANAL yayi dake dakin yace;"sorry babe nayi maki alkawarin bazan so ko wace mace ba amman wannan dole na sota na cuceta da kananun shekaru nasan da kinada rai kema zaki tausaya Mata"


Daukar photon dake kusa dashi yayi nata ya manna a kirji yasa blanket ya rufe jikin shi tareda Lumshe ido kamar wanda ke bacci.


Babu abinda yake jira sai kiran Momy yaji antafo da ita.



             *************

Koda na farka har yanzu tafiya ake.

Wani gari muka tsaya ganin kowa ya fita nide nayi zaune.


Matar kusa dani tace;"Baki fitowa ki sayi abinci idan zaki zagaya ki zagaya daga nan ba'a kara tsayawa sai Bauchi dan mun wuto Kano kadan duk kina bacci"


Nace;"to"


Na fito na sayi ruwa na wanke fuskata na sayi awara na kara sayen ruwa na koma mota babu kowa suna waje saicii nike hannu baka hannu kwarya saboda inajin kunyar cin abinci cikin mutane.


Jin dadin awarar yasa na sabko nazo na kara lokacin sun Fara komawa.


Ina Zama saiga matar nan ta dawo tana bala'i wai abincin wurin tsada.

Na mika mata awarata nace gashi;"tace a'a nagode nasiyo da kwai da kaji kayan wurin nan tsada gwara da baki saya ba wallahi shekara goma sha ina saida abinci nasan yadda harkar take amman sun nan saboda sun rena ma mutane wayo kamar ka roko"


Ta mikoman kazar nace;"a'a tabar shi nagode"

Tace;"ke kada kidamu ai kamar uwa nike wajen ki dan nabaki abu ai bakomi bane kuma abun kusa da bakii"

Daman miyau na ya cinke dan kusan wata kenan kullun cikin son kajin nike.


Na amsa na fara chii kallona take.

Tace;"diyata mijin ki rasuwa yayi ne?"

Na girgiza kai nace;"banda miji ai"


Kallona take har saida taga kamar na tsargu kana tayi murmushi tace;"To yanzu Katsina kike ko Bauchi"

Nayi shiru ta dafani tace fadaman diyata kila na iya taimakon ki dan duk wata Unguwa dake a Bauchi nasan ta kila neman dangin ki kikazo ko?"


Nace;"bansan kowa ba a Bauchi kuma banida 'yan uwa sai Baba na yana Katsina".


Murmushi tayi tace;"kada kidamu gidana akwai wajen zama zan rikeki har ki haife abinda ke cikin ki"

Nayi saurin kallon ta ta girgiza daga kai alamar tabbatar wa.

Tace;"yara masu kalar matsalar ki nasha rigesu kada kidamu munan ba komi bane zakiyi rayuwar ki cikin jin dadi kinji"


Murmushi nayi nace;"Nagode"

Naji Dadi sosai shikenan basai naje wajen Inna Rabi ba kilama aje nema na har chan saboda munyi magana da Shatu kila ta fadama Baba ga inda zani yanzu ko na huta shikenan.



Hankali ya dan kwanta cikin ma kila ta zubdashi.


Hakan yasa naci na koshi sai bacci.


Tadani naji anayi na bude ido a hankali naga matar nan ce tace;"muje har mun iso"


Cikin sauri na bude ido ba kowa motar tace;"muje nima har na tari Mai Napep din da zai kaimu kayana na ciki"


Na fito ina fitowa zobena dake kan jikina ya fado.


Nayi saurin dukawa na dauka.

Ta rike hannu na har mukaje wajen abun hawa.

Sai kallon abinda muka hau nike.

Naita kallon gari tare da mamaki yadda yake da kyau har muka isa wata unguwa gidan a bakin titi yake.


Ta bude na dauko mata kayan ta tace;"a'a da kinbar su keda bake kade bace"


Kunya naji na dukar da kai a kasa.


Ta kama kayan ta shiga dasu.


Ta nunaman wani daki tace;"ga dakin ki nan, daman na kori wata ne saboda butulci tayi man hadda kayan sawa amman kila sunyi maki yawa sai nakai nan makwabta a rage maki"


Nace;"Nagode"


Tace;"Suna na Asabe mai abinci, amman suna kirana da Hajiya, minene sunan ki?"


Nace;"Nanah"

Tace;"zan maki duk abinda nikeso kamar diyata, amman fa duk abinda nasaki zakiyi"


Nace;"insha Allah zan maki biyayya"


Tace;"to bari nasa akawo maki abinci dan nasan ciki akwai takura, shiyasa ma idan hankali ya kwanta nan da kwana biyu idan kina so nakai ki wajen likitina ya rabaki da shi"


Cikin murna nace;"yawwa Hajiya wallahi daman banson na haihu saboda inason komawa gida wajen Baba".


Tayi murmushi tace;"idan  nayi maki wannan aikin ai nima ba tafiya zakiyi ba sai kin biya la'ada"

Bangane nufin taba hakan yasa nayi murmushi kawai na mike nanufi bandaki saboda yin alwala.


Na dawo na shiga dakin kallon dakin nike yayi kyau sosai hadda gado ga wajen saka kaya.


Rufin dakin mai kyau sosai ba kalar namu bane kasan ma bantaba ganin Shiba.


Nayi hamdala na saka Hijab naje inda abin sallah yake shinfide na tada Sallah.


Na gama biyan sallolina kana na linke abin sallah.


Naga abinci mai kyau ga kaji a ciki da ruwa a gefe.

Na zauna nacii na mike na fito.

Na iske wani yaro yana wanke-wanke


Nace;"Hajiya aida kin gyaleshi ni zanyi maki"

Tace;"Tafi ki huta ga gajiya atare dake, inada masu aikina ke aikin jawo customers amman ba yanzu ba saina rabaki da wannan abun na cikin ki kana".


Nace;"to nagode Allah ya saka da Alkhairi".


Na mike na koma daki na kwanta.

Na lumshe ido nace;"Allah na gode maka".

Jin motsin cikin yasa na aza hannu na bisa cibiyata.

Nace;"Shatu idan na rabu dashi ban maki aldalci amman kiyi hakuri".

Na kwanta na Lumshe ido ina tunanin magan ganun Shatu.


Mutuwa ake idan akaje zubda ciki........?
Sabo ne wajen Allah Kuma.......?
Anya zan iya kode na hakura nabar cikin nan, nasha abinda zai zubda min ciki amman yaki zubewa.

Anya ba akwai matsala ba.

Mikewa nayi na zauna bisa gado ina shafa cikina gabana na faduwa.


      Kode nabar cikin nan na hakura dasu Baba?

Inason Babana yanzu mizan yi kenan??


              *************

Bacci ya dauke shi yana farkawa yayi part din Momy cikin sauri.

Yana isa wajen Momy ya iske Momy a parloun ta.

Tsaye take tana kaiwa da kamawa wa a cikin parloun


Ganin har ya shigo bataji shigowar shiba yasa yake tunanin lafiya mike damun Momy haka har ya shigo bata san ya shigo ba  kuma kamar hawaye take kode rasuwa akayi a family dinsu?


Yace;"Momy"

Cikin daga murya, tayi saurin kallon shi.


Cikin sauri ta iso inda yake ta kama duka hannayen shi biyu.

Tace;"please Anu zauna"

Ya zauna yana kallon ta mike faruwa ne da Momy itada zata bashi labari mai dadi antaho da yarinyar amman ya haka.


Momy ta kalleshi hawaye suka taru a idon ta tace;"sorry Anu kowa da kalar JARABTAR SHI WANNAN SHINE JARABTAR MU"



Kasa gane mitake nufii yayi yace;"Momy fadaman mike faruwa?"


Momy da kyat ta nemo na tsuwar ta motsa baki zatayi magana........

-----------------A hankali Momy tace; "Ta mutu jiya da daddare".


Saurin mikewa Anuwar yayi jikin shi har kyarma yake ya rike kanshi yana cewa; "No! No!! No!!! Please Momy kice wasa ne"


Ganin ta kasa motsi yasa yazo yana girgiza ta yana cewa; "Momy bana son irin wannan wasan kada ki sake pls taya zata mutu tabar ni kamar dai ni nafi kowa rashin sa'a a rayuwa Momy zanje da kaina sun yaudare ki basu jeba wallahi".


Momy ta miƙa mashi wayar hannunta ta danna wani Vedio.

Fuskar Inna ce ta bayyana tana kuka tana jan majina tace;"Ai munyi rashin Nana ɗiya tace tana da biyayya jiya tace man zata je wajen dangin Baban ta shine na barta ta tafi ashe ban kwana muka yi da ita".

Ta ƙara fashewa da kuka gwanin ban tausayi tana goge hawayen ta da Hijab din jikin ta tace;

"Tunda ta tafi hankali na bai kwanta ba, ashe tana zuwa ta iske yaran su zasu je yin icce ta bisu a dajin maciji ya sareta ta mutu koda aka kawo ta gidan su Babanta ta mutu". 


Wani kuka Inna ta fashe dashi sannan tace; "yanzu haka saurin da nike chan zanje domin kuwa yanzu da safe aka aiko man, daman ita kaɗai ce gare ni hakan yasa idan na tafi bazan ƙara dawowa garin nan ba, zan tafi nima wajen dangina, nagode maku sosai". 


Ta ida maganar ta a cikin kuka mai taɓa zuciya.

Wani irin ihu yayi ya buga wayar a bango ta tar watse.


Momy banda hawaye babu abinda take, ko motsin kirki ta kasa.


A fusace ya bar ɗakin yayi part din shi.


Yana zuwa ya kori hadiman shi cikin tsawa ya rufe ɗakin.


A bakin mirror ya tsaya yana kallon kanshi.

Yace;"meyasa ban da sa'a a cikin rayuwa ta ne? meyasa duk abin da naso sai ya nisan ceni? Nayi alkawarin bazan sake son wata mace ba ballan tana hakan ta kara faruwa dani".

Rike kanshi yayi idanun shi sunyi jawur tamkar garwashi.


Wata irin kuwa yayi, ya zudda kayan dake jere bisa mirron tare da kallon kan shi a mirror hannu yasa ya nushi mirror ya fashe ya zube ƙasa.



Ya kara buga hannun shi a bango yana kiran; "Why! Why!! Why!!!". 


Hawaye suka zubo mashi rabon da yayi hawaye tun ranar mutuwar MANAL saidai fuskar shi da idon shi su chanxa amman ba dai hawaye su zubo ba.


Hannun shi na jini ga glas ya shiga jikin shi amman kamar ma baiga abinda ke faruwa dashi ba.


Kwankwasa ɗakin ake Momy ce dan yana jin muryar ta.


Toilet ya shiga yana son yin wanka bai san tayaya zai haɗa ruwan wankan ba ma.

Idan yana cikin ɓacin rai idan ya shiga cikin ruwa yana ragewa.


Tsaki yayi ya fita ya zauna a bakin bed ɗin shi.


Rike kanshi yayi dake masa zafi yayi.


Gaf! Yaji an bude kofa Momy ce tana maida numfashi kamar wacce tayi gudu.



           **************

Na kasa fidda shawara guda na koma na kwanta ina shafa ciki na.


Ina kwanciya aka kira sallar magriba na tashi nayi sallah, sai ga Hajiya ta shigo tace; "mi kike so kici yanzu". 


Nayi murmushi nace komai ma Hajiya.

Tayi murmushi tace;"bari asamo maki abu mai dan dadi". 


Ta fita na cigaba da Azkar ana kiran sallah na mike nayi Isha'i.


Ina gamawa ta shigo da kayan abinci.

Ta aje a gaba na tace; "Gashi nan tuwon shinkafa ne miyar Ganye nasan zaki soshi ga gasasshen nama nan". 


Nace; "Hajiya bani da bakin maki godiya nagode sosai Allah ya saka maki da Alkhairi"

Dariya tayi tace; "ba komai yima kaine".

Ta juya ta fita na sabko naji dadin abincin sosai naci na sha ruwa.

Na bude nacii naman sai kamshi yake sosai, naci na koshi, nayi hamdala naji cikina ya motsa na shafa cikin ina murmushi, lalle idan na rabuda shi shikenan zan koma wajen Baba.


Wani tunani nike yanzu fa saboda cikin nike cin kayan dadi gashi haramun ne zubda ciki idan na mutu yanzu wuta za'a sanii?. 

Hankali na ya dan tashi nace; "wazan tambaya yanzu saɓo ne idan mutum ya zubda ciki??"


Shiru nayi na kasa fidda shawara, na hau gado na kwanta.


             *************

Momy tace; "Anuu menene yaji ma kanka ciwo haka?? Kaifa musulmi ne kuma kasan Allah yana Jarabtar bayin sa, kila yarinyar nan ba Alkhairi bace shiyasa Allah ya amshi abunsa, kayi hakuri dan Allah kaɗau ki hakan kaddarar kace bawa bai isa ya kauce ma kaddarar saba kayi hakuri ka kwantar da hankalin ka gobe za'a fara biki kayi hakuri ka daure kodan lafiyar Daddyn ka, sannan yanzu kasan su mai Martaba basu san da wannan al'amari ba, idan ka chanza zasu yi zargin wani abu, kuma kasan duk mai Martaba yaji ka taɓa yima karamar yarinya fyaɗe hankalin shi tashi zai yi sosai, ban san ya zasu dauki abun ba kayi addu'ar Allah yasa haka yafi Alkhairi sannan kasa ta a cikin addu'ar ka hakan shine soyayya".


Sai a lokacin ta numfasa tana kallon shi tace;"nasa anba iyayen ta kudi masu yawa wa'inda duk inda zasu je to zasu ishesu rayuwa na wani lokaci"


Kama hannunshi dake jini tayi tace;"taso muje part dina, a gyara dakin a chanxa abun da ka fashe ga hannun ka jini na zuba muje"


Mikewa yayi tana rike da hannun shi har suka isa part dinta.


Da kanta ta gyara mashi hannun shi taba shi magani ya sha.


A zuciyar Momy ta rasa ya zata yi ta so ta fada mashi gobe za'a fara bikin shi a Abuja, amman takasa ta yaya zata gaya mashi yana cikin wannan halin kuma idan ango baije ba akwai matsala ga hannun shi da raunii.


Ta gumi tayi ta rasa mafita akan al'marin Prince.

Tana zaune parlour sai ga daya daga cikin hadiman ta tashigo, kallon ta tayi cikin girmamawa ta duka tace;"sako ne daga Mai martaba"


A hankali tace;"ace mai sakon ya shigo"


Ta juya sai ga Sani ya shigo daman shi an saba dashi a ko ina, ya dukar dakai ya kai gaisuwa cikin girmamawa sannan yace;

"Mai martaba yana neman Yarima yace ya kira wayar shi bai dauka ba Allah yasa yana  cikin koshin lafiya"



Tace;"yana lafiya Amman yana hutawa kace za'ayi mashi magana"


Tashi yayi yana godiya ya fita. 


Mikewa tayi bedroom ɗin ta ta nufa inda tabar shi.


Yana tsaye bakin window hannun shi mara rauni yana cikin aljihu idon nan nashi jawur tamkar garwashi.


Momy ta isa inda yake tace;"Mai martaba yana neman ka, amman baza kaje ba, dan hankalin shi zai tashi idan ya ga rauni a hannun ka, nasa a chanza maka abubuwan daka bata, zanje part din Ammie yanzu"


Ta juya tabar shi nan.


A hankali ya furta meyasa iyayen shi ba zasu fahimce shi ba. 

Runtse ido yayi yana istigifari meyasa yakasa yadda da mutuwar MANAL da wannan yarinyar mai fura?? A matsayina na musulmi bai kamata ace nakasa yadda na mutuwa.


Damke hannun shi yayi wanda yaji rauni, wani irin zafi yake ji amman kara damke hannun yake yana  cije leben shi har saida yayi jini, sannan ya fara maida numfashi.


Momy ce da Ammie suka shigo ɗakin ganin abinda ke faruwa yasa suka yi saurin isowa.


Ammie tace;"Lafiya Yarima mike faruwa?"

Momy tayi saurin cewa; "kin gani baida lafiya ba zai samu zuwa Abuja ba gobe, yace man da ciwon kai ya tashi sosai kinsan halin shi da taurin zucciya shine fa ya buga hannun shi a glas"


Ammie tace;"Haba Yarima yanzu fa ka girma  ya kamata ace kabar taurin ran nan, kamanta tayi man alkawarin ba zaka taɓa kara saka kan ka irin wannan abun ba"


Shiru yayi bai tanka ba saboda bazai iya magana ba.


Ganin Ammie na hawaye yasa yayi saurin rike hannunta yana kirgiza kai.

Rumgume Ammie yayi yana sakin ajiyar zuciya.


A hankali yace;"Ammie Uwa zata iya yima diyar ta karyar mutuwa?"

Cikin sauri Ammie ta ɗago fuskar shi tana kallon shi cikin mamaki meyasa yace haka?


Daga kai yayi, ganin abinda yayi yasa Momy cewa;"Anuu dina ya kamata ka huta hakanan kaji"


Rike hannun Ammie yayi yana jiran amsa.

Shafa sumar shi tayi tace;"duk wata Uwa dake duniya indai ita ta haifi diyar ba zata iya yi mata kazafin mutuwa ba, domin kuwa  yi mata fatan mutuwa wani babban al'amari ne kaji ko"


Daga kai yayi Ammie tace;"miyasa ka tambaya ne, wani abu ya faru?"

Momy tayi saurin cewa;"a'a ya kamata ya kwanta ya huta kinsan ciwon Kai"


Mikewa yayi yace;"zanje dakina"


Daga haka yabi ta gaban su ya wuce su duka suka bishi da kallo cikin so da kauna

A tare sukayi ajiyar zuciya.


Yana zuwa daki yayi salloli ya koma ya kwanta.


Zafi sosai a jikin shi sai kyarma yake hakan yasa ya jawo blanket ya rufe jikin shi.



            ************

Ina bude ido naji ana sallolin asuba na tashi na shiga inda Hajiya ta nuna man tace bandaki ne cikin daki na.


Nayo alwala na dawo nayi raka'atanul fijir na zauna ina lazumi da hannu saida aka tada sallah kana nima na tashi na tada Sallah.


Ina gamawa na duba dakin ko ina babu alkur'ani nace a fili;"kila ba musulma bace Hajiya ta rika shiyasa tayi mata butulci tunda gashi ba Qur'ani a cikin daki, dan nasan musulma bazata iya zama daki babu Qur'ani ai bun kunya ne gareta"


Sai kuma nayi tunani nace kila kuma ta tafi da abunta bari safiya tayi na amso daya daga cikin na Hajiya.


Haka na zauna na fara tilawar na Kai har safiya ta waye.


Naji masu aikin Hajiya na aiki a tsakar gida.


Na mike zanje gaida Hajiya.

Ina zuwa bakin kofata mukayi kicibis zata shigo cikin dakina.

Na duka har kasa na gaida ta tace;"Haba Nana aini kamar Uwace anzama daya basai kinyi wahalar dukawa ba".


Nayi murmushi ta nuna man wani Abu tace;"ga wadirof nan akwai kaya ki duba a ciki naga bakida kiba wadan na sukayi maki saiki fidda kamin nadawo zan karo maki wasu wadan da sukayi maki yawa kuma ki ajesu waje daya idan nadawo kiban dan kada a tara maki tsumma"


Cikin murna nace;"lah Hajiya kibar su kala biyu ma sun isheni basai kin sawo man wasu ba"

Tace;"a'a nafison ki fito kiyi kyau, duk da kyaun ki ko wane irin kaya kikasa kyau suke maki ko tsumma ne bare sabbi, tunda nike bantaba cin karo da mai kyaun ki ga kuruciya shiyasa komi kikeso zaki samu"


Washe baki nayi nace;"Hajiya Nagode"


Tace;"bakomi yanzu za'a aiko maki da abinci idan bai maki ba ki fada akawo maki wani"


Ta juya ta fita na mike na buda inda ta nunaman nafara fiddo kayan duk babu kalar da nasani ganin kayan nike wasu iri ban taba ganinsu ba.


Atamfa a ciki kala hudu ne sai leshi daman bantaba daura leshi saide nagan shi jikin Yaya maimuna diyar Inna.


Cikin murna nike kallon leshin kara tabawa nike a hankali nace;"Yau nice zan saka leshi?"

Kara tabawa nike ina juya nace bama kalar nasu Yaya maimuna ba wannan yafi kyau.


Kayan sunada yawa sosai inagama fiddowa na mike zan gwada leshin doguwar rigace amman ga mamaki ba kalar dinkin garin mu ba wannan tun daga sama har kasa fadin su guda kallo nike cikin mamaki.


Ina kallon ta saiga Hajiya ta shigo cikin shiga Mai kyau sai kamshi take tana tamnar cingam ga gilashi a idonta hannunta rike da jikka Mai kyau.


Sakin baki nayi Ina kallon ta tace;"Na shirya zani cefane kasuwa kin gwada kayan ne, dan nasan kala nawa zan kara".


Cikin sauri nace;"yanzu zan gwada".

Tace;"saka black and maroon din les din hannunki abu ga farar fata nasan zai maki kyau sosai gashi yanada tsada"


Banson yadda zan saka ba dan mamaki nike ai wannan rigar idan nasaka ta zata matseman jiki kila ko numfashi ban iyawa.


Ta mikowan wani Abu tace;"ga towul nan cire kayan ki kisaka".


Na amsa nayi hanyar bandaki tace;"ke ina zaki bade cire kaya ba to aini macece ko zindir kina cire kaya a gaba na".


Na dukar da kai saboda ban iyawa.

Karatun Baba na tuna akan tsiraici.

Taya zan fada mata komu mata haram ne kallon tsiraicin juna.


Tace;"lalle kanki na cikin kwali tafi ki chanza watarana a gaba na zakiyi zindir"


Nayi gaba na shiga banda cikin raina nace;"Allah ya rabani da nuna tsiraici na ga kowa"


Na cire kayana na daura wannan Abun naga duk cinyoyi na a waje.  

Nadade saida naji tana cewa;"lafiya Nana naji shiru"


Kana nayi shahada na fito ina matse kafafuna.


Dan karamin tsoki tayi tace;"nifa banason kauyan ci wallahi zanyi fama kenan?"


Ta mikaman tace;"Ina bireziyar ki?"

na dukar da kai saboda kunya saida ta maimaita kana nace;"A garin mu sai anyi aure ake sakawa "


Ta kalleni naga gabana take kallo na, naji babu dadi kafafuna har kyarma suke.

Ni kaina kwanakin nan naga gabana yayi girma sosai ya ciko shiyasa kullun gidan Inna da Hijab nike yawo dan gabana har yafi na Ma'u sosai kuma basu Dade da yin haka ba.


Wani wuri ta buda saiga bireziyoyi da yawa da wando sun fito ta dauko guda.


Tace;"saka"


Nace;"ban iyaba bansan ya ake sakawa ba"

Ta matso kusa dani ta dauraman tace;"kinga yadda nayi gobe ke zaki saka da kanki"


Na daga kai ita ta nunaman nasaka rigar.

Tace;"zage zip din"

Nace;"ban iyaba"

Tace;"lalle ba karamin  daji kika fito ba"

Tazo ta saka man Zip na kalli kaina rigar ta matseni tun daga sama har kasa kirjini sun fito sosai sunyi sama kusan rabi duk a bude.

Tace;"yi tafiya nagani zaki iya yin tafiya cikin street gwon"


Ina takawa naji kamar rigar ta barke na juya naga wajen kafata ne a baya duk ta bude har kusan kinyata.


Nace;"bayan rigar ya barke ana ganin jikina"


Tayi murmushi tace;"haka dinki yake wallahi ban taba ganin wadda kaya suke ma kyau kamar ki ai duk kyawun ki dakika saka kayan nan sai naga ada banga komi ba tobarai  na fiddo ki gari, kuma cikin ki cikin Nonu wanki yake shigewa shiyasa bazamu zuddashi yanzu ba sai ya fito kana amman da wahala yayi girma sosai tunda a Nonuwan ki yake shigewa, namayi mamaki dana ga har ya danyi tudu, kilade ba guda bane"


Nayi shiru kunya duk ta isheni miyasa wasu matan basujin kunyar fadin wani abu kai tsaye.

Kunya fa tana daga cikin imani....

Hajiya ta tsunke man tunani tace;"saka wannan nagani naga dakika saka street gwon kamar ke kikayi kanki, saka English wears nagani dan nasan wane zan siyo maki da yawa, amman basai kinje toilet chanza kaya ba"

Nace;"to"


Wasu riga da wando ne taban a tunani na kasan kaya ake sakasu.

Nasaka wandon da kyat ya matseni fiye da rigar dana cire, taban wata riga saman kafadar ta a bude nasaka.

Ihu tayi tace;"lalle sai an ringa layi a Restaurant dina Allah na gode maka jari a hannu wallahi nayi tafiyar sa'a yarinya tamkar ita ta kera kanta"


Nide sakin baki nayi ina kallon ta bangane mi take nufi ba.

Tace;"daga gobe garin nan zai fara cika duk yaran manyan kasar nan da iyayen su zasu shigo Bauchi dan haka damu da Hotel zamu sha ciniki kinga kenan Allah ya kawo ki lokacin da ya kamata dan ko makaho ya laluba ki saiya dawo gidan chin abinci na balle masu ido"


Ta rikitaman kwakwalwa narasa fahimtar ta toni ina ruwana da wasu yaran manya


Ta juya ta fita cikin farin ciki.


Na tashi na maida kayana na zauna saiga wani yaro ya kawo man abinci harda dafaffiyar kaza da soyayyan kwai.

Abinci ne iri-iri masu dadi aka ajeman ga lemu bana kwalba ba shi akwali yake ga sanyi.


Nayi murmushi na fara chii.


         **************

Koda ya tashi da safe jikin shi ya saki yayi sallah ya zauna yana fitowa parlo ya iske kayan Breakfast ya zauna ya fara chi.

Jin abincin yake tamkar madachi saboda yadda yakejin zuciyar shi.

Magani ya gani a gefe yasan Momy ce yayi murmushi bai tsaya duba na komi minene ba ya balla yasha ya mike ya koma daki.

lumshe ido yake saboda yadda yake jin bacci.


Koma wa yayi ya kwanta bacci ya dauke shi.




             ************

Na gyara dakina hadda abubuwan kamshi na fesa naje nayi wanka na dawo duk cikin kayan banga wadan da zan saka ba sai wata doguwar riga bata da matsewa na saka na zauna naji shiru nayi kewar littattafai na.


Haka nayi cikin daki Hajiya bata dawo ba sai yamma ta iso da kaya masu yawa.


Na fita muka gaisa tace;"ga tailanan ya auna ki dan inason ya kawo kayan da wuri akwai wasu sabbin riga da wando dana siyo maki saiki saka gobe".


Kallona tellan keyi irin kallon da Lado keman yana hada miyau yana lasar labban shi matso kusa dani yayi.

Cikin raina nace"wai Hajiya karuwan ci take nufin nayi kenan??....kode ba haka take nufi ba nakasa gane inata dosa"


Jin mutum ya matso kusa dani yasa nayi saurin kallon shi.

Washe baki yayi yace;"Hajiya lalle zaki sami kaya ai koni da zaki taimakaman kiban ita kwana daya da har shekara ta kare kyauta zan maki dunki kuma dunkuna masu kyau wadan da idan mace ta saka su sai namiji ya kalla"

Zaro ido nayi ina kallon Hajiya dake murmushi tace;"wannan kalar manya ce kajii tana da tsana sosai"


Kallon gabana yake yace;"konan za'a huta"

Na runtse ido hawaye suka zuboman ina farin ciki zan huta ashe yanzu nafara fuskan tar rayuwa.


Jin hannu a gaba na an damki gabana nayi saurin bude ido.

Telan da Hajiya ta kawo ne.

Jinayi tamkar zuciyata zata fashe banson lokacin da wanka mashi mari ba.


Kallo na suke su duka cikin mamaki Hajiya ta zaro ido.

Tana kallo na ranta a bace.


Mamaki nike a gaban ta fa namiji zai ketaman mutunci to miyasa ta chanza fuska?

Tsawar da tayine ya maidoni daga duniyar tunanin dana shiga.

Jikake tas!! Karar marin da tayi mani.

Jinayi kaina na juya jawa nayi gaba nayi baya na sulale kasa.

Tush!!!



Qurrah

----------------Kara na saki mai razanar wa.

Wani irin zafi naji a ƙasana na runtse ido nace; "Hajiya ki taimakaman ciwo"


Da Hajiya da tailanta suka kalli juna tailan yace; "Hajiya taho ga shawara na samu".


Tace; "Shehu ban son jin komai game da yarinyar nan tabar man gida, dan iskanci haihuwar ta nayi da zan rike ta inbata chi da sha da sutura sannan na mata duk abinda take so kenan a banza zanyi shi".


Jan hannun ta yayi suka shiga ɗaki tana ta bala'i yana bata hakuri.


Suna shiga ya kalli Hajiya yace; "Hajiya yarinyar nan karama ce yanzu dole taji tsoron iskan ci tunda baki san abinda iyayen ta sukayi mata ba kila korarta suka yi kinga yanzu kenan bazai bata sha'awa ba  tunda kince man ciki gareta kuma shege ne"


Hajiya ta kalle shi tace; "to me kake nufi inta ajiyar ta ina kiyonta ita da abinda ke cikinta?"


Yayi murmushi yace; "Ina nufin ki biyo mata ta baya"

Tace; "kamar ya kuma ta baya?"


Yace; "yanzu muje ki kaita asibiti a duba ta ki kyaleta ta raini cikin ta kuma haihu, sannan kafin nan ina son ki nuna mata gata ki kashe mata kudi kamar ke kika haifeta"


Cikin bala'i ta zaro ido tace; "na lura dan kana kwaɗayin yarinyar nan ne yasa ka ruɗe, kana kallon taɓa ta fa za kayi shikenan ta mareka, to ina ga na kaita kwana da wani Allah kaɗai yasan me zata yi". 


Yace; "lallai Hajiya duk barikin naki baki gama gogewa ba to bari kiji, ina nufin duk abinda kika sai mata yana nan a rubuce tana haihuwa ki miƙa mata takarda kice ta biyaki cikin wata guda koki dauke mata yaro ko yarinya, wallahi hankalin ta sai ya tashi uwa da Ɗah babu abinda ba zata iya yi ba, ko kuma ki ce ta nemi kuɗin idan kuma ba haka ba, to zaki kai yaran Ɗan nata  office din 'yan sanda zaki kaita a kulle kisa a gwada ko kwana guda tayi wallahi da gudu zata bi layi". 


Shewa tayi tare da guda suka kashe ita da Shehu tela tace; "zan baka ita ka rage yunwa amman kwana guda idan komai ya kankama". 


Dariya sukayi yace; "ya kamata ki maido su Teema Baby da Sady ai sunyi nadama kinsan Teema ba dama, kuma idan taga yanda suke jin dadi kinsan fa zuciya yadda take kuma suna jan ra'ayinta a hankali kila da sai ta zudda cikin da kanta tabi layi". 


Hajiya tayi shiru tana tunani tace; "kafa kawo shawara mai kyau" muje mugani mu kai ta asibiti.


Ina nan kwance riƙe da mara ina gujin wahala.

Da mamaki naji muryar Hajiya tana cewa; "Innalillahi Nana tashi muje asibiti,  Allah yasa wani abu bai faru ba". 


Ta kama ni muka fita Shehu ya bude motar Hajiyar ta sakani baya muka yi hanyar asibiti.

Ido a lumshe saboda yadda nike ji. 


Kawai farkawa nayi na ganni kwance bisa gado da wata igiya a hannu na.


Hajiya tace;"sannu kin farka daman ƙarin ruwan ya kare bari na kira likita yazo ya cire maki".


Daga kai nayi ina kallon ɗakin mai kyau cikin raina nace komai yasa Hajiya ta hakura??. 


Ina nan sai gata da wani likita.

Yace;"yar gidan Hajiya kin tashi ko?"


Ni dai kallon shi nike nakasa magana.


Yace; "Hajiya yarinyar nada kyau sosai kunyi saurin yi mata aure, Allah ya taimaka cikin bai zube ba amman tabi a hankali banda daukar kaya masu nauyi banda aikace-aikace Allah ya sauketa lapia".


Hajiya tace;"Doctor ina son wata alfarma ba mu taɓa irin wannan aikin da kai ba, so nike ka zudda cikin ta huta". 


Cikin sauri nace; "Nikam ina son abuna, babu kyau zubdawa kuma mutuwa ake". 


Na idasa maganar da kuka likitan kallo na yake sosai yace; "Hajiya ita 'yar wannan yarinyar BARIKI ta fito ko FADE aka yi mata , ai kamar bata kosa ba yanzu haka tausayin ta nike". 


Hajiya tace; "Kai ko aita kosa wai kaga yadda ake aure a kauye, sannan cikin jikinta shege ne kila fade akayi mata". 


Jin jina kai yayi yace; "Hajiyar kibar cikin nan tunda tana son abunta kada asamu matsala kinga har tana ambaton mutuwa sannan cikin ya fara girma, inda dai lokacin da yake ƙarami to da za'a iya zubawa, sannan kuma zubda ciki ai sai Jamil".


Hajiya tace; "shikenan ba matsala ai kamar gobe ne". 


Yace;"to shikenan ga magunguna tasha, saura awo kuma". 

Hajiya tace; "kada ka damu komai kai zaka taimaka ai". 


Ya kalleni yace; "kada ki damu ni bana zubda ciki Dr Jamil ke zubar da ciki kuma an chanza mashi asibiti, yanzu ni zaki dinga ganii har ki haihu sunana Dr MUBARAK M NASIR zan taimaka naga kin haihu lafiya insha Allah". 


Hajiya tace; "bani number Dr Jamil". 


Yace; "Banda number shi amman zan saman maki kafin ku dawo"


Tace; "nagode"

Mika mata takardar hannunshi yayi ya juya ya fita daga dakin.


Tace; "idan kina iya tashi muje gida to ki tashi, dan nasan kin samu karfi ruwan da aka saka maki". 


Nace; "Eh Hajiya marar ta saki, kamar babu ciwo"


Ta kamani tace;"sannu kinji Allah ya baki lafiya kinji ki haifaman jikoki". 

Naji dadi amman naji kunya.

A cikin zuciyata ina jin farin ciki idan na taba cikina naji ya taso.

Na rasa meyasa yau nike son cikin kamar ba cikin shegeba sai wani jin dadi muke.

Kama hannu na tayi muka isa inda motar ta take na shiga gaba ita kuma ta zagaya tazo ta tuka.




           ♦♦♦♦♦

Koda ya tashi daga bacci yaji ya dan samu saukin ciwon Kai.


Message ya tura a waya sai ga Sanii ya shigo cikin sauri.

Toilet ya nuna mashi Sani yace;"Allah ya kara haske da farin ciki a Rayuwar magajin sarki mai A dalci idan ka gama akwai fura a parloun kasa, saƙo ne daga Gimbiya Rahama Maman MANAL". 


Duk kuncin da yake ciki bai san lokacin da murmushi ya kubce mashi ba lokaci guda fuskar shi ta chanza zuwa murmushi.


Ya ɗaga ma Sani kai, Sani ya shiga ya hada mashi ruwan wanka tare da saka kayan kamshin da suka saka toilet din kamshi.


Ya shiga wanka yayi ya fito ya iske kayan da aka tanadar mashi .


Ya saka yazo bakin mirror yana kallon gashin kanshi da yake son gyara wa.


Cikin sauri mai mashi gyaran kai ya iso ya fara yana gamawa aka saka mashi wasu cover shoes kalar kayan ne ya saka mashi.


Yarima ya mike ya fito.

Yana sabkowa a parlon kasa yaga Rahama zaune da diyarta.


Cikin sauri ta aje diyar tazo ta rungume shi tace; 'i miss you so much Bro".


Murmushi yayi ya cire ta daga jikin shi ya nufi kujerar data aje diyar ta.


Cikin sauri ya dauketa fuskar shi da murmushi yayi kissing dinta a koshi ya mannata a kirjin shi yana sakin ajiyar zuciya.


Rahama dake tsaye hawaye sun cika mata ido ta iso ta hada su ta rungume tace;"Ina rokon Allah ya baka yara masu Albarka ya saka farin ciki a rayuwar ka na har abada, MANAL tace tayi fushi ba ka zo ganinta da wuri ba"


Murmushi yayi yace;"lokacin Dad baida lafiya amman tana raina a koda yaushe".


Kama hannunshi mai rauni tayi tace;"Bari na zuba maka fura daga Abie"

Ta sake shi ya zauna bisa kujera rungume da MANAL.


Ta zuba mashi fura ta mika mashi.

Ya amsa yace;"thank you"

Tayi murmushi yana son Rahama a cikin kannansa yana son Rahama sosai dan lokacin ita ta koya mashi yadda ake kalaba har yakema MANAL itama MANAL din tafi sabawa da ita akan kowa.


Bayan ya gama sha tace;"Su Momy sunyi Abuja wajen Mother's day gobe kuma sister's day amman a Katsina za'ayi shi, please Yaya Anuu ka taimaka kaje ko Deena ne". 


Baiko kalle ta ba sai ma ɓata fuska da yayi yace; "tashi kibar nan". 

Tace;"bazan karaba Yaya kasan a koda yaushe ina tare da kai kayi hakuri ". 


Shiru yayi yana ma Jaririyar Hannun shi wasa yana mika hannun shi tana rikewa.


Ganin baida lokacin ta tace;"Zan wuce"

Yace;"me kike jira?"

Tace;"MANAL"

Shiru yayi yana kallon diyar yace; "zan kawo ta idan tayi kuka". 

Tayi dariya tana hanyar fita tace;"Kulsoom ta huta mijin ta ya iya reno"


Tana gama fadin haka tayi saurin fita tana dariya.


Murmushi yayi ya cigaba da abinda yake.

Sosai yake kula da yarinyar kamar zai maida ta ciki, daya ga ta fara alamun kuka sai ya fara mata rawa da waka.

Duk kalolin wakokin da yakema MANAL yake mata.

Har saida tayi bacci Sani ya kira ya bashi wata takarda yasawo mashi madara kalar wadda ya ringa ba MANAL din shi tare da nuna mashi wani gado na yara, ya fadi mashi a MALL din da za'a sameshi, da mayukan wanka duk ya rubata mashi kalar da yake so.


Ba'a fi one hour ba sai ga Sani dasu baki daya.


Babu abinda Anuwar bai iya ba indai game da Jaririya ne.

Dare nayi yayi mata wanka ya sakata a gadonta.


Saida Ammie ta aiko kusan sha biyu na dare aka bada ta.


        ♦♦♦♦♦

Tunda muka dawo take ta ji dani kamar ba ita ce ta mare ni ba, kulawar da take nuna min har tafi na baya naji daɗi sosai .


Kwance nike ina jin bacci hankali na kwance.


Ga kayan marmari da Hajiya ta sawo man har saida na rage, ga kuma gasasshen Nama mai yawa shima.



Haka Hajiya taita jii dani a cikin kwanakin nan.


Ina jin hayaniyya sosai na masu sayen Abincin Hajiya.


Amman ban taɓa zuwa shagon taba kuma akwai kofa daga cikin gidan zuwa shagon ban fita saidai naji hayaniyya ko kofar daki nabar fita saidai a kawo man abinci a daki na.


Kuma hakan bai damu Hajiya ba sai ma cewa da take na zauna likita yace sai na kula banda aiki.


Ina jin dadin yadda Hajiya  ke nuna ma cikina kauna.


Yau mun tashi safiyar Jummu'a tun kafin asuba ta waye naji motsi ina lekowa naga yaran Hajiya maza na aiki.


Sai aikin abinci ake nace kodai biki akeyi ne.


Nayi sallah nayo wanka yau ma kamar kullum saida nayi tilawar hadda dake cikin kaina saboda babu Qur'ani a ɗakin. 


Ina gamawa na hau gado na kwanta.

Na dade ina bacci hayaniyya ce tasa na tashi.


Naje na wanke bakina da abinda Hajiya taban na wankin baki.

Na bude abincin da aka ban kamshin ya bugi hancina.


Na zauna naci nasha ruwa ga lemun kwali mai sanyi.

Shiru nayi ina tunanin wannan wane irin kirki ne ga Hajiyar nan tunda nazo gidanta ban taɓa cin abinci ba Nama ba kuma Naman mai yawa ga lemun kwali.


Hajiya ta shigo yau adonta yafi na kullum tace; "zan kawo maki sabbin kaya ki saka yau da gobe saboda zaki dinga fita saboda jikin ki ya dinga saki, zama waje daya yana iya haifar ma cikin nan matsala, Amman idan kina motsa jikin za kiji sauki kuma ko wajen haihuwa zakiji saukin ta". 


Nayi saurin yin murmushi nace;"Nagode Hajiya Allah ya saka maki da Alkhairi"


Tace; "ba komai yanzu zan aiko maki dasu doguwar rigace yanda zakiji dadin kayan babu matsi"

Nace;"to Hajiya wai biki kuke ne?"


Tace;" a'a babban dan sarki kuma ɗa ga president shine ake bikin shi garin ko ina cike yake shiyasa muke cikin chiniki,  shiyasa tun kafin asuba muka tashi muka saka sanwar abinci bakiji kide-kide ba"


Nace;"Allah sarki ba kuda nisa kenan?"


Tace;"Eh babu nisa sosai nan ai bayan gidan ne wajen kofar shigar bayin gida"


Nace;"Bayin suma tasu kofar damaban"

Tace;"Eh daga baya akayi ta sabon ginine akayi masu duk saboda auren"


Najin jina kai cikin Raina nace kowa ne bawa da tashi rayuwar wani ta talaka wani mai kudii gamunan dai nima nayi hamdala nace;"Allah na gode maka yina musulmai da kayi shi  kaɗai ma ya isheni abin alfahari Allah kayi mana rabo a gobe kiyama". 


Jin shiru yasa Hajiya tace;"Bari in aiko ki tashi ki kara wanka zuwa la'asar sai mu fita, daga yanzu zamu dinga fita da yamma kullum har ki haihu kodan lafiyar ki, kinga har wajen gidan Sarki sai mu dinga zuwa yauma chan wajen zamu mu kalli wasannin gargajiya Yau har Yarima saiya fito ya zaga gari shiyasa Yau kamar sallah take a gare mu"


A hankali na maimaita;"Yarima....."


Na mike naje nayi wanka hakanan naji ban son fita saboda yanzu cikina ya fito ina jin kunya Amman ba zan iya yima Hajiya gardama ba.


Ina fitowa na iske Hajiya ta mikoman wata riga ta less mai shegen kyau tace;"gashi nan Bubu ce yadda zakiji dadi babu takura".

Na amsa na saka tace;"Masha Allah ta koyi maki kyau ga turarruka nan ki saka, kinga Man da zakiyi amfani dashi saboda ke hasken ki yayi dai-dai basaikin saka wani mai ba kuma gaki da ciki kiyi sauri ina jiran ki ina shago".



          ♦♦♦♦♦

Ba ruwan shi da mutanen gidan ko Mai Martaba a masallaci suke gaisawa bai zuwa part dinshi.


Ammie kuma mutane sunyi mata yawa saboda gidan cike yake tun laraba har zuwa yau jummu'a.

Tun safe ake kawo mashi MANAL sai dare azo a dauketa hakan yasa baidamu da abinda akeyi ba kullum yana nan yana hidimar MANAL shi kaɗai a part ɗin shi sai idan yana bukatar wani Abu kana yasa akawo mashi.


Jawad ne kanin shi ya shigo cike da fara'a a fuskar shi yace;"Ina Angon Kulsoom yake gani na iso tare da Daddy"


Yana rungume da MANAL ya gama bata madara dinta, goge mata madarar dake gefen bakin ta yake.

Baiko kalli Jawad ba sai cigaba da abinda yake yayi.


Jawad bai damu ba yazo kusa dashi ya fada bisa kujera yana kiran;"Wash nagaji kamar dai nine angon, Ango yana dakin shi yana hutawa ni kuma ina chan bani Abuja bani Katsina"


Anuwar bai kalle shi ba yace;"waya saka ka?"


Dariya yayi yace;"kayan da kasa nayi order ne na kawo maka yau jummu'a ya kamata ka saka su dan zamuyi hawa gobe daurin aure Daddy yayi maku dinki"

Kallan shi yayi yace;"banso malam ina ruwan ka da kayan da zan saka, nace maka zanyi kwalliya ne"


Jawad yayi dariya yace;"kasani ko wadda ka sayesu saboda ita ta gani , saboda nasan tana hawa social media kuma photunanka na yau da gobe babu inda ba zasu je ba, wai ka kalli Vedion shagalillikan da aka sha a Abuja da Katsina ko ina maganar shi ake ka duba yanzu a Twitter ko Facebook ko IG kai ko ina maganar bikin ak,matsalar guda babu Ango kowa yana son ganin ka kaga yau sai farar fatar nan tayi duhu saboda yadda za'a dinga daukar ka da Camera ka kaga zaifi ka saka kayan"


Wani kallon Anuwar yayi ma Jawad yayi tsaki ya cigaba da wasa da MANAL.

Jawad yace;"Na rantse maka sai ta ga photan nan kuma sai ta yaba, kayan a fili sunfi kyau wallahi"


Anuwar yace;"Naji zan saka kodan Daddy amman bana son shegen surutun nan naka tafi nagode" 


Jawad ya mike yana dariya yace;"sai mun hade zuwa an jima"


Ya bar part din yana dariya.


Kiran hadiman shi yayi suka zo suka hada mashi ruwan wanka,ya kira aka dauki MANAL, yayi wanka ya saka kayan.


A bakin mirror yazo ya tsaya lalle kayan sun mashi kyau yace;


Ina ma ace wadda aka saya saboda ita tana nan ta gani nasan zata yaba"

Murmushi yayi yana kallon mirror Allah ya jikan su baki daya.



Fitowa yayi part din Abie ya nufa yasan Daddy yana chan.


Ta baya yabi saboda bai son hayaniyya yana zuwa ya shiga kai tsaye.


Daddy da Abie suna zaune da sauran iyayen shi sun sha kwalliya suma sosai.


Ya dukar da kai kasa yana kwasar gaisuwa.

Su duka suka amsa cikin fara'a.


Daddy yace;"lalle wannan kwalliya ta daban ce Ango ya kamata wadda akayi dan ita tagani".

Su duka sukayi dariya kowa da abinda yake cewa shi dai kanshi a kasa.


Hakanan Yau kodan tana jummu'a kuma ya dade bai zagaya ba, hakanan yaji yana son fitar.


Daddy yace;"Nan zaki yi sallah ko, dan har angama kwalliyar dawa kan tun dazun Sallah muke jira ayi".


Yace;"To Dad".


Daddy yace;"Anya Yau ba'a wani abu a kasa ba? naga Yau kana dokin zagaya kodan duk dokin auren ne"


Ya ida Maganar yana murmushi.

Murmushi Anuwar yayi haka nan ya tsinci kanshi cikin farin ciki tunda ya saka kayan nan ko miyasa??


Ohow....



         ♦♦♦♦♦

Ban iya kwalliya ba saita kauye shiyasa banyi ba mai ne kawai na shafa  sai farar hoda sai Man baki.

Hajiya ta shigo tace;"har nagaji wallahi so nike kiyi sauri mu fita musamu wajen zama inda zamu fi kallo, amman har yanzu baki ida ba"


Nace;"Hajiya Dan kwalin ne babba ban iya daura shi ba".


Tace;"fito waje ga 'yan mata nan su daura maki".


Na fito gidan cike da mata da maza su duka suka kallo ni.


Duk sai naji babu dadi jinayi kamar na koma.

Na kalli Hajiya naga tana kiran wata da zata fita hankalin ta na chan.


Anya kuwa zan iya zuwa?? 

Mutanen dake cikin gidan Hajiya sune suka ni ruɗewa ina ga na shiga taro, Kai bazan iyaba komawa zanyi daki.


Bazan iya fita ba amman kuma lafiyar jiki na kuma fa, to ko dai na tafi ko kuma nayi wata dubarar????



Qurrh
