---------------Jikin shi har kyarma yake yayi saurin sakin hannun ta.

Abie ne ya matso inda suke ya ɗaga d'an ya tsanta yaga lallai zoben tambarin masarautar sune wanda yaba Anuwar, kallon Anuwar yake cikin mamaki lokacin Anuwar ya dukar da kanshi a kirjinta domin yaji numfashin ta.

Mike yayi da ita a hannu ya kalli Dad yace;"Dad ka taimakaman ka kira Doctor kada ta mutu idan ta mutu nima mutuwa zanyi Abie kutai makaman MANAL dina ce"


Jin ya ambaci MANAL yasa su duka sukayi tunanin lallai ya zauce.


Cikin sauri Mummy ta kamashi sukayi hanyar part din ta.


Suna tafiya saura suka biyo su baya.


A bedroom aka shigar da ita aka kwantar.

Waya Mummy tayi tazo ta dafa Anuwar da yake rumgume da ita kamar zai maidata cikin jikin shi.


Dafashi tayi amman bai dagoba sai kara kan kameta yake.


A haka har Doctor ya shigo dakin.


Mummy fita tayi dan tasan Anuwar bazai fita ba.

Tana fitowa ta iske su Mai martaba a parloun ta tare da yara tunda ta fito take kallon yaran da idanunta da sukayi jawur saboda kuka, sun rike Sani da kagan su kasan sun tsorata.


Kallon yarinyar tayi ta kalli photon Manal dake parloun, ta maida kallon ta ga yaron komi nashi kalar na Prince kamar yayi kaki, zuwa tayi ta zauna tayi shiru kamar yadda kowa yayi shiru a parloun.


Farhana dake hawaye ta kalli Sani tace;"Kawu su bamu Ummien mu mutafi gida, wallahi bazamu kara dawowa ba mun yafe zamu zauna bamu da Baban"


Mummy da hawaye suka cika mata ido tace;"minene sunan maman ki"

Farhana tace;"Nanah"

Mummy tace;"Baban kifa"


Farhana tace;"Yarima"

Su duka parloun suka maido hankalin su wajen ta.

Nifra ce ta shigo parloun kamar an jefota.


Farhana ta kura mata ido tace;"Yaya kalli mai kama da Ummien mu su bamu tamu wannan ce tasu"


Sukabi hannunta da kallo inda take nunawa Nifra dake tsaye tana raba ido.


Mummy tace;"Sani su waye dangin yaran nan"


Sani cikin girmama ya nuna Baba yace;"a gidan mu take zaune tsawon shekara shidda saboda dangin ta sun koreta saboda cikin yaran nan, itace wadda Yarima ke nema shiyasa naje na taho da Baban ta to bansan ita bace saida aka aikota ta kawo mashi fura ta ganshi su kuma yaran sune wadan da Yarima ya taba ba Maman su jini wasu yan biyu da aka taba haihuwa a MANAL hospital yace shine zai zaman masu uba to shine da ya tafi bai kara dawowa ba sai yanzu shine idan suka tambayi waye Uban su saina ce masu Yarima shine suka rike abun kullun suna maganar shi yanzu da yazo shine na kawo su sugan shi kwanan su biyu a tare dashi cikin gidan nan"


Duka dakin suka hau kallon yaran, Mummy cikin tashin hankali tace;"sune nan kenan Sani?"

Ta nuna wani katon photo na Yarima da Yara a hannun shi dake a parloun ta.

Yace;"eh sune ranar da aka haifesu ne nan"

Mummy ta kalli Dad tace;"Inagafa itace yarinyar da Yarima yayima fade a Katsina"

Cikin tashi hankali su Abie suka kalleta Ummie dake kusa da ita ta rike mata hannu cikin tashin hankali tace;"yaushe Yarima ya taba ma mace fade?? Yarima dako kallo mace baiyi kai Hajiya kila de kin manta kode Jawad kike nufi?"


Mummy ta kalli Dad tace;"ya kamata su sani tunda abin yazo a haka, ya kamata kada abar yaran nan subar gidan sai anyi tabbacin yaran waye, nifa abin ya fara rudani inaga yaran Yarima ne"


Nan Mummy ta fadi yadda akayi shi yasa yake neman ta.


Su duka banda Sallallami babu abinda suke

Mai martaba ya kalli Baba yace;"kaine Uban yarinyar?"


Yace;"Eh sanadiyar zuwan ta tallah wani yayi mata fade bamusani saide aka tashi tana da ciki a lokacin baninan wannan shine sanadiyar barinta gida"


Abie yace;"ta fadamaku sunan wanda yayi mata fade ko tasan shi?"


Baba yace;"banson komi game da haka ba saide munyi magana ta kawarta tace cikin dare abin ya faru bataga fuskar shi saide zobe da yabata, inaga zoben ne a hannunta"



Abie da Ummie suka dauki sallallami, dai-dai lokacin da Doctor ya fito.



Suka maida kallon su.

Dad yayi saurin mikewa yace;"ina Anuwar ya lafiyar shi? Yana lafiya?"

Doctor Ashraf yace;"eh saide yarinyar itama ta tashi daman lafiyar ta kalau firgitar da tayi ne yasa numfashin ta daukewa ta suma, amman yanzu Alhamdulillah zaku iya ganinta"



Cikin sauri su Farhana dake jikin Sani kwance sunyi lamo kamar suna bacci suka mike 


Farhan yace;"Kawu muje mu dauki Ummie kada ta kara faduwa"


Sai lokacin su Abie suka kara kare mashi kallo yadda yake maganar da kuma siffar jikin shi take.



Nanah na bude ido ta jita cikin jikin namiji kuma irin kamshin da bazai iya gushe mata ba, bata yadda itace ba kara runtse ido tayi tana cewa a fili;


"Ya Allah ka taimaka man kada ya kara cutar da rayuwata shine ya kara dawowa ya Allah kasa bashi bane Yarima ba ya Allah ka taimaka man zai kara mani wani cikin"


A hankali ta bude ido ta kalli girjin shi, cikin sauri tayi saurin mikewa daga jingine da take da jikin shi.



Zata fizge daga jikin shi yayi saurin rikota ta kara fadowa bisa Kirjin shi, kan kameta yayi ya matse ta gam yana sakin numfashi.



Sun dauki lokaci a haka su duka zuciyo yinsu bugawa suke kamar zasu fito Nana ta kasa kwace wa sai hawaye dake bin kumatun ta shikenan tata ta kare.


Tuna abinda yayi mata yasa cikin zafin nama ta tura shi.


Baya yayi ya jingina da gado kallon juna suke su duka idanun su sunyi jawur.


A hankali ya furta;"MANAL"


Kallon shi take ta kasa dauke idon ta akanshi  kanta ke wani irin juyawa wani irin ciwo da zafi kanta ke mata.


A hankali take maida numfashi, jitayi numfashin zai dauke zatayi baya yayi saurin rikota ya kara rumgume ta.


Shiru tayi ta kwanta a bisa kirjin shi wani irin ciwo kanta keyi mata kamar zai cire data runtse ido saita hango wasu mutane na dawo mata, amman ta kasa tuna komi.


Jin ana shafa bayan yasa ta tuna mike faruwa cikin sauri ta mike tace;"Baba na, na shiga ukku ni Nanah"


Tashi tayi daga jikin shi kallon juna suke tace;"kaine Wanda ka rabani da Babana ka rabani da mutunci na kasa jama'an kauyen mu suka tsaneni ka shiga tsanina da Baba ka nisan tani da iyaye na na haifar maka yara da bakasan kamannin suba, taya zan yafe maka"


Kuka take sosai ya kasa dauke idon shi akanta, saboy gaban shi na faduwa jikin shi duk ya mutu.

Tace;"kai waye? Ya Allah kasa bashi bane Yarima dan Yarima na insha Allah bazai zama macuci ba irin ka, ka fadaman kai waye ina Baba na?"



Turo kofa akayi suka shigo tayi saurin juyawa.


Kallon su take tana lumshe ido kamar tasan su.


Mummy tace;"MANAL...?"


Dad yace;"Hajiya kada ki rude mana kamar Anuu akwai kamanni fa, wasu idan kika gansu sai kice yan biyu ne amman basu da wani alaka ya kamata ki saita natsuwar ki abi komi a hankali"


Kallon shi tayi tace;"Ina cikin hankali na, nifa uwace taya za ai na dau cikin ta na haifeta sannan nakasa gane diyata wallahi wannan itace kuma yaranta yaran Anuwar ne itace wadda Anuwa yayi ma fade lallai Anuwar Yau na kara yadda babu wata Aljana data shafeka lallai tun shekarun baya kaga MANAL ba aljana bace"



Abie yace;"kuyi shiru hakanan mubi komi a hankali bazamu bari subar gidan nan ba har sai an gano gaskiya"



Farhana ce ta kwace daga hannun Sani tayi wajen Mamanta tana isa ta rumgume ta, Farhan ya iso yana murmushi Farhana ta dago ta kalli Anuwar tace;


"Baba kaga Ummien mu ko, dan Allah kace masu su kyale mu mu zauna dakai muna sonka sosai kada su rabumu su Indo suyita mana gori bamu da Baba, Baba munji dadi da muka ganku a tare Baba kaman alkawari bazamu kara rabuwa ba Ummie zata dawo nan"


Rumgume su yayi baki daya yace;"bazamu kara rabuwa ba har abada MANAL tawa ce"


Cikin saurin nayi saurin fita da jikina cikin nashi.


Kaina a kasa saboda akwai mutane a dakin amman ya rumgume mu.


Sabkowa nayi daga bisa gadon.

Cikin sauri Anuwar ya mike ya rungumoni ta baya.

Saura kadan numfashi na ya dauke saboda yadda naji anrungumeni a gaban su Baba.


Kasa motsi nayi na kasa daga kai na kalli mutanan cikin dakin.


Juyowa yayi dani ya kara rumgume ni yana sakin ajiyar zuciya.


Yace;"Bana iya rabuwa dake nayi kewar ki, bazan iya rayuwa mai dadiba idan babuke ni nasan kece kika dawo man"


Jikina kyarma yake nakasa motsi a gaban kowa yau Namiji ya rungume ni, hadda mahaifina da yaran dana haifa runtse ido nayi.


Anuwar ajiyar zuciya yake jiyake kamar ya maidata ciki idanun shi sun makan ce ya manta da wasu iyayen su da yaran dake zaune kara rungume ta yayi sosai yace;

"Kiyiman alkawarin bazaki kara rabuwa dani ba zamu kasan ce a tare"


Cikin daga murya Dad yace;"bazaku kasan ce a tare ba har abada, kuma kasaki yarinyar mutane so haukane akan ka aka fara mutuwa? soyayyar Manal tana niyyar maidaka mahaukaci miyasa bazaka dan ganaba ka dauki kaddara a gaban iyayen ka ka rungumi mace kasake ta nace"


Mummy tace;"wallahi wannan diyar kace"

Dad ya kalleta rai bace yace;"daman da saninki yake komi kenan? Kece kika goya mashi baya kenan"

Mummy zatayi magana ya daga hannu yace;"idan har Manal ce miyasa ta kasa ganeki a matsayin ki na mahaifiyar ta, Anuwar yafi kowa kusan ci da ita miyasa bata ganeshi ba ai yakamata ko maganar shi taji ta dawo hankalinta inma losing memory tayi miyasa tun tuni bata tuna kowa ba, har alakar  kusanci  ta shiga tsakanin su bata tuna komiba, yanzu gamu a gaban ta tana mana kallon bata sanmuba a haka zata zama diyar mu"


Mummy ta taso tazo inda muke tsaye hannu na cikin na Yarima tace;

"Kifadaman wacece ta haifeki waye mahaifin ki, suwaye dangin ki na ainahi?"


Hannuna na kyarma na nuna Baba ina hawaye.


Kallo dakin nayi banga su Farhana ba, na kalli Anuwar ina mashi kallon tuhuma.


Yaya Sani ne ya shigo ganin ina waige-waige cikin dakin yasa yace;"suna kasa wajen Inna"


Ajiyar zuciya nayi, Mummy ta isa gaban Baba ta kalle shi ido cikin ido tace;


"Daga ganinka ko a fuska kai mutunan kirkine nasan bazaka mana karya ba saboda akwai gobe kiyama zamu koma ga Allah inaso ka fadaman gaskiya Nanah diyar kace?"


Baba ya kalli mutanen yace;"dogon labari ne a tsaye bai dace ba"


Dad yace;"kinsan bazan yadda da wannan dramar  da kuka shirya keda Anuwar ba, idan har MANAL ce muna da tabbacin mutuwar ta ko ana mutuwa a dawo ne?"


Mummy ta kalleshi tana hawaye tace;"Ba'a mutuwa a dawo, amman kasan Alhaji NURA SAIKAYI babban matsafi ne idan mutuwar Manal bata gamshe shiba bai yadda da itaba zai iya abinda zai mantar da ita dangin ta, kada ka manta lokacin da ya mutu tsafin yasa babu wanda yaje wajen gawar sa saboda tsoron da yakeba mutane ta yadda Allah ya maida halittar sa, kaima a lokacin Allah ne ya baka mulkin shiyasa kayi shi, Allah ne ya nuna mashi shike badawa, zai iya komi dan ya rabaka da diyar da kafiso a duniya maybe yaga mulki dole sai kayi shi, hakan yasa yayi maka haka dan ya dasa maka bakin ciki"



Shiru Dad yayi maganar ta tayi tasiri a zukatan wadan da kenan.

Abie yace;"Tabbas hakane zai iya mantar da ita koda tanason tunawa ta kasa, amman yanzu abin yafi mu saurari bayanin wannan bawan Allah sannan muje wajen Babban yaron shi Alhaji NURA zamuji komi dan tun kamin ya mutu suka rabu, hakan yasa Alhaji NURA saida ya lalata mashi dukiya ya koma baida komi Koda wannan ya ishemu ishara sannan a auna jinin yarinyar danaka dana Anuwar dana yaranta wannan shine yanzu muje zuwa Salllah idan aka gama sai a zauna ayi magana hankali kwance yanzu zansa ta taho da yaron Alhaji NURA kamin mugama Sallah yanzu azo a dibi jinin ku"


Dad ya aminta da maganar Mai martaba suka juya har zasu fita Mai martaba ya juyo ya kalli Anuwar daya rike hannun Nanah kamar za'a kwace mashi.

Yayi murmushi yace;"Kai baka Sallar, kuma matar ka ta fita tana kuka ya kamata kaje wajenta, tunda taji kanada wasu yaran kaga ka mata laifi"

Mummy ta kallesu tace;"aiko a kaunar da Anuwar kema Maifurar shi kasan akwai alamun tambaya, Ammie kalli yadda sukayi kyau"


Ammie murmushi tayi tace;"Nifa yaran nan inde ba yaran Aljanu bane to yaran Anuwara ne babu tantama tuni na yadda, yaro karami amman ace yanada zuciya da izzah sannan komi nasu iri daya ai abun dubawa ne lamarin"


Ammie ta kalle shi tace;"muje kayi sallah a cikin jam'i"


Rumgume ni yayi ta baya ya kwantar da kanshi bisa kafadata yace;"Ammie bazan iya rabuwa da ita ba, a tare zamuyi"

Mummy tayi dariya Ammie tace;"idan akayi bincike ba Manal bace saikayi istigifari akan shige mata da kakeyi ko kunyar yaran ka bakaji a gaban su ka rumgume Maman su hadda lumshe ido, duk randa ka kara haka a gaban mu saina saba maka kai har yau bakajin kunyar mu"


Mummy tace;"kagan ka Anuu yawan shige mata yasa a wanchan karon Mai martaba yace baza'a barku kutafi ba babu aure ta fara girma shine yanzu ma a gaban shi kayi halin naka, koda yake na manta kace idan kana tare da Manal baka iya control din kanka"



Ta ida maganar tana dariya suka fita suna murmushi.


Maida kallon shi yayi gareta jan hannunta yayi ya zaunar da ita gefen gado ya kura mata kallo duk saita ta kura saboda babu Hajib a jikinta.


Yace;"yanzu Manal baki gane Yaya Anuu din kiba?"


Na dukar dakai nace;"nide banson kuba kasa karman hannu kuma kabar rikeni babu kyau kumani tafiya zanyi saboda babu kyau keban cewa tsakanin Namiji da Mace"


Murmushi yayi ya maso saide najini cikin kirjin shi.

Gabana faduwa yake na tuna lokacin da yasakani a kirjin shi, dago hannuna yayi yana kallon zoben yace;"daman kina saka shi?"


Dagowa nayi na kalleshi nace;"Ban taba sakashi ba sai yau, kyauta nazo nayi dashi shiyasa na saka shi kuma bana bukatar ka sake tabaman jiki"


Kallona yaie ya maimaita...kyauta.

Yace;"wazaki ba kyautar shi"


Na kalleshi gabana na faduwa saboda yana man kwarjini nakasa mashi rashin mutum cin dana tanada ga duk wanda ya bataman rayuwa amman shi na kasa mashi na dukar da kaina nace;

"Saboda Yarima yasa nazo gidan nan inason na ganshi shizan ba, ka fadaman wanene Yarima na asali wanda ya taba ban jini wanda ya ceto rayuwata"


Murmushi yayi yace;"miyasa baki yadda danine Yarima ba"

Kai tsaye nace;"saboda duk d'an Sarki Yarima ne"

Murmushi yayi yace;"muje muyi alwala lokacin Sallah na kurewa kuma mu ake jira Matar Yarima dan gana son Yarima a idanun nan naki"

Mikewa nayi ina kallon kozanga Hijab dina.

Nunaman bandaki yayi yace;"ga toilet nan"

Na shiga ina kallon wajen Babu buta hakan yasa nayi tsaye shigowa yayi nayi saurin juyawa nunaman yadda zanyi amfani dashi yayi.


Nayo alwala ina fitowa an ajeman Hijab da abun Sallah.

Zuwa nayi nasaka duk yana tsaye yana kallona.


Natada Sallah ina gamawa naga shima yana sallah yana gamawa na mike na linke abin sallar na mika mashi ya amsa.

Kama man hannu yayi yazo bakin madubi dani ya aza kanshi bisa kafadata yana ganin hawaye na zuba yasa yayi saurin daga kanshi ya juyoni.


Nace;"miyasa zaka kara cutar dani a karo na biyu, niba matar ba  amman tunda muka hadu kaki barina"


Shiru yayi yana goge mata hawaye.

Kama hannunta yayi batace mashi komi ba suka fito dakin.


A hankali suke taka takalen benen dakin suna sabkowa jama'an dake parloun suna kallon su

Cikin sauri Mummy ta mike ta kamo hannun Nana.

A bisa kujera ta zaunar da ita ta zauna itama.

Aza kan Nana tayi bisa jikinta.

Mai martaba yayi sallama yace za'a diban jinin su kana yaron Alhaji NURA ya fara magana sai Baba.


Bana son allura hakan yasa da za'a sakaman abun dibar jini na runtse ido cikin sauri Yarima ya dawo kusa dani.

Shida Mummy sai faman sannu sukeman Mummy ta rumgume ni tana sannu Anuwar kuma yana rike da hannu na yana sannu.


Doctor yafita da jinin mu, Mai martaba yayi gyaran murya yace;

"Muna jiran ka"

 Ya kalli yaron Alhaji NURA

Yaran Alhaji NURA ya gyara zama zai fara magana.....kowa  ya maida hankishi ga yaron Alhaji NURA danjin ya labarin yake.


MARYAM D'ANKUDII {QURRAH}
*JARABTAR MU*

Page-83-84



---------------Kulle idona nayi ina tunanin kode mafarki nake, anya kuwa gaskiya ne?

A hankali na bude idona naga babu kowa a dakin cikin sauri na juya nasa hannu biyu na ture shi.

Yana zaune yana kallona na dauki wani katon hijab dake bisa abin sallah na saka na fito ina fitowa a parloun na iske su Mummy zaune suna magana ita da Ammie.

Mikewa sukayi Ammie ta kamo ni nide kaina a kasa.

Ammie tace;"Ina marar kunyar yake bai fitowa ne shi?"

Nayi shiru ina murmushi ina murza yatsun hannu na.

Mummy tace;"kina gani fa yaga Kulsoom a tsaye amman koya saki MANAL saima fizgarta yayi ta fada jikin shi, hakan ba daɗi ai sai ya boye kwadan shi a gaban matar shi, wannan ai cin mutunci ne idan mu iyayen shi mun san halin shi da kuma abinda ke tsakanin shi da Manal ita fa Kulsoom tun jiya ta kulle dakin ta tana kuka wai ashe tun a London baya kulata zanyi maganin shi". 


Ammie tace;"Allah ya shirya shi, bansan yama akayi yasan tana nan ba saboda shifa yasa na boye ta amman saida ya ganota bari naje naji da baki". 

Mummy tace;"muje kiyi wanka ke ake jira duk mutane sun halarta, haddasu Hajiyar mu duk sun iso". 

Nace;"to Mummy"


Koda muka isa naga Kakata mahaifiyar Mummy hadda kuka ta rungume ni babu abinda take sai hamdala sai sauran yan uwan Mummy ne kowa sai tabani yake yana cewa lallai itace kamar su maidani ciki saboda kauna, Mummy tace aje a haɗa man ruwan wanka.


Ina fitowa na iske abinci naci Nifra ce ta shigo ita da wata wai ita zata man kwalliya duk abinda ake yara na suna raina nayi kewar su sosai amman babu halin tambaya ko Inna tun jiya rabona da ita haka Baba babu yadda zanyi na jure, motsi kaɗan zanyi za'ace minake so.

Haka akayi man kwalliyar ni kaina dana kalli kaina a madubi saida nayi mamaki.

Wani doguwar riga Mummy ta aje man tace;"idan angama zaki je airport keda Malam Liman tarbo iyalen sa tun jiya Dad dinku yayi waya azo dasu". 

Cikin murna nace;"nagode Mummy"


Na lumshe ido ina tuna kauyen mu da Shatu da Jabe Ma'u matar Kabiru dan maigari, kirarin ta na tuna nayi murmushi.

Ko yaran Shatu nawa? Allah Sarki kila Jabe yayi aure haka dai naita tunanin yan kauyen mu Mani koya chanza wannan motar da kullun saita lalace a hanya ohow.


Hajiyar su Mummy ta dafa ni tace;"Masha Allah lallai farin cikin gidan ta ya dawo ina ma Yarima murnar abokiyar rayuwar shi ta dawo, banson wane kalar farin ciki nake ciki ba farin cikin da muke ciki bai musul tuwa, abin al'ajabi shine zuri'a data shiga a tsakanin ku Allah zamuyi ma godiya munyi kewar ki kina a zukatan mu". 

Murmushi nayi na rungume ta kana na goge mata hawayen fuskar ta.


Anuwar ne ya shigo da jallabiya a jikin shi yace;"Mummy please ina MANAL?"

Yayi maganar ne kamar zaiyi kuka Ammie ce ta shigo dakin, ta harare shi tace;"to marar kunya ba zaka kara ganin MANAL ba sai nan da One week kuma kabar part din nan ko kunyar sirikan ka bakaji"

Sai lokacin ya sosa keya ya gaida yan uwan Mummy dake a parloun.

 yace;"Ammie miyasa baki tausayi nane idan bazan iya jure rashin taba kona one hour balle one week wallahi jiya banyi bacci ba shiyasa nazo muyi tare da ita Wallahi Ammie......"





Mummy tace;"Anuu idan ba haka akayi maka ba bazaka gyara ba ace sunan kana da mata amman ba abinda ya dameka da ita ba'a haka yanzu shawara zan baka ina so ka koma kamar yadda kuke ada kaida Kulsoom yadda kake nuna mata kauna da sauran su, hakan zaisa a baka MANAL cikin ruwan sanyi amman ace tsakanin ka da matar ka ko gaisuwa da kyar kake amsawa yarinya tazo da kibar ta amman abinda kake mata yasa duk ta rame ita kanta Hamrah ta rage samun kulawa gaskiya ka chanza"


Kallon Mummy yayi yace;"Amman Mummy kinsan daman auren dole kukayi man saboda babu  MANAL yanzu kuma MANAL ta dawo Mummy kusan yadda zakuyi da ita"

Barin wurin Ammie tayi hanyar dakin da tasan MANAL na ciki ta nufa.

Cikin mamaki da tashin hankali daya bayyana a fuskar Mummy tace;"Anuwar kana cikin hankalin ka? kasan ko hauka kake baka fara sakin Kulsoom rashin adalcin namu zaiyi yawa sai a ce san kan mu yayi yawa duniya zata zagemu, to bari kaji wani abu nima ina goyon bayanta inde bazaka chanza ba to kaida Manal sai hange"


Daga haka tabar shi nan abin duniya ya ishe shi ya rasa taya zai bullo ma al'amarin daman kullun Daddy da Mummy ke goya mashi baya to yanzu Mummy ta cire hannun ta.


Part din Dad yayi kai tsaye yana zuwa ya hango wajen cike da mutane bai shiguwa gashi daga shi sai jallabiya ga idanun shi sun chanza.


Kai tsaye yaje part din shi a parloun su ya iske Kulsoom da kawarta.

Cikin sauri ta taso tazo tace;"Prince Hamrah bata da lafiya daman wayar ka nake nema"


Zaro ido yayi cikin hanzari yayi hanyar dakin yana zuwa ya isketa kwance cikin bargo

Saurin tallabo ta yayi yana taba jikin ta yana cewa;"Baby... Hamrah Baby.."

Ya kasa fadin magana duk ya rude Kulsoom ce ta shigo dakin tare da Hanan  ganin yadda ya rude yasa suka kalli juna su kayi murmushi.


Magana yake Hamrah amman taƙi ta amsa sai Kulsoom tace;"tun jiya take kuka wai kabar son ta wasu yara kake so, nayi lallashi amman taki bari wai ba zata bari ba sai idan kadawo wajen ta"


Maida kallon shi yayi ga Hamrah dake kwance  jawota yayi ya rumgume yace;"ina son ki Hamrah ta, amman su ai 'yan uwan kine kibar wannan maganar suna bukata ta fiye da ke saboda su basu san dadin uba ba". 


Kulsoom tace;"Haba Prince kada kace haka mana yaran da yanzu haka koni banda ikon ganin su, saidai na kalle su a Nigerian News a TV amman gamu gida daya ba'a bani ikon ganin suba matakan tsaron da aka saka a part din su ko kai ba'a saka a part din kaba, sannan kace suna bukatar ka bayan....."


Daga mata hannu yayi yace;"banson irin wannan maganar a gaban yarinya taya kina neman saka mata tsanar 'yan uwanta kenan an fifitasu akan ta kada kiman ta farin cikin da akayi lokacin da muka samu Hamrah Daddy yana kokari akan ki da 'yar ki haka Mummy kisan kalar maganar da zakiyi". 


Mikewa yayi yabar dakin da Kulsoom da Hanan suka kalli juna Hamrah ta aza kuka Kulsoom suka bishi koda suka iso parlor har ya shige dakin shi Kulsoom tace;"kin gani ko itama Hamrah dana dogara akan son da yake mata amman kalli saboda nayi mashi maganar yaran nan itama yabar ta, ya zanyi da rayuwata, ada gidan nan kullun cikin tattalin mu ake wane color food mukeso  zamuci  hadimai kullun cikin son kyautata mana suke. Amman lokaci guda sun chanza yanzu haka part din nan babu wata hadima ko guda shiko abincin bamu ciba duk daya ne tunda diyar so ta dawo, abinda keban mamaki ba Anuwar kaɗai ke haukan sonta ba ke hatta ma'aikata yadda kika san uwar suce ta dawo jiya bamuyi bacci ba yauma da alama saboda gidan yau yafi cika, ita walimar Nifra da mukazo anbar maganar ta ke intakaita maki ko beran gidan nan sonta yake balle mutanen gidan na shiga ukku na ni Kulsoom"


Hanan tace;"kiyi hakuri kinsan saboda ke nadawo Nigeria yau bai kamata kiyi saurin sarewa ba tun yanzu ya kamata kisa ka kissa a cikin al'amarin yanzu kinga babu yadda za'ayi naje maiduguri saboda Malam ya riga daya mutu bansan wajen wa zani ba, amman kafin musamu wani ya kamata muyi wani abu, ina da wata dubara idan kikayi amfani da ita zaki samu sauki kuma zaki samu shiga wajen iyayen shi fiye da baya"

Cikin sauri ta rike mata hannu tace;"ki taimakaman Hanan ke kaɗai kika rageman"


Tace;"ki shiga cikin jama'a ki nuna farin cikin ki komai za'ayi ayi dake ki boye kishin ki ki maida komai ba komai ba, zaki samu shiga fiye da tunani ki, hakan zaisa iyayen shi su fara jin tausayin ki tunda suna son ki kinga kenan laifi zai koma wajen Prince". 


Ajiyar zuciya tayi tace;"nagode Hanan haka ne yanzu bari naje nafara bashi hakuri daga nan naje wajen Manal din amman kinsan yadda na gansu bisa gado dazun ji nayi kamar na saka ihu saboda azabar bakin ciki hadda fa jawota ta kara fadawa jikin shi abinda bai taɓa man ba a gaban iyayen shiba". 


Hanan tace;"ke dai ki boye kishi daman itace mace ta farko da kika fara tsana Anuwar ya ganta itace mace ta farko da kika taba kishi akan Prince ashe kishiyar kice Mai fura". 


Tsoki Kulsoom tayi ta mike tace;"koma ki kula da Hamrah tunda yau Babu masu kula da ita kafin su Mom dinku su iso"


Dariya Hanan tayi tace;"yau nice house girl kenan?"


Kulsoom dakin Anuwar ta shiga yana nan zaune kallon pictures din su shida twins ranar da aka haifesu yake.

Kallo daya yayi mata ya dauke kai  ta iso ta duƙa tana hawaye tasan kuka yana saurin karyar mashi da zuciya tace;"nasan nayi kuskure amman kayi hakuri bazan ƙara ba ni kaina ina kokarin fahimtar da Hamrah amman taƙi ganewa amman nasan zata so 'yan uwanta kuma zata saba da rashin ka, kayi mana afuwa mun tuba amman muma zukatan mu suna cikin farin ciki akan samun MANAL kuma twins dinta na Kane munyi farin ciki sosai ina matuƙar jin dadi dana tuna nice salar haduwar ku a Katsina ina alfahari da hakan". 


Kallon ta yayi saiya tuna maganar Mummy sai kuma magan ganunta sukayi tasiri a zuciyar shi kama hannunta yayi ya mikar da ita yace ta goge hawayen ta taje su shirya hadda Hamrah idan tana iyawa suje wajen Ammie a tare.

A tunanin Kulsoom magan ganunta sunyi tasiri a zuciyar shi hakan yasa cikin murna ta fita.


Tashi yayi yaje ya shirya yana fitowa suka hadu a parlo harda Hamrah sunyi kyau sosai yana murmushi yaje ya kama hannun Hamrah suka fita.



Kai tsaye part din Ammie sukaje Ammie na ganin su tare ta washe baki ta kama hannun Hamrah ta azata bisa jikin ta.

Anuwar sai kalle kalle yake a parloun Ammie tasan abinda yake nufi ta share tana mashi magana saide yace umm yana kara kallon hanyar Bedroom din Ammie.


Wata kuyanga ta shigo ta zube tace;"Allah ya taimaki fulani mutan Katsina sun iso anje a dauko su daga filin jirgi Gimbiya MANAL Kuma....."


Anuwar ya maida hankalin shi yaji me za'ace akan MANAL din Ammie ta daga hannu kuyangar bata ida magana ba.


Ammie tace;"gani nan zuwa"

Ta mike tace;"Ni zan fita saina dawo Hamrah ga abinci chan nasa aka dafa maki dan nasan kinaso kici kafin na dawo na dauke ki kije wajen 'yan uwan ki". 




Kulsoom ce ta kunna TV tana kallon Nigerian News.


MANAL ce aka nuno tare dasu Nifra a airport din Bauchi sai daukarta ake tamkar ba itaba kayan jikinta kansu abin kallone ballan tana yadda aka tsara kwalliyar, wurin ya cika sosai mikewa Anuwar yayi rai bace ya fita.


Part din Abie ya nufa dan yasan Dad yana chan.

Su duka ya iske iyayen nashi ya kalli Dad yace;"Dad miyasa MANAL zata fita ba tare da izinina ba nifa mijin tane Dad ka taimaka ta dawo maza sai kallon ta suke a Nigerian News naganta Dad wallahi banson kowa ne namiji ya ganta Dad bata da Hijab fa"


Su duka sakin baki sukayi suna sauraren shi Abie ne yace;"ni nace taje ita da Malam su taro iyalan Malam da suka iso yanzu Daddyn ka yasa aka dauko su dan yaga bukatar haka a tare da ita kuma duk kallon da mutane sukayi mata baidame kaba na tsawon shekaru da fitar da tayi duk basu dame kaba sai wannan? To ita matar taka Kulsoom da ko ina na Nigeria photunan ku suke yawo ita ya akayi baka yi magaba ba itama ai matar kace"


Shiru yayi duk yabi ya rude babu mai tausayin shi yau ko abinci bai ciba.




Suna tsaye a airport tana kusa da Baba  tareda Yaya Sani daman yaran duk inda suke Inna na wajen su rabonta dasu tun jiya.


Nifra ce ta rike hannu saiga su Inna sun fito daga cikin jirgi hadda Ma'u da yaran ta.



Kukan tafarayi sun kusa iso ta ruga ta rumgume Ma'u kana ta rumgume Inna su duka kuka suke.


Nifra taje ta kamo hannun ta suka shiga mota kuka take sosai duk abinda Inna tayi mata hakan baisa ta tsane ta haka Ma'u jinsu take tamkar wane sashe na rayuwar ta.


Hannunta cikin nasu sukayi part din Abie.

Nace;"naji dadi Inna daki warke"

Tace;"ai yanzu Alhamdulillah ido, kafa ce tayi saura yanzu itace dole duk inda zani saida sanda kiya feman duk alhakin kine ke bina, nayi nadamar biye ma zuciya da son abin duniya Allah ya nunaman abu na su kudin dana amsa basuyi man Rana ba nayi dama kiyafe man Nana"


Zata cigaba da kuka MANAL ta rumgume tana goge mata hawaye  kamata tayi  tace suje ciki


Inna kamar tana tsoron taka kasan dakin saboda yadda taga wajen Ma'u da Yaranta sai raba ido suke daka gansu kasan bakin shiga ne.

Abie yace;"suna bukatar hutu kuma su samu natsuwa akaisu sashen da aka sabke Malam zuwa dare Sai a zauna idan hankali ya kwanta"

Mike sukayi jiki na kyarma dan da Nana kade suke sakin jiki duk sun rude kamar tsoron mutanen suke.


Kulsoom ce da Hanan sukayi sallama a parloun fuskar Kulsoom dauke da murmushi tayo inda MANAL take.


Zata rumgume ta Inna ta shiga tsakanin su tana kara kallon Kulsoom tana kallon Hanan da itama hankalin ta akan Inna ta zaro ido.


Kulsoom ta kalleta cikin mamaki haka dakin kowa hankali shi ya dawo kansu kowa da kalar tunanin sa miyasa Inna ta dakatar da Kulsoom daga rungumar MANAL.


Kulsoom da bata gane Inna ba a tunanin itace wadda MANAL ta zauna a gidan ta nan gumi.


Inna sai kara bude ido tana kallon su kowa dakin abin ya shi mamaki.


Na yanje MANAL kusa da Kulsoom tace;"wallahi sune...... wallahi sune.."


Abie yace;"Lapia minene ya faru?"


Inna tace;"sune wallahi..... itace......."








MARYAM ANKUDII

Addu'ar Tafiyar Da Kuncin Zuciya

لاَ إلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ الْعَظِيمُ الْحَلِيمْ، لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ رَبُّ العَرْشِ العَظِيمِ، لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهْ رَبُّ السَّمَوَّاتِ ورّبُّ الأَرْضِ ورَبُّ العَرْشِ الْكَرِيمُ. 
La ilaha illal-lahul-'azeemul-haleem, la ilaha illal-lahu rabbul-'arshil-'azeem, la ilaha illal-lahu  rabbus-samawati warabbul-ardi warabbul-'arshil-kareem.

Babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai Allah, mai girma (wanda babu abin da yake girmama a gare shi), mai hakuri (mai jinkirin azaba tare da ickakken ikon aiwatar da ita). Babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai Allah, Ubangijin Al'arshi, mai girma. Babu abin bautgawa da gaskiya sai Allah, Ubangijin sammai Ubangijin kasa, Ubangijin Al'arshi, mai yawan baiwa.


اَللَّهُمَّ رَحْمَتَكَ أَرْجُوفَلا تَكِلْنِي إِلَى نَفْسِي طَرْفَةَ عَيْن، وَأَصْلِحْ لِي شَأْنِي كُلَّهُ لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ أنْتَ.
Allahumma rahmataka arjoo fala takilnee ila nafsee tarfata 'ayn, wa-aslih lee sha'nee kullah, la ilaha illa ant.

Ya Allah! Rahamarka nake kauna, don haka kar ka kyale ni da kaina ko da kiftawar ido ne, kuma ka kyautata mini sha'ainina dukkansa; babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai kai.


لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ أنْتَ سُبْحَانَكَ إِنِّي كُنْتُ مِنَ الظَّالِمِينَ.
La ilaha illa anta subhanaka innee kuntu minaz-zalimeen.
Babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai kai. Tsarki ya tabbata gare ka, lallai ni na kasance daga cikin azzalumai.

اَللهُ اللهُ رَبِّ  لاَ أُشْرِكُ بِهِ شَيْئاً.
Allahu Allahu rabbi la oshriku bihi shaya.
Allah! Allah ne ubangijina, ba na yin tarayya da shi da wani abu a cikin bauta.



DEDICATED TO.....MY SISTERS😘

Page-85-86

Bissimillah Rahmanir Rahim



---------------Baba yazo ya kama hannun Inna daga MANAL yace;"kuyi hakuri Rakiya nada matsalar ido  kila ciwon ne ya tashi yasa take ganin mutane ba dai-dai ba, ko kuma shiga jirgin nan ne ya rudata tunda har yanzu a tsorace take kuyi mana afuwa"

Tunda ya fara maganar Inna ke kallon shi ya ja hannunta yace;"mutafi mana"

Inna ta fizge hannunta tace;"wallahi bazan jeba yau saina tona asirin komai a yanzu sannan zan fadi duk kalar rayuwar da Nana tayi idan yaso su kasheni na huta, zuciyata yau tas take tunda ga Nana har ta yafeman saboda haka bani da wani fargaba komi zai faru saide ya faru". 

Ta kalli sauran yan dakin tace;"kuyi man afuwa na zalinci yarinyar ku amman nayi nadama nayi kuka har nagaji nayi Dana sa ni a kan abinda na aikata, rayuwar Nana ta zama darasi ga kowa". 

Nan Inna ta fara basu labarin zaman da Nana tayi a gidan ta da ukubar da tayi mata tun tana yarinya take tallah haka take yawo babu ko takalmi a kafarta har ta girma har zuwan su Kulsoom gidan babu abinda Inna ta rage.

D'akin duk kuka suke idan ka cire Mai martaba da Daddyy da Anuwar, amman har Baba kuka take haka itama Innar dake bada labari.

Tana idawa taje gaban Manal tace;"kiyi man duk hukuncin daya dace dani ban taɓa jin labarin mace azzaluma iri na ba, rashin hakuri da tsananin kishi da zargi sun kaini sun baro ga son abin duniya daya rufe man ido, hakan yasa na kaucema hanyar kirki nazamo azzaluma na gina ramin mugunta na fada a ciki na lalata tarbiyar diyata da kaina sannan nazo nayi sanadin saka rayuwarta cikin wahala ko wannan kaɗai ya isheni bakin ciki a duniya Nana tun a duniya Allah yayi maki sakayya lallai yau ni naga ribar hakuri kiyafe man". 


Manal ta duka tana kuka ta rike hannun Inna tace;" Inna miyasa zaki fadi abinda ya wuce? Inna wallahi bantaba rikeki a raina ba kitashi Inna"


Mummy dake Kuka tazo ta kama Manal tace;"tunda nike a tarihin rayuwata ban taba tunanin akwai mutane masu kalar rayuwar da kikayi ba koda ban sanki ba labarin ki kawai naji na tabbata saina zubda hawaye ballai ace yar ciki na ita tayi wannan rayuwar Allah mai iko"

Kuka Mummy keyi sosai ta kara rungume diyarta a jiki Ammie suka taso sukazo Suma hawaye suke"

Mummy tace;"a lokacin da Anuwar yaban labarinki haka kawai naji inason taimaka maki haka Nifra har sabani muka samu da Dad dinki Amman na daure a lokacin bikin Anuwar da Kulsoom ya hanani zuwa da kaina nayi aike ashe jini nace"

Anuwar yace;"Mummy kun kara yadda dani ko daman nace maku banji a jikina ta mutu, Ammie idan baki mantaba har tambayar ki na taba yi nace Uwa zata iya yima diyar ta kazafin mutuwa? Kikace a'a daman rashin yadda danayi da mutuwar ta yasa naje da kaina"

Ya kalli inda Kulsoom take tsaye tana hawaye yace;"miyasa kika hanani ganawa da abinda nakeso? Miyasa kika nisan Uba da yaran sa? Kinsan kinada kaso da yawa a cikin wannan Al'amarin miyasa zaki yaudare mu?"

Kuka take sosai tace;"Anuwar wallahi......"

Naga mata hannu yayi hakan yasa tayi shiru.

Daddy yace;"Nima yau son kaina yasa nayi nadama naji kunyar hada ido da diyata na hana ayi taimako ashe jini na nayima rashin adalci lallai son kai ba karamin cuta bane garemu Allah yasa mudace, yasa mana tausayin juna da jin kan juna"

Abie yace;"Alhamdulillah...babu abinda zamuce saide muce mun godema Allah  Anuwar kayi hakuri Kulsoom tayi ba dai-dai ba, ba agareka ba har a wajen mu to amman haka Allah ya tsara dan haka ina mai baka hakuri, sannan wannan labari na wannan baiwar Allah ba komi bane a cikin rayuwar Nana face darasi muna rokon Allah ya bamu hakuri da juriyya a cikin wannan rayuwar tamu,  dan haka zamu iya zuwa kowa yayi Sallah iyalen Malam Kuma a kaisu a part din da aka sabki Malam"

Farhan ne da Farhana suka shigo tare da Jawad da Inna na bayan su.


A guje sukazo suka rungume MANAL suka ce;"Ummie munyi kewar ki sosai muna tareda Baban mu da Inna da kuma sabon Kawun mu kin ganshi ko yana kama da Baban mu amman Baban mu yafishi kyau saboda Baban mu nadaban ne"


Kowa saida yarinyar ta saka shi murmushi yadda take maganar Inna ta kalleta tace;"Nana wannan ai kece ta biyo ta kalli Farhan ta kalli Yarima tace ikon Allah wannan Kuma ga wanda ya biyo, yaran waye masu kyau haka kamar naku keda wannan"

Ta nuna Yarima MANAL ta dukar da kanta a kasa Mummy tace;"yaran sune daman mijin tane, cikin da kuka korota dashi sune ta haife a gidan Inna ga yaran nan da suna jarirai"

Ta nuna mata picture din dake a bango Yarima da twins.

Inna tace;"ba'a gidana ta haihuba saide ranar data haihu ranar tazo gidana, tayi zamane anan gidan wata Hajiya mai saida abinci anan bayan gidan"

Nan Inna tabasu labarin da Nana tabata tun daga randa suka hadu da Hajiya har zuwa yau.


Yarima ya kalli Kulsoom yace;"duk kece kika yi sanadin haka yanzu baccin da Allah ya azata akan hannu na gari da irin gidan Hajiya ne daba zan yafe makiba wannan baso bane kin cutar dani kinsan na tsani karya inhar kina sona miyasa bazakiyi zaman gaskia dani ba"


Kulsoom ta goge hawayen dake bin fuskarta tace;"wallahi Yarima ka yadda dani sonka ne ya makantar Dani na aikata haka a tunanina mayya ce ko aljana saboda yadda kakeson ta ni kuma ina ganin idan har kana cikin hankalin ka bazaka kulata ba saboda yadda naga ni baka kulani ba balle yar fura mai saida fura shiyasa nayi haka, amman kayi adalci kada kamanta inason ka itafa wadda kakeso shin tana sonka ne ni sone yaja nayi haka"


Inna tayi murmushi tace;"Yarinya banso nayi maku kutse a cikin lamuran ku akwai wani sirri da ban fadaba saboda sirrin Nana ne amman dole a fada anan tunda abin yazo a haka kiyi hakuri Nana baki fadaman ba amman zan maki kutse a lamarin ki"

Ta kalli Anuwar tace;"Kaine namiji na farko da Nana ta kasa boyen kishin ta akan ka, sannan kaine namiji na farko da Nana ta fara so tayi kukan so harta gaji jinin da kabata takan zauna tana kallon hannun ta tamkar zataga jinin ka a jikin ta tsabar so zuciyar ku a hade take  "


Kowa dakin shiru yayi yana al'ajabin wannan Al'amarin basu taba haduwa ba amman zuciyar su ta hadu.


Inna tayi murmushi ta kara cewa;"Son da take makane yasa tayi sadaukar da abinda ya kasan ce kamar gadon yaran ta shine abu mafi muhimmanci a rayuwar ta tazo dumin tabaka shi nayi mamaki sosai kalar son da Nana ke maka ban ida shiga ruduba saida naga ta dauki wannan zoben Wanda Uban yaran ta yabata zata baka ashe kaine Uban su kuma mijin ta itace MANAL din da ake magana kaji amman ke yarinya kibar maganar Nana bata son shi"



Nakasa gane farin ciki nake murna nayi mataukar mamaki da Inna ta fahimci halin da nake ciki kuma bata taba nunaman tasani ba, na dukar dakai saboda yadda naji an tona asirin zuciyata.


Abie yace;"Alhamdulillah wannan abu nasu akwai ban al'ajabi a ciki yanzu abinda yafi kowa ya tafi yayi Sallah Yarima kuma naga ka dauki abinda Kulsoom tayi da zafi eh gaskia tayi ba dai-dai ba Amman muna neman mata afuwa a wajen ka duk da laifin ta Babba ne amman inason kayi hakuri bawa baya kaucema kaddarar sa Allah ya rubuta hakan saiya faru dan haka tunda har Nana da yaran ka suka dawo babu abinda zamuyi sai godiyar Allah abar maganar haka"


Mummy ta kama jikokin ta tare da Anuwar suka fita.


A tare muka nufi part din da aka sabke Baba.

Koda muka isa an aje masu nau'o'in abinci.

Inna har yanzu tana hawaye saide tasa gefen zane ta goge hawayen ta.


Na nuna masu yadda zasu amfani da bandaki mukayi Sallah a tare mukaci abinci.

Nan muka fara fira nace;"Ma'u Shatu ta haihu?"


Ma'u tace;"Umm ai Shatu ta wahala bayan tahowar ki itama mijin ta dake zuwa birni neman kudi sukayi hadari ya dawo jinya daga karshe ya mutu, wai daman ashe haka yar rugar su ke cewa farar kafa gareta saboda yana auren ta daya koma wai Uban gidan shi ya kore shi, saiya fara kabu-kabu da mashin nan mota ta takashi akwai daman sunata zargin farar kafa gareta saiga wannan akace batada kashin arziki shiyasa har yau batayi aure ba, saide idan watannin nan saboda nayi wata biyu da zuwa chan, dataji nazo ko saida tazo ta tambayeni babu labarin ki nace eh wallahi tama fini rama gashi ko haihuwa batayi dashi ba hakan yasa kuma ake tunanin ko juyace abubuwa de babu dadi yanzu haka bata rugar su duk tsawon shekarun nan tana wajen dangin Innar ta saide tana yawan zuwa"


Shiru nayi zuciya naji duk ta kare babu dadi wai daman har yanzu su yan kauyen nan sunada wannan halin Allah sarki Shatu itama an nuna mata kiyayya kamar ni hawaye ne suka zubo na goge.


Hakade mukayi firar duk babu dadi Nan suke fadaman yaran Yaya Maimuna shidda Yaya Auwalu kuma yayi aure matar shi babu dadin zama itada Inna bata da mutunci ko kadan.


Saida nayi Isha'i kana saiga mai aikin Mummy tace Mummy na jirana  nace ma Baba nizan tafi na kwanta saboda Mummy ta aiko kirana.

Sukace babu komi Inna banda addu'a babu abinda takeyi.


Har bakin wajen da zaka fita part dinsu suka rakoni nace su koma haka ai kusa.

Naso kwana dasu Inna a tunanina su Mummy zasubar ni na kwana dasu kodan yadda nayi kewar su.

Su Inna na juya suka koma naji an rungume ni ta baya zanyi ihu kawai saiji nayi an juyo dani kamshin turaren jikin shi yasa na gane wanene.



Part din kusa dasu Baba naga Security ya bude mashi get hannuna ya rike sosai yajani muka shiga ina turjewa bana shiga daukata kawai naji anyi.


Runtse ido nayi wannan shine karo na farko a rayuwata da inada wayo aka daukeni.

Ina budewa na ganni ya azani bisa gado yayiman rumfa dashi muka hada ido kallona yake.

Cikin sauri na yun kura amman babu yadda zaniyi saboda yayiman rumfa da jikin shi 


Bata fuska nayi mikewa yayi nayi saurin mikewa na sabka bisa gadon.


Har najuya zanyi hanyar fita ya rikoni yana ida cire rigar shi.

San kwacewa nake hakan yasa ya dan sizgoni na fado a jikin shi kara rungume ni yayi kana ya fara zageman Zip din rigata rikewa nayi cikin sauri nayi saurin juyowa ina kallon shi cikin mamaki.


Murmushi yayi yace;"Babu abinda zan maki sarkin yan tsoro nasan kin gaji daman nasa an hada mana ruwan wanka ne"


Zanyi magana ya ida zuge Zip din rigar ta fadi kasa nayi saurin kare gabana duk da inada Bra nayi saurin dukewa kasa.

Wadirof ya bude ya dauko tuwol mikar dani yayi ya dauraman tuwol din ya zame kallabin bisa kaina gashina dayasha gyara ya zubo bisa bayana.


Kamshin gashin ya bigeshi ya kama gefen kashin yana sun sunawa tareda Lumshe ido.


Yadda yake tabani a hankali hakan yasa naji wani iri kamar ana shafata ganin zai chanza man yanayi yasa nayi saurin kwacewa.


Kai tsaye toilet na nufa ina shiga nayi saurin rufe kofar.


Na dade ina kwanta cikin ruwa masu dadin kamshi ga flowers a ciki.


Saida nagaji dan kaina kana nafito.


Yana bakin gado ya dago ya kalleni naga idanun shi kamar yasha wani abu.


Bakin mirror na zauna ganin shi nayi bayana tsaye.


Bance komi ba na daukar da kaina saboda banason muna hada ido idan muka hada ido rasa wata duniya nake nikeyi saboda idanuwan shi yadda suke rikitar dani.

Ina duniyar tunani naji ana shafani nayi saurin firgigi.


Ganin yana shafaman Mai abin yaban mamaki.


Kasa tsaida shi nayi saboda yadda yanayin yazo man.


Yana gamawa yafara gyaraman kaina ya daureman gashin nawa waje daya.


Mikar dani yayi duka jikina ya mutu babu kwari na kasa kuma daga ido.


Wata riga ya dauko ya sakaman rigar kamar net haka take iyatar bisa cinyata.

Yana gamawa ya zame tuwol din jikina.


Wani irin yarrrrrrr naji a jikina nayi saurin kallon shi cikin fuskar tashin hankali.

Kareman kallo yake hakan yasa nayi saurin fizgar tuwol din hannun shi na aza bisa rigar.



Sai lokacin na fara kyarma nace;"su Mummy basu bani izinin kwana da kaiba zanje saida safe


Jawoni yayi ya fara zubaman turarruka kana yace;Amman ai sun san ke matata ce ko?"


Nace;"Amman ai kanada wata sai anzauna an fidda kwanaki kana dan Allah ka barni na tafi"


Jan hannuna yayi ganin zan bata mashi time yasa ya dauke ya aje bisa gado ya jaman bargo shi kuma ya tafi toilet.


Ganin haka yasa na sabko nayi parlour ina zuwa na kama kofa zan bude taki buduwa haka nayita kokarin budewa amman ko motsi batayi.

Komawa nayi na kwanta bisa daya daga cikin kujerun dakin.

Lumshe ido nayi ina tuna rayuwar da Shatu keyi.

A hankali na furta ya kamata na ganta nima na saka mata.


Hawaye ne suka zubo man.




MARYAM ANKUDII{QURRAH}

Addu'ar Tafiyar Da Kuncin Zuciya

لاَ إلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ الْعَظِيمُ الْحَلِيمْ، لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ رَبُّ العَرْشِ العَظِيمِ، لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهْ رَبُّ السَّمَوَّاتِ ورّبُّ الأَرْضِ ورَبُّ العَرْشِ الْكَرِيمُ. 
La ilaha illal-lahul-'azeemul-haleem, la ilaha illal-lahu rabbul-'arshil-'azeem, la ilaha illal-lahu  rabbus-samawati warabbul-ardi warabbul-'arshil-kareem.

Babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai Allah, mai girma (wanda babu abin da yake girmama a gare shi), mai hakuri (mai jinkirin azaba tare da ickakken ikon aiwatar da ita). Babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai Allah, Ubangijin Al'arshi, mai girma. Babu abin bautgawa da gaskiya sai Allah, Ubangijin sammai Ubangijin kasa, Ubangijin Al'arshi, mai yawan baiwa.


اَللَّهُمَّ رَحْمَتَكَ أَرْجُوفَلا تَكِلْنِي إِلَى نَفْسِي طَرْفَةَ عَيْن، وَأَصْلِحْ لِي شَأْنِي كُلَّهُ لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ أنْتَ.
Allahumma rahmataka arjoo fala takilnee ila nafsee tarfata 'ayn, wa-aslih lee sha'nee kullah, la ilaha illa ant.

Ya Allah! Rahamarka nake kauna, don haka kar ka kyale ni da kaina ko da kiftawar ido ne, kuma ka kyautata mini sha'ainina dukkansa; babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai kai.


لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ أنْتَ سُبْحَانَكَ إِنِّي كُنْتُ مِنَ الظَّالِمِينَ.
La ilaha illa anta subhanaka innee kuntu minaz-zalimeen.
Babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai kai. Tsarki ya tabbata gare ka, lallai ni na kasance daga cikin azzalumai.

اَللهُ اللهُ رَبِّ  لاَ أُشْرِكُ بِهِ شَيْئاً.
Allahu Allahu rabbi la oshriku bihi shaya.
Allah! Allah ne ubangijina, ba na yin tarayya da shi da wani abu a cikin bauta.


DEDICATED TO.....MYTHANKS SISTERS😘


Bissimillah Rahmanir Rahim



Page-87-88



---------------Jin motsi yasa nayi saurin tashi tare da goge hawayen dake a fuskata.


Kallona yake sosai da alama ya dade a haka, juyawa yayi zuwa bedroom sai gashi da key a hannu nagane abinda yake nufi yana tunanin ko saboda ya hanani fita yasa nake kuka.

Lumshe ido nayi inada da kishiya fa kuma tun kamin na ganta nake kishin ta wani tunani yazo man nasan idan na kwana da shi zan batama su Mummy da Ammie to amman ina tsoron kada wani abu ya shiga a tsakanin mu.


Jin tsayuwar mutum akaina yasa na mike tsaye ina hawaye Hijab yake mikoman.

Ban amsaba cikin sauri na rungume shi sosai na kan-kame shi nace;"Yaya Anuu inason ganin Shatu"


Wani irin yarrrrr yaji a jikin shi jin ta ambaci sunan data dade bata ambata to waya fada mata haka take kiran shi ko ada?


Sakin Hijab din yayi ya rungume ta.

Daukarta yayi suka isa bisa bed kwantar da ita yayi shima yabi bayan ta ya kwanta.


Rumgume ta yayi sosai yace a hankali;"kiyi hakuri zan kaiki kona saka a daukota duk yadda kika zaba amman kibar zubar da hawayen ki haka kinji"


Kai na daga na kara shigewa jikin shi ina shakar kamshin jikin shi.


Tun ina tsoron kada yayi mani wani abu jin shiru yasa na sakii jiki nayi bacci tun da asuba na mike a tare sukayi Sallah sannan suka koma a cikin jikin shi na kara komawa na kwanta.


Sai wajen Nine kana suka tashi ita tafara yin wanka tana shiryawa ya tashi.

Ta gaidashi tana dukar da kai murmushi yayi yace;"kada kiyi komi ni zan maki Make-up bari na fito"

Ya dade kana ya fito yana fitowa da wata riga kalar jikin towul ya daureta a gefe na kasa daga ido na kalle shi.


Zuwa yayi bakin mirror din towul ne a jikina kuma karami ganin yada bacci yasa na daura shi.


Cikin salo yake shafaman mai  ya gama kana ya fara shaman powder mun dauki lokaci Ina duba fuskata naga ba wata kwalliya akayi daga powder sai jan baki da kwalli amman nayi kyau sosai dan ni bazan iyayinta hakaba.


Na dukar da kai ina wasa da yan ya tsana nace;"zanje na dauko kaya"

Mikewa yayi ya dauko wasu kaya kusan kala guda wando da riga ya mikoman zaro ido nayi ya bata fuska inajin tsoron shi hakan yasa na amsa naje toilet na saka ina fitowa sai naji babu dadi ko ina ya kamani gashi yana kallo na.


Zan saka Hijab ya mikoman wata bakar riga mai Zip a gaban ta na saka yazo ya zageman zip din.

Azaman mayafin ta yayi nace;"kabari na fara fita dan Allah"

Murmushi yayi yace;"ai saide na nuna maki hanya ta baya ba'abi ta gaba saboda akwai mutane zasu takura maki ko kamin ki isa aikine kuma sai anyi shelar kin fito lokaci guda gidan zai chanza mutane zasu fito kuma nasan tsoron mutane kike shiyasa ma na fada maki hakan"


A tare muka nufi wata hanya babu mutane ko kadan sai karar tsuntsaye a part din Baba muka nufa har inda zan shiga ya nunaman.


Yayi tsaye yana kallo na hannu cikin aljihun wando.

Har zan shiga na juyo muka hada ido murmushi yayi man ni kuma na daga mashi hannu ina murmushi.


Hakanan naji zuciyata tayi haske wani yanayi na shiga tareda so mai karfi lokaci guda.

Tsaye nayi na kasa shiga ina murmushi tare da lumshe ido ina tunanina cikin jikin shi.

Dariya nayi nace a fili;"yanzu daban kwana ba danayi ma kaina kilama Kulsoom zaije wajen ta"

Saina bata fuska na burza kofar su Baba na shiga.


Muka gaisa nan muka fara fira saiga aike nazo ana nemana.

Na mike muka fita tare dasu.


Part din Ammie aka nufa dani.

Ina zuwa na iske Anuwar yana latsar waya yana bisa kujera one seater.


Kulsoom na gefen shi bisa kujera itama One seater kallon su kawai nayi na dauke kai haka kawai naji raina ya baci neman tsari nake na daure.

Kulsoom tayi saurin mikewa tace:"MANAL sannu da zuwa antashi Lafiya jiya naso ganin ki akace kina wajen su Baba ashe a chan zaki kwana nayita jira anan"


Na dan kalli Prince dake latsar waya kamar bai ganni ba nace;"eh ina chan yauma da wuri zan koma bazan fitoba kinsan an dade ba'a haduba jiyama ai nayi ma wasu uzuri amman yau Babu shi Allah de yasa lafiya kike nemana?"


Murmushi tayi tace;"daman inaso musamu lokaci na baki hakuri sosai sai gashi bansamu ganin kiba dan Allah ki yafeman"


Nace;"Babu komi ya wuce;"


Kulsoom tace;"zan iskeki har daki na kara baki hakuri nagode sosai ki tambayar man Anuwar yafiya"

Na kalle shi naga yadda yasha mur fuskar shi babu alamun wasa na dauke kai dan nima ganin su a tare ya bataman rai sosai daurewa kawai nayi.


Ammie ce ta fito da fara'ar ta tace;"Wai breakfast ne na shirya mana hadda su Farhan na aika a kawosu muyi, dafatan kun tashi lafiya?"


Nayi murmushi nace;"lafiya kalau Ammie "


Ammie tace;"ai naso jiya ace wajena kika kwana amman yau zan roki alfarma wajena zaki kwana"


Nayi murmushi nace;"to shikenan Ammie yau tun yamma zanyi ban kwana da kowa bana kara fita sai gobe"

Ina maganar ina kallon Anuwar ta wusiyar ido ina murmushi.


Sai a lokacin ya kalleni kallon tuhuma na juyar da fuskata.

Sai yace;"amman kinsan ana bukatar ki, ya zakiyi da masu bukatar ki, a chan suna bukatar ki sosai kada kiyi haka ki bari kizoma Ammie kwana nan gaba amman yanzu suna bukatar ki sosai akwai kewa mai yawa a tare dasu kada ki cutar dasu"


Nagane inda maganar ta nufa, hakan yasa nayi murmushi nace;"kada ka damu zan yini a wajen sune Baba baida matsala"


Ammie tace;"yaushe kafara damuwa akan matsalar wasune? Daman kana iya magana mai tsayi?"


Baice komi ba saiga su Farhan hadda Hamrah tareda Nifra a tare dasu.


Su duka suka fada jikina suna murnar ganina nima jinayi nayi kewar su su duka lokaci guda sun chanza sunyi wani irin kyau su duka sun sha gyaran gashi kamar yaran turawa.


Farhana tace;"Ummie ni yau a wajen ki zan kwana nayi kewar ki"


Nace;"bakuga mutane bane"

Sai lokacin sukaga Anuwar murmushi yake yana kallon mu.


Da gudu suka fada kanshi suka rungume shi suna dariya Nifra tace;"Yaya Anuu bari na dauke ku, kuna burgeni idan nagan ku a tare"

Da wayar hannunta ta fara daukar su.


Kowa dariya yake kana suka gaida Ammie da Kulsoom dinning table muka nufa.

Muna zuwa muka zauna su Farhana ammanta dani sai zuba take tanaba Anuwar labari.


Hamrah na kama hannunta ganin Anuwar yanata Farhana dake zuba labari, a bangaren Kulsoom komi nata tanayin shine jiki a mace, dan magan ganun Anuwar da Manal da sukayi yasa ta gane daki guda suka kwana.


Nace;"Hamrah ta baki gaida Ammie da Dad naki da niba"

Yarinyar karama amman saita kara bata fuska nace;"Kai bakison da siya maki wani abu, bazan fadamaki abun ba saikin saki fuska kinyi murmushi kin gaida su Ammie"

Sai nayi mata rad'a a kunne sai tayi murmushi tace;"Ammie good morning Dad morning"

Saita kalleni tace;"MANAL good morning"


Anuwar ne ya bata fuska ya kira sunanta ta kalleshi yace;"kada ki kara kiran sunan ta haka Ummien kice bakiji yadda Farhana ke Kiran taba"


Bata fuska tayi ganin haka yasa na kama hannun nayi murmushi rad'a nayi mata a kunne.

Hamrah tace;"ohh sorry Dad"

Yayi murmushi yana kallona yace;"ki chanza"

Ta daga kai tace;"Okay Dad then smile for me"

Murmushi yayi kana aka zuba ma kowa za'a zubama Hamrah nace abar shi ganin Anuwar ya zubama su Farhana ya zuba masu drink a glas cup Nima na zuba mata hakanan mukayi breakfast yana kula dasu ina kula da Hamrah Ammie sai murmushi take muka tashi.


Muka dawo parloun Ammie Nifra tace;"zata wuce da yara suna fita ni kuma na mike nayi bedroom din Ammie.


Zama nayi ina tunanin mizanyi ne Ammie ta shigo tace zataje dakin Mai martaba nace bakomi.

Bayan fitar ta nafara mata goge-goge saiga Anuwar ya shigo na kalle shi na cigaba da abinda nake nima na rama dazun haka yayiman.


Ina bakin mirror ina gogewa ya shigo ya rikeman kugu ya aza kanshi bisa kafadata.

Nace;"Dan Allah ka kyaleni dazun saboda a gaban matar kane ai yi kayi kamar baka ganni ba"


Murmushi yayi yana kallon mu a mirror yace;"miyasa kikeson nisan tamu? Shine zakice da Ammie zaki kwana miyasa haka? Kinsan bazan iya jurewa ba  kuma dazun koda kika shigo raina a bace yake wai Ammie tace ranar Friday zakuje Abuja, zakije kuma wajen dangin Mummy kuyi three days shine nace gaskiya bazan iyaba shinefa tayi zuciya ta tafi daki saida kika shigo ta fito kinji dalilina"

Nace;"yanzu ina matar ka?"

Murmushi yayi saboda yadda nayi maganar.


Juyo dani yayi ina kallon shi ya jaman hanci kadan yace;"kishi ko? to tana parlour"

Bata fuska nayi nace;"ni babu wani kishi tambaya nayi kuma kasan bai dace ba mu muna nan ita tana chan"


Baice komiba ya cigaba da shafa gefen wuyana yana kallona yana murmushi ture man gyalen jallabiya ta yayi ya fadi kasa ya fara shin shina gashin kaina.

Hannun shi ya saka ya zugeman zip din rigata ta zame.


Kallon shi nayi na zaro ido zanyi magana ya saka bakin shi cikin nawa.


Ina kokarin na tureshi amman na kasa.

Mun dade a haka kana ya sakeni ya fara shafa bayana.

Idanuna suka kawo kwallah nace;"a dakin Ammie muke fa"

Kallona yake idanun nan nashi sai kara chanza color suke.

Kasa kallon shi nayi jikina duk ya mutu banida kuzari balle na kwace.


Jin hannun shi cikin rigata yasa nayi saurin fadin;"Subhanallah"

Na zaro ido na samu na fizge nayi baya na fada bisa gado ina kallon shi mu duka maida numfashi muke.


Hawaye suka kawoman nace;"Dan Allah ka gyaleni a dakin Ammie nike"

Kallona yayi yace;"to miyasa zakice a dakin Ammie zaki kwana? Ina bukatar ki a yau bazan iya jurewa ba"


Shiru nayi ina kara matsawa gefen gado.

Nace;"to ai ba'ace na koma wajen kaba, su Ammie zasu man fada kuma ai kanada mata kaje wajenta mana"

Kallona yayi yace;"a tunanina nidake babu haka a tsakanin mu kada kimanta shekara nawa mukayi bamu tare dake ki tausaya man mana, ko kinaso na karo ta ukku"

Zaro ido nayi nace;"to Amman ka raba mana kwana kada mushiga hakkinta kuma ni yanzu ai bazan iya komawa ba ina kunyar Mummy"

Murmushi yayi yace;"kinanan da halinki na kafiya ga tsiwa ga surutu Farhana ba haye tayi ba"

Kwankwasa kofar akayi nayi saurin sabka bisa gado duk na yamutse shi saboda har karshe na haye, nazo na dauki rigata na kalle shi nace;

Yanzu idan Ammie ce mizamu ce mata kaga abinda nake gudu ko?"

Murmushi yayi ya xauna bisa gadon naje na bude Mummy ce.


Murmushi tayi tace;"yau za'ayi walima zuwa anjima muje zaki dauki kaya"

Nayi murmushi dan bata shigoba banson taga Anuwar.

Zan fita naji yace;"Kai Mummy ina zakije man da ita"

Murmushi tayi, ni kuma numfashi na ya kusa daukewa.


Dakin ta shiga taga yadda gadon Ammie dana hau yayi, Murmushi tayi tace;

"Anuu bakajin magana daman kana nan Dad dinka na neman ka ya kiraka baka daukaba"

Dafe kai yayi ya zaro wayar yace;"ohh Mummy banjiba kuma kinga tana tare dani bansan ya akayi ba"


Murmushi Mummy tayi tace Allah ya shirya ka.

Tashi yayi yace;"Mummy muje a tare"

Mummy tace;"Babu inda zani dakai ka zauna idan mun fita ka fito a parlo nabar matar ka kuma kasan tana nan ka shige daki da MANAL hadda rufe kofa"


Bata fuska yayi yace;"Amman Mummy firafa mukeyi nida ita"


Murmushi Mummy tayi tace;"gaskiya ne ai naga alama"


Taja hannuna muka fita mukabar shi nan.


A parlour muka iske Kulsoom duk sai naji babu dadi muna fitowa ta mike tace;

"Manal kin fito muje natayasu shiryaki"

Murmushi nayi na kama hannunta.

Zamu fita na juya na ganshi tsaye yana kallon mu kwallo nayi mashi, shi kuma murmushi yayi tareda yima hannun shi Kiss ya turoman nayi murmushi kana zan zuja naga Kulsoom na kallon mu murmushi tayi.


Muka fita part din Mummy muka Isa.


A dakin hadda su Nifra da Muhammad kanena nan saida mukaci abinci mukayi Sallah kana.


Da yamma nida Nifra kaya iri daya muka saka harda Farhana da Hamrah.


Munyi kyau sosai Farhan dashi da Anuwar iri daya.

Yadda aka hada wajen kamar ana wani biki haka wurin ya hadu tareda manyan mutane munsha dauka sannan munsha kyau nida yarana.

Friend din Dad dina sun mani kyaututtuka Wanda ni ban masan mi suke nufiba sai Nifra taman bayani.

Munyi pictures nida Mummy da Nifra da Muhammad da Dad munyi dasu Walida su Rahama a ranar ta iso naga diyarta mai sunana yarinyar tayi wayo sosai gaskiya naji dadi yadda naga abun har mamaki yaban.

Haka munyi pictures dasu Inna ana cikin daukar photunan na fara kwallah Anuwar ya gogeman nace a hankali;

"Shatu"

Murmushi yayi ya dauko wayar shi yabar wajen yana waya.

Ganin haka Mummy tazo ta jawoni muna pictures da yan uwanta.


Saiji nayi an rikoni na juya a tunanina Ma'u ce.

Ina juyowa naga Shatu wata irin kara nayi na rungume ta.


Anuwar ne tsaye yana kallon na.


Kallon shi nayi ina son yayiman karin bayani aljanu yasa suka daukota komi.

Dan nasan ko jirgi bai isa tashiba daga maganar mu balle mota ko minti goma ba'ayi bafa.


Kallon Shatu nake lallai itace.

Tabata nike inakara tabbaci.

Na kalli Anuwar nakasa magana saboda yadda na rude Anya gaske ne?


MARYAM ANKUDII{QURRAH}

Addu'ar Tafiyar Da Kuncin Zuciya

لاَ إلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ الْعَظِيمُ الْحَلِيمْ، لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهُ رَبُّ العَرْشِ العَظِيمِ، لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللَّهْ رَبُّ السَّمَوَّاتِ ورّبُّ الأَرْضِ ورَبُّ العَرْشِ الْكَرِيمُ. 
La ilaha illal-lahul-'azeemul-haleem, la ilaha illal-lahu rabbul-'arshil-'azeem, la ilaha illal-lahu  rabbus-samawati warabbul-ardi warabbul-'arshil-kareem.

Babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai Allah, mai girma (wanda babu abin da yake girmama a gare shi), mai hakuri (mai jinkirin azaba tare da ickakken ikon aiwatar da ita). Babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai Allah, Ubangijin Al'arshi, mai girma. Babu abin bautgawa da gaskiya sai Allah, Ubangijin sammai Ubangijin kasa, Ubangijin Al'arshi, mai yawan baiwa.


اَللَّهُمَّ رَحْمَتَكَ أَرْجُوفَلا تَكِلْنِي إِلَى نَفْسِي طَرْفَةَ عَيْن، وَأَصْلِحْ لِي شَأْنِي كُلَّهُ لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ أنْتَ.
Allahumma rahmataka arjoo fala takilnee ila nafsee tarfata 'ayn, wa-aslih lee sha'nee kullah, la ilaha illa ant.

Ya Allah! Rahamarka nake kauna, don haka kar ka kyale ni da kaina ko da kiftawar ido ne, kuma ka kyautata mini sha'ainina dukkansa; babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai kai.


لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ أنْتَ سُبْحَانَكَ إِنِّي كُنْتُ مِنَ الظَّالِمِينَ.
La ilaha illa anta subhanaka innee kuntu minaz-zalimeen.
Babu abin bautawa da gaskiya sai kai. Tsarki ya tabbata gare ka, lallai ni na kasance daga cikin azzalumai.

اَللهُ اللهُ رَبِّ  لاَ أُشْرِكُ بِهِ شَيْئاً.
Allahu Allahu rabbi la oshriku bihi shaya.
Allah! Allah ne ubangijina, ba na yin tarayya da shi da wani abu a cikin bauta.

----------------Matsowa yayi dai-dai wajen kunne na yace;"ina jiran ki da anjima"

Kallon shi nake baki sake ya kanne man ido guda nayi murmushi duk sai naji kunaya ta kamani

Na rungume ta munyi pictures dasu Inna dasu Shatu haka matan Yaya Sani da Salisu da dalibaina duk sunzo, sai da aka kira sallah kana aka tashi nide Mummy taja hannu na, hannuna na cikin na Shatu muka bita.

Sai lokacin Mummy tace;"Ina kikasamu wannan"

Nayi murmushi nace;"Shatu ce"

Zaro ido Mummy tayi tace;"Shatu! Ba'a Katsina takeba yaushe tazo?"

Murmushi nayi nace;"Yau nima Yaya Anuu ya kawoman ita nayi tunanin kinsani"

Tace;"a'a bai fadaman za'a daukota ba, amman naji dadi shiyasa nikeson Anuu"

Rumgume ta Mummy tayi tana murna.

Lokaci guda aka hada mata abincika kala-kala Mummy sai nan takeyi da ita naji dadi haka Nifra tayi mamaki itama sai kokarin faranta mata take.

Saida mukayi sallar isha'i kana mukaci abinci.

Mummy tace;"Bari nasa abaku wuri ku gaisa zan hana kowa shigowa part din"

Nan Mummy suka fita zaune muke a bedroom din Mummy Shatu sai kallon dakin take tace;"Nanah bangane komi ba jiya shabiyu na dare akazo gidan mu nan ake fadaman wai anason tafiya dani zuwa Bauchi ke kikasa a daukoni dayake ina gidan kanwar Maman mu ada naki yadda saida nayi waya da Baba cikin daren nan kana na yadda saida suka nunaman photunan ki, kuma abinda yasa aka yadda jami'an tsaro ne Nana Nan gidan kune kenan?"

Hawaye suka zuboman nan na fadama Shatu komi mu duka murna muke muka rungume juna nan ta fadaman halin data shiga na jajan ta mata sosai.

Muna zaune dare yayi sosai ina tambayar ta yan kauyen mu saiga Nifra da alama daga bacci take.

Tace;"ga waya inji Yaya Anuu"

Ta mikoman waya na amsa nayi shiru yace;"ina waje"

Ya kashe wayar na mika mata tace;"tunga part din mu ya tasoni har daki ya iskeni Wai wallahi sainazo na tadaki Mummy ta hana shi Key din Part din yasan ni taba ajiyar key din, wannan ajiya tasani wahala ni tareda ku zanyi bacci dan ban iya komawa"

Tana ida maganar tabi gado ta kwanta na kalli Shatu tayi murmushi.

Ganin kamar bacci ya dauki Nifra yasa na mike nashiga nayi wanka,nayi ma Shatu rada kana na kara saka wasu turarruka dake gaban Mirror din Mummy na fita.



Ina fita na ganshi a tsaye yana kallon wurin.

Hannuna ya kama yace;"da baki fitoba anan zan kwana kuma nasan sainayi mura dasai kin sha jinya"


Ya kama hannuna mukabi wani wata hanya mun danyi tafiya kana muka isa part din jiya.

Muna shiga na kalleshi nace;"nagaji Yaya Anuu bacci nakeji kaina ke ciwo"

Lokaci guda naga ya rude nayi murmushi a zuciyata naji dadi.

Na kwanta ina bata fuska yana lallashi kana na samu nayi bacci.

Sai asuba na tashi Ina tashi shima ya tashi mukayi sallah.

Kwantawa nayi na koma bacci.

Yau na dade ina bacci ina tashi nayi mika na ganshi shima yana rungume dani yana bacci, yadda na zabura yasa shima bude ido nace;

"Na shiga ukku Yaya Anuu Mummy"

Murmushi yayi yace;"kin man wayo ko?"

Na kalleshi kamar zanyi kuka nace;"Yaya Anuu Mummy ta isa zuwa daki kada taga banan na kwana ba"

Na mike cikin sauri na nufi toilet wanka nayi na wanke baki na fito yau na dan saki jiki dashi kamar jiya shiyayi man komi.

Yau atamfa ya mikoman mai blue na saka doguwar rigace dai-dai jikina tayiman kyau sosai aza d'ankwalin nayi bisa kai saboda ban iya daurawa ba.

Parlour na fita ina jiran ya fito.

Sai gashi shima yadine jikin shi blue yayi kyau sosai nayi murmushi nace;"yau dakin Ammie zan kwana, yanzu idan naje bansan mi zance ba budeman na wuce dan Allah"

Murmushi yayi yace;"baki biyani daukowar danayi ma Shatu ba daman na daukota danki biyani, sai gashi har yanzu baki biyani ba, kinga kenan sai kin biyani kana na gyale ki, muje a part din Abie zamuyi break nayi waya"

Ido na zaro hawaye zasu zubo nace;"na shiga Ukku Yaya Anuu miyasa zakaman haka yanzu shikenan kowa yasan dakin ka na kwana nashiga ukku yanzu dawane ido zan kalle su"


Murmushi yayi yace;"wasa nake munyi magana da Nifra na fada masu da safe kikazo waje na"

Murmushi nayi nace;"muje"

A part din Abie mukaje duk mutanan gidan na nan hadda su Baba dasu Inna muka gaisa da kowa naje kusa da Baba na zauna na rike hannun yaron Ma'u yayi murmushi.


Abie nan yayi jawabi tare da kara masu Baba godiya da Inna(Maifura) kana yace yanason Baba ya dawo Bauchi da iyalen shi da zama.


Baba yace a'a saboda akwai iyalen Yayan shi da yan uwa kamar bai masu adalci ba.

Mai martaba yace bakomi sunyi maganar da Dad duk za'a dauki nauyin su naji dadi sosai dan nasan duk abinda akayima Baba baza'a a biyashi ba abinda yayi man ba ya dauki tsana da zargin jama'a saboda ni, yabar garin su gaskiya nasan baza'a biya Baba ba kodan kaunar daya nunaman.


Sannan Inna har tadawo cikin gidan daman Yaya Sani an yantashi daga hadimi ya koma D'an gida naji dadi sosai sai Shatu da Dad yace ta zauna har tasamu miji tunda batada aure naji dadi sosai haka aka tashi nanufi part din Ammie achan naci abinci.


Na iske Ammie a daki na zauna tace;"Daman ke nake jira ki gama anshi sha"

Ta mikoman wani abu cikin cup babu gardama nasha.

Tayi murmushi tace;"Nasan Yarima babu hakuri shiyasa na baki kikasha yanzu gobe zaku Abuja su Malam (Baba) Kuma gobe zasu wuce Katsina domin ban kwana kinga dole aje wajen dangi ayi masu bayani kada suji shiru"

Nace;"Ammie nima inason na bisu inason zuwa kauyen mu"

Shiru Ammie tayi tace;"gaskiya hakan ya kamata bari zan ma Maimartaba magana"

Murmushi nayi nace;"yawwa Ammie"

Tace;"yanzu kinci abinci zuwa anjima kadan akwai kaza na nan nasa aka dafa maki"

Murmushi nayi bance komi ba sai yanzu nagane abinda take nufi da bashi da hakuri da tace Yaya Anuu baida.


Fira muke sosai su Farhana suka shigo nabar dakin nayi parlo nan tasa aka daukoman kazata naci ni kade kana nayi part din Inna nabar su nan.


Muna fira da Inna tabani itama wani abu na tamna sannan itama taban wasu ruwa wai Yaya Sani tasa ya samo mata iccen nide nasha hakanan badan nasoba saboda yadda sukeda da'ci.

Nace;"Inna Yau wurin ki zan kwana tare dasu Farhana"

Tace;"Haba Nana saboda fa ku huta yasa na dauke yaran ba wajena suke kwana ba b'angaren Fulani (Ammie) suke kwana kona mahaifiyar ki wanibi kuma Nifra ke zuwa tace suje su kwana"


Nace;"toni Inna gaskiya nagaji a daki daya fa muke kwana dashi amman babu wanda yasani, ina tsoron kada a sani"


Zaro ido Inna tayi tace;"yanzu a daki daya kuke kwana baki fadaman ba nayi maki wani abu"

Nace;"nifa Inna babu abinda ke shiga tsakanin mu"

Tace;"rabani da wannan maganar yaran zamani to yanzu ya kikeso nayi maki shida matar shi nayi maku katan ga, to hakan bazai yiyuba yau nizan shirya ki kuma har bakin kofar Yarima saina kaiki"

Nace;"Kai Inna yanzu goya mashi baya zakiyi"

Murmushi tayi tace;"har yanzu da sauran ki to bari kiji ba'a sake da dama ki chanza tunani ba'ayi ma maza haka"

Mun dade muna fira da Inna duk firar da muke tana kwatan taman yadda zanyi kula miji ne tareda bani labarai nada cikin hikima.

Na dade kana na nufi wajen su Baba nan muke fira dasu Shatu da Ma'u firar mu lafiya kalau cikin kwanciyar hankali labarin kauye muke hadda wakokin kauye nazuwa rafi da daddali da sauran su.

Saida mukaci abinci dare a tare kana nayi masu bankwana nayi part din Mummy.

Nan na iske su Farhana suna wasa.
Mummy tace;"yau ina zaki kwana gobe zamuje Abuja ada hadda ke amman yanzu Dad dinki yace zakije Katsina muma zamuje wajen iyayen Shatu amman mu daga Abuja zamuje sai dan Dad dinki keda meeting"


Nace;"to shikenan Mummy bari naje na kwanta"

Farhana tace;"Ummie saida safe nikam wajen Mummy zan kwana Farhan yace ni kuma wajen Uncle"

Nayi murmushi na fita kai tsaye part din Inna na nufa zan wuce wani waje naji ankama hannu na nayi saurin waigawa nagan shi.

Murmushi yayi man niko tsoro ya kamani.


Zan kwace ya daukeni yayi part din da muke kwana.

A toilet ya sabkeni ya fito na kulle nayi wanka na fito.

Ko kamin ya fito har nasaka kayan bacci na saka turarurruka na hau gado na kulle ido nida tsiya nayi bacci.


Jim kadan naji shi ya hau gadon na daure banyi mutsiba.

Juyowa yayi dani Ina kallon shi nan idona ya fara kyarma.

Murmushi yayi yafara mani wasu wasanni haka dai tun ina turjewa haka yayi galaba akaina.

A ranar Yarima ya kara kusanta ta, nasha wahala tamkar budurwa shi kuma babu abinda yake sai kalamai masu dadi.

Bayan munyi sallar asuba muka koma bacci da safe na shirya nan ma saida ya kara gajiyar dani da kyat nabar part dinshi.

Part din Mummy komi an hada mana na tafiya.

Saida na sake wani wanka kana na chanza Kaya na fito mukayi ma kowa bankwana amman ga mamakina banga Anuwar ba sai Kulsoom data rakomu.

Banda su Farhana zamu tafi, su Mummy zasu tafi dasu a chan zamu hadu.

Har zamu shiga mota ban ganshi ba duk nabi na rude anaman ban kwana hankali na yana ga hanya kozai fito hakanan nashiga mota.

Bayan muje airport Yaya Sani ya mikoman waya yace;"Inji Yarima na amsa"

Haka a airport nayita tunanin zaizo har muka bar Bauchi bai zoba.

Muna isa Katsina kai tsaye Dan-musa muka nufa amman bamu kwana ba munde gaisa da mutane Baba yayi masu bayani bayan la'asar muka nufi Katsina muna zuwa muka wuce Batagarawa.


Anan ne wajen tashar motar naga Matii ya d'an fara manyan ta da motar shi ta kara tsufa sosai mamaki nake daman mota na irin shekaru haka kuma tsohuwa.

Kallo suka bimu dashi na saida motar muka fito dani da Shatu dasu Baba.

Naje wajen Matii na ina Isa ya duka kasa.

Nace;"matii baka ganeni ba yace eh Hajiya bansan kiba"

Shatu tayi daria yace;"a'a Shatu ina kika samu wadan nan mutanan hadda motar soja a bayan su kuma naga kina dariya kuma naga kin chanza ina kika samu wadan nan kayan jikin naki tode mafarki"

Murmushi nayi nace;"Matii zakaje kauyen mu yau"

Nayi maganar kalar yadda nake mashi cikin mamaki yake kara kallona yace;"yar gidan Liman...?"

Nayi dariya nace;"kaga gama Baban chan dasu Ma'u sun fito ashe"

Nan Shatu ta fadi mashi sunan Dad ya zaro ido yana mamaki.

Nace;"Matii daman motar nan na nan? har yanzu tana lalacewa?"

Murmushi yayi yace;"Ban dade da gyarataba ta dade gareji tana son injin amman yanzu lafiya kalau take, amman fa tana tsayawa"

Mukayi murmushi na fidda kudi masu yawa daga jikar hannuna da Mummy ta cikata da yan dubu na bashi kana mukayi ban kwana yace zaizo har gida ya karaman godiya idan na huta zuwa gobe 



Muka koma mota muna isa kauyen mu gidan mu muka nufa.


Nan Sani yaje ya kawo katihu daga Katsina muka gyara gidan sosai.

Gidan su Shatu muka nufa nan Naga su Jabe harda iyali an aje ya girma saida Daddare kana Yaya Sani yazo ya daukeni.


Na shiga dakina duk ya kara mutuwa hawaye suka zuboman Baba ya shigo yace;"Dena kuka dare yayi kizo muje dakin Innar ki tana chan ta gyara maki kafita"

Nace;"A'a Baba a dakina zan kwana kallon gadon karana nayi dake jingine da sauran kayana gasunan har sun fara shigewa cikin kasa.

Baba yace;"har nasaida gidan, Wanda ya saya yaki cikaman kudin rabi ya bada dana gaji na maida mashi kudin shi saboda yace baidasu ban taba tunanin zamu kara kwana a gidan nan ba gobe su Maimuna dasu Auwalu zasuzo"

Nayi murmushi saiga Sani yace;"Dad yace amaidani a Katsina na kwana sai fada yake"

Na girgiza kai nace;"Yaya Sani anan zan kwana zanba Dad hakuri"

Yace;"to bari ya daukoman katifun da ya siyo akwai saura"

Nace;"a'a yabar shi"

Ya juya ya fita mukayi ban kwana da Baba na sabke gadon karan da duk gara ta cinye nasa zane na kwanta hawaye nake sosai.

Saiga Inna tana kirana tace;"minene haka Nana ga shimfida chan anmaki hadda sabon bargo da zanan gado Sani ya siyo muje ki kwanta"

Inna tayi-tayi nakiya harda kuka nayi mata akan ta gyaleni

Tunda safe na tashi na share gidan na debo ruwan rijiya nayi wanka.

Fita nayi gonar Baba naje tana nan anan naga Jabe na kiwo yakoje ya kiraman Shatu mun dade kana naje gidan su Ina zuwa sai Yaya Sani a rude su Dad su inso yanata fada inanaje.

Ina zuwa muka gaisa yace;"Manal bade anan kika kwana ba banace ki zabi gidan su Kulsoom ko G-hourse ko Hotel ba miyasa zaki kwana Nan"

Ina daga Ido naga Anuwar na kara kallon shi cikin mamaki.

Nace;"Dad kayi hakuri"

Mummy tace;"a fitoda su Farhana"

Suna fitowa sukazo suka rungume ni suna murna.

Muka shiga gida aka shimfida masu tabarma.

Bayan su gaisa da Baba Dad da Mummy nabin gidan da kallo Mummy tace;"Ina wayar da Yayan ki yabaki tun jiya yake kira a kashe shiyasa yau tun da safe ya sai gashi sai muka taho wayar faduwa tayi ne"


Nace;"ban iya kunna waba tana daki bisa gado"

Nifra tace ina dakin na nuna mata taje ta dauko ta kunna.

Tana shiga ta buga kara da Mummy da Dad da sauran mutane sukayi wajen ta.


Muna zuwa tace;"Mummy kalli bazan iya shigaba dakin ba manyan windows duhu sannan kalli wanchan abu kada ya tsirine kuma sama wayar take"

Na kalleta cikin mamaki dan a tunanina maciji ta gani tunda kusa da daji muke.

Nace;"ai wannan shine dakina kuma wanchan ai gado nane"

Su duka suka shiga suna kallon dakin Mummy hadda kwallah.

Nace;"Mummy nifa nasa kaina kwana anan ina kaunar wurin ne Mummy"

Rungume ni Mummy tayi Nan baya yayi masu jagoranci zuwa gidan su Shatu.

Nida Yaya Anuwar da Nifra mukace kasa zamu hadda Ma'u nace mata mutafi.


Nan na nuna masu gari bayan munje gidan su Shatu muna hanyar dawowa saiga Lado kusa da gidan mu gidan mu mutane sun zagaye mutanan gari hadda mai gari mata da maza.

Ma'u tasha toka tayi tsoki Lado ya sabko da kyat daga bisa jakki duk ya kara tsufa.

Yace;"Ma'u yau kece a garin inasu Rabi'u yanzu daga dawa nake naji ance kunzo hadda baki"

Tace;"Nana mutafi"

Ya kalleni ya gwale ido yace;"Nana ce haka?"

Ma'u tayi tsoki ta tafi muka gaisa nayi mashi kyau.

Nifra tace lallai da ba'ayi cikin su Farhana ba aka koreki da labari baiyi dadiba aka hadaka da wannan abun ai an cuceka.

Muna zuwa muka iske Yaya maimuna tazo da Yaya Auwalu muka gaisa sai da mukayi fira kana mukayi bankwana zamu wuce nayima Yaya Maimuna kyauta mai tsoka sosai nace saitazo.

Muka wuce muna zuwa motocin su Mummy zasu wuce.

Na shiga dakina ina kallon shi nayi murmushi na fito na iske Inna daki ta shirya muka fita nida Anuwar muke mota daya yanzu.

Muna zuwa bakin hanya naga Matii na sabke yan tallah da mutane.

Nasa aka tsaya hakan yasa su Mummy suka tsaya naje inda Matii yaceman yazo jiya matakan tsaro suka hanashi shiga yau ma yaje amman ba'a bashi ba nace bakomi nan na fadama Anuwar Dad yayi mashi kyautar mota naji dadi sosai Matii sai godiya yake kamar zai kwanta hadda kuka.

Muna zuwa Katsina muka wuce a airport.

Koda muka isa gida kai tsaye part din Inna na nufa nida Shatu dan a wajen Inna zata zauna.

Dare nayi Anuwar yazo mutafi naki yadda yaje sai yayi kwana biyu ga matar shi.

Fushi yayi ta tafi kwana biyu na koma part din shi.

Ranar naga soyayya nan yake fadaman su Farhana dasu aka koma Abuja sun koma hannun Mummy.

Nayi murmushi Bayan kwana biyu aka ba Baba gidan da aka ginama Yarima na shakatawa nakusa da Gubi mallaka mashi takaddu Babu harda kuka nima naji dadi sosai, Dad yayi haka saboda gidan na kusa da ruwa koda zaiyi noman rani ga kayan itace.


Inna aka daukeman ita aka maidata wajen su Farhana a Abuja 

Shatu ta koma wajen Ammie.

Yau Koda na tashi babu lafiya hakan yasa na koma part din Ammie daman wajen Kulsoom yake.

Kwana mukayi ba lafiya tun da safe Ammie ta kira Anuwar aiko yazo, yana zuwa yabi ya rude dan nayi yaushi sosai ko hannu van iya dagawa yakira Doctor, Doctor na zuwa naga Doctor Mubarak.

Yayi murmushi yace;"Nana bari na aunaki"

Na kalleshi daman ya ganeni?

Yace;"kinyi mamaki ai nazo bikin Walimar ku nan nikejin labarin komi, nakoga Teema ta gidan Hajiya da yara biyu nan nabata labari jiya tazo tace nayi mata hanya tana son zuwa ba'a bari ta ganki"

Nakoji dadi na bashi Number ta nace yabata yana gama abinda zaiyi yace;"Prince Congratulation princess nada....."

Ai bai idaba ya rungume ni Ammie sai murna take Kulsoom ma data shigo sai murna suke.

Wannan ciki danayi har su Mummy saida sukazo waina koma a chan nayi rainon ciki Prince yaki yadda saboda Mai martaba yace babu inda zashi yanzu sai ya koyi zaman Fada.

Kusan kullun Ammie na tare dani haka Yarima.

Ana gobe zan wuce saiga kiran Teema na dauka tace itace tana waje.

Nasa ake aka taho da ita duk ta ya mutse kamar ba Teema ba 'yar gayu, nace;"Subhanallah! Teema lafiya kikayi baki haka?"

Tace;"Nana naji komi wajen Doctor kinji dadin ki, Kuma fari ai daman na maine yanzu nabar shafawa nayi aure barikin abubuwa sun faru nabar gidan Hajiya naje na auri wani tsohon saurayina saiya fita ya samo yanzu yaran biyu duk mata gasunan"

Nace;"Allah sarki Allah ya yara yanzu Ina Hajiya"

Tace;"Hajiya babu mai sayen abincin ta saboda ana zargin tanada HIV shiyasa bamai zuwa saye yanzu icce take saidawa a bakin gidan ta komi ya kare idan kika ganta duk ta tsufa abinda tayi ke bibiyarta tunda lokacin Number Maman  Doctor Mubarak tasamu ta kirata tace mata ta Karuwa yake so waitayi cikin shege wai so yake yace nashine kuma banashi bane hakan yasa tunda safe tazo ta tafi dashi, a lokacin ni tasa na samo mata kawai saboda zai taimaka maki yanzu ai ga abunta tagani lokacin tayi amfani da dama ta lalata yaran mutane tasasu hanyar banxa yanzu Allah yayi masu sakayya"

Mun dade muna fira kana Teema ta tafi tace yanzu zatabiya taba Hajiya labari nayimata kyauta mai tsoka tanata godiya.


Bayan kwana biyu naji wai Doctor Mubarak keson Shatu naji dadi sosai Ma'u Kuma Yaya Sani zaiyi ta biyu suma naji dadi dan yanada hali mai kyau sosai.

Tare da Anuwar mukaje Abuja daga chan muka wuce Spain a chan zanyi renon ciki hadda Mummy shi kuma Yaya Anuwar Dad yasa ya koma.

Bazan iya musulta yadda nasha gataba a wajen Mummy.

Anan na haihu nasamu namiji aka saka mashi sunan Abie.


Muka dawo anan Bauchi akayi biki nan na iske Kulsoom da ciki anyi bikin su Shatu da Ma'u.

Kullun kowa cikin kokarin faranta man yake inajin dadin yadda ake nunaman kauna.

Yau na samu tsarki daman tunda nazo ina wajen Ammie.

Prince ya shigo na kalle shi ina shayar da Sultan.

Yace;"Gaskia yau zaki koma dakin ki"

Nayi murmushi nace;"daman ni daki gareni a gidan nan?"

Yace;"to shikenan yau anan zan kwana"

Ammie ce ta shigo tace;"daman nagaji nima da wannan zaryar taka ace mutum baida hakuri"

Ya sosa keya yace;"Ammie....."

Daga mashi hannu tayi tace;"zan maida maka matar ka yau shikenan"

Yayi murmushi yace;"Nagode sosai Ammie na"

Bayan tafiyar Anuwar akace nayi bakuwa ina fitowa naga Hajiya na saki fuska muka zauna muka gaisa nace;"ba Hajiya bace"

Tace;"nice Nana dan Allah ki taimakaman kiyi hakuri da abinda nayi maki sharrin shedan ne"

Nayi murmushi nace;"Bakomi daga yanzu zan saka duk wata abaki abinda zaki sayi abinci baida matsala"


Tayita godiya Kulsoom data iskemu tace;"ba ita bace ta cutar dake ba? Naji kince Hajiya gashi tana rokon ki yafiya"


Nayi murmushi nace;"ita mana"

Tace;"Kuma zaki taimaka mata Bayan abinda tayi maki?"

Nace;"ai babu komi duk mutum yayi maka Sharri tokai ka maida mashi da Alkhairi hakan shine dai-dai saboda kada mutaru mu zama daya"

Tayi murmushi nima nayi na koma daki.

Vedio call muke dasu Nifra da Farhana da Farhan sunje shopping Dubai Anuwar ya shigo.

Na juya na kalle shi nan ya amsa sunayi.

Bayan sun gama yace;"Gobe za'azo maganar auren Nifra"

Nan nayi murna Sister ta zatayi aure.

Babu abinda zance saide nace Alhamdulillah rayuwata nakan zauna ina tuna nice Nana mai fura muna zaune da Kulsoom lafiya Dad ya sabka bisa mulki ya tattara iyalen shi sun koma Dubai da zama ina yawan zuwa Anuwar kullun cikin kokarin kyautata man yake haka nima.

Nifra tayi aure ta auri wani d'an kanwar Mummy ansha biki har a Dubai mun sha biki kana muka Nigeria.

Su Farhana sun girma suna wajen Mummy mu kuma mun koma Spain saboda a chan Anuwar yake aiki.

Yau mun tashi zamuje Bauchi munada bikin Jawad.

Koda muka isa wajen Inna na zauna.

Naje wajen Baba su Ma'u sunzo biki da Shatu munji dadin haduwa yanzu Ma'u da Shatu ne friends dina.

Muna rayuwa cikin kwanciyar hankali da kaunar juna ina zaune bedroom din Ammie saiga Anuwar ya shigo da Daddare.

A hankali ya furta;"Nanah Khadija"

Nayi saurin juyowa saboda yaune rana ta farko da wani ya kirani da sunan haka nayi murmushi.

Ammie tace;"Uwar tamu kake fadi haka saina fadama Mai martaba"

Mu duka mukayi murmushi yace;"muje dare yayi hakanan nagaji da jira"

Ammie tace;"Hali na nan ko kunyata bakaji tashi kibi mijin ki"

Mukayi ban kwana muka tafi part din yau wani salon sone yazo dashi na daban yau ko MANAL din ba'a cewa sai Nanah Khadija gaskiya a daren yau munsha soyayya.

Ina bisa jikin shi yace;"Allah nagode maka daka dawoman da Manal dina"

Nayi murmushi nace;"Allah na gode maka daka mallaka man Yaya Anuu a matsayin miji"

Murmushi mukayi tareda Hamdala.

Yace;"Nayi kewar ki da kika tafi jinayi duniyar ta isheni ina matukar kaunar ki kada ki kara tafiya kece abokiyar rayuwa"

Nayi murmushi nace;"bazamu kara rabuwa ba rabuwar da mukayi a baya babu yadda zamu saboda  itace JARABTAR MU"


Alhamdulillah....


Anan na kawo karshen JARABTAR MU.

Allah ya yafe mana kura kuren mu...Ameen
